### G095 — PR #14492 — VideoBackends/Vulkan: Drop BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z workaround

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon/DriverDetails + UberShaderPixel + Vulkan backend (MoltenVK path) + Metal backend + Common (macOS-only helper) + DolphinAnalytics
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-02 (merge `5e95d5e7f5`), 5 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Common/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Core/Common/CommonFuncs.h`, `Source/Core/Common/CommonFuncsObjC.mm`, `Source/Core/Core/DolphinAnalytics.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoBackends/Metal/MTLUtil.mm`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/DriverDetails.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/UberShaderPixel.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoBackends/Vulkan/VulkanContext.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/VideoBackends/Metal/MTLUtil.mm`, `Source/Core/VideoBackends/Vulkan/VulkanContext.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/UberShaderPixel.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Apple fixed the 'discard with early-Z' GPU driver bug in macOS 14. Upstream adds a Common::MacOSVersion/GetMacOSVersion() helper (new CommonFuncsObjC.mm) and makes DriverDetails::Init use the macOS version (major*100+minor) as the driver version for API_METAL and for Vulkan with DRIVER_PORTABILITY (MoltenVK). It limits BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z to macOS 11.0-13.x (1100..1400) and makes the uber pixel shader use the framebuffer-fetch discard emulation only when framebuffer fetch is available. It also drops the hacked-MoltenVK framebuffer-fetch support from the Vulkan backend: Vulkan no longer reports bSupportsFramebufferFetch, and on macOS <14 with an Apple GPU it now shows a PanicAlert recommending Metal or macOS 14+.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Not LOW because rubric rules 2/3 exclude it, even though the real impact on XR's targets is nil. All 5 commits apply cleanly on XR HEAD (alone/greedy clean; merge-tree re-verified for 750c6ed705, 5a94e63e42 and 26e58ea349). Every behavioral effect is gated to macOS: `#ifdef __APPLE__` in DriverDetails::Init, CommonFuncs.h and DolphinAnalytics; bug-table entries that only match OS_OSX; and the MoltenVK-only (is_moltenvk && Apple) framebuffer-fetch block. Windows D3D11/D3D12/Vulkan/GL and Quest Vulkan/GLES therefore get identical behavior and byte-identical uber-shader text. However, this is a shader-generation and backend-capability change in VideoCommon/VideoBackends. It edits two heavily XR-modified caution files: UberShaderPixel.cpp (XR's VR exact-screen-depth export and passthrough coverage modes) and VulkanContext.cpp (+524 in XR: multiview/FDM/coverage capability flags). It also removes a default (`bSupportsFramebufferFetch = false`) whose safety depends on XR's backend-info reset paths. None of this is a behavior-neutral cleanup, so it needs a quick human apply plus build check rather than an auto-pick.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** (1) Metal/MTLUtil.mm: XR only added the MTLPixelFormatR8Unorm <-> AbstractTextureFormat::R8 cases in ToAbstract/FromAbstract (~L333/L362, for the passthrough-coverage R8 format). Upstream's hunk is in PopulateBackendInfoFeatures at ~L256 (it replaces the cocoa_ver version calculation with 0.0), so the two are disjoint. (2) VideoCommon/UberShaderPixel.cpp: XR's 'Exact virtual-screen depth' block (`if (uid_data->vr_screen_exact_depth) depth = I_VR_SCREEN_DEPTH_NEAR + ...`) ends two lines above upstream's modified `if (early_depth && HasBug(BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z))` line. XR's ClearUnusedPixelShaderUidBits forces early_depth=0 under exact depth, and XR added UBER_PIXEL_SHADER_CODE_VERSION=5. Upstream's added `&& host_config.backend_shader_framebuffer_fetch` is only evaluated when HasBug is true (macOS), so XR's shader text and UIDs are unchanged and no code-version bump or cache invalidation is needed. (3) Vulkan/VulkanContext.cpp: XR's PopulateBackendInfoFeatures additions sit above the removed MoltenVK block: bSupportsMultiview, bSupportsVRFoveatedEFB, max_fragment_dual_src_attachments, and the R8 coverage query under `#if defined(_WIN32) \|\| defined(ANDROID)`, whose comment excludes MoltenVK because of its framebuffer-fetch blending path. XR does not use the removed locals device_name/vendor_id/is_moltenvk there; InitDriverDetails has its own. Upstream also removes the `bSupportsFramebufferFetch = false` default from PopulateBackendInfo (XR L539). That is safe because g_backend_info is reset to {} by VideoBackendBase::PopulateBackendInfo (VideoBackendBase.cpp:270, called from Core.cpp:273) on every boot, before VKMain::InitBackendInfo/Initialize; this includes XR's persisted-VR-context reuse path (VKMain.cpp:215/315). XR's OpenXRUtilitySession::InitializeVulkan (OpenXRUtilitySession.cpp:709-711) calls VulkanContext::PopulateBackendInfo without that reset, so a stale true value (e.g. left by a previous GLES run on Quest) would no longer be cleared there. That session renders only through ImGui_ImplVulkan, never reads the flag, and restores the saved g_backend_info, so it is unaffected. Not touched by this PR, but they consume the same flags: XR-modified PipelineUtils.cpp ApplyDriverBugs (EarlyWithFBFetch selection) and ShaderCache.cpp. Those flags never change on Windows/Quest. The new PanicAlertFmtT can only fire on macOS 11-13 with an Apple GPU on MoltenVK.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick the five commits as a unit, in landing order: 36d8dbc590 -> 750c6ed705 -> 98ca653774 -> 5a94e63e42 -> 26e58ea349. All apply cleanly on XR HEAD and need no XR code changes. Then build Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON) and the Quest flavor (assembleQuestDebug) to confirm VulkanContext.cpp compiles with the removed locals and the new Common/MsgHandler.h include. Optionally smoke-test one Vulkan VR game on PC and on Quest; uber shaders should be unchanged, so the shader cache stays valid. Do not split 26e58ea349 from 98ca653774 and 5a94e63e42 (see dependencies). Land this group before G157 (9ae9c12938) and G237 (e222665eb0), whose hunks were written against this PR's post-change context. XR's MoltenVK comment in PopulateBackendInfoFeatures becomes moot but needs no change.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** macOS is not an XR target platform (no Metal OpenXR backend; XR's passthrough coverage is already compiled out on macOS), so the practical risk is negligible. MEDIUM comes purely from the rubric (shader/backend behavior change in heavily XR-modified caution files). 36d8dbc590 alone is standalone-safe: it is an Apple-only Common helper plus an analytics refactor. ${FOUNDATION_LIBRARY} already exists at XR HEAD (root CMakeLists.txt:374), and Windows/Android compile all of it out. No Android, INI, STATE_VERSION or toolchain changes; DolphinLib.props/.vcxproj are unaffected (.mm is macOS-only). The upstream target 771fb154059c keeps this PR's end state (bug range 1100..1400, GetMacOSVersion in use), so there is no later revert. Downstream textual dependents: G157 9ae9c12938 (its MTLUtil.mm hunk context includes 750c6ed705's `DriverDetails::Init(..., 0.0,` line) and G237 e222665eb0 (its DolphinAnalytics.cpp include context assumes 36d8dbc590 has removed `#include <objc/message.h>`).
- **Commits:**
  - `36d8dbc590` CommonFuncsObjC: Make function to fetch the current macOS version common — **MEDIUM**: Adds Common::MacOSVersion and GetMacOSVersion() (CommonFuncs.h plus new CommonFuncsObjC.mm; APPLE-only target_sources entry and ${FOUNDATION_LIBRARY} link) and replaces DolphinAnalytics' raw objc_msgSend version query with it. Touches no XR-modified file and is compiled out on Windows/Android, so it is standalone-safe. Kept at group risk because it is the PR's prerequisite. G008 36f45dce44 only neighbors it textually in Common/CMakeLists.txt; both alone and greedy simulations apply clean, so it is not a dependency.
  - `750c6ed705` DriverDetails: Use macOS version as the driver version — **MEDIUM**: DriverDetails::Init now overrides m_version with the macOS version (major*100+minor) for API_METAL and for Vulkan with DRIVER_PORTABILITY, under #ifdef __APPLE__; Metal passes 0.0 instead of computing the version itself. It touches XR-modified MTLUtil.mm at ~L256, disjoint from XR's R8 format cases, and DriverDetails.cpp, which XR did not modify. No effect on Windows/Quest. It does change MoltenVK version semantics on macOS, but every other OS_OSX bug entry uses an unbounded -1/-1 range, so only 98ca653774's new range is affected.
  - `98ca653774` DriverDetails: Mark BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z as fixed in macOS 14.0+ — **MEDIUM**: Narrows BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z (Vulkan MoltenVK and Metal on Apple GPUs, OS_OSX only) from always to macOS 1100..1400. This disables the workaround on macOS 14+. It touches only DriverDetails.cpp, which XR did not modify, and has no effect off macOS. The range is only in macOS-version units once 750c6ed705 is applied.
  - `5a94e63e42` UberShaderPixel: Fix missing check for framebuffer fetch for BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z workaround — **MEDIUM**: The uber pixel shader now uses the `discard_fragment = real_ocol0 = initial_ocol0` framebuffer-fetch emulation only when host_config.backend_shader_framebuffer_fetch is set; otherwise it uses a real discard. This is in XR-modified UberShaderPixel.cpp, two lines below XR's exact-screen-depth block, and applies cleanly. The condition is only reachable when HasBug is true (macOS), so XR's generated uber shaders on Windows/Quest are byte-identical: no UID change and no UBER_PIXEL_SHADER_CODE_VERSION bump needed. It is a prerequisite for 26e58ea349, which turns MoltenVK framebuffer fetch off.
  - `26e58ea349` VulkanContext: Drop support for BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z workaround — **MEDIUM**: In XR-modified VulkanContext.cpp: removes the MoltenVK/Apple framebuffer-fetch detection and the bSupportsFramebufferFetch=false default in PopulateBackendInfo, and adds a PanicAlertFmtT (new Common/MsgHandler.h include) when BUG_BROKEN_DISCARD_WITH_EARLY_Z is present. The hunks are disjoint from XR's VR capability flags, and the removed locals are unused by XR. The removed default is safe because g_backend_info = {} runs on every boot (VideoBackendBase.cpp:270 via Core.cpp:273), and the reset-less OpenXRUtilitySession path never reads the flag. Without 98ca653774 the alert would fire for every Apple-GPU MoltenVK user; without 5a94e63e42 uber shaders on macOS <14 would use framebuffer fetch it no longer has.

### G097 — PR #14140 — VideoCommon: add a graphics mod feature to modify EFBs with a custom material, enhance bloom

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon GraphicsModSystem + Resources (custom materials/shaders), TextureCacheBase EFB-to-texture copy path, Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-03 (merge `798f581c38`), 12 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/PrintAction.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/PrintAction.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/ScaleAction.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/ScaleAction.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/SkipAction.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/SkipAction.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/GraphicsModAction.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/GraphicsModActionData.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/GraphicsModManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/CustomResourceManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/CustomResourceManager.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/MaterialResource.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/MaterialResource.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/ShaderResource.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/Resources/ShaderResource.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/CustomPipelineAction.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Runtime/Actions/CustomPipelineAction.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GraphicsModSystem/Config/GraphicsModGroup.cpp`, `Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods/All Games Blurred Bloom/all.txt`, `Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods/All Games Blurred Bloom/blur.ps.glsl`, `Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods/All Games Blurred Bloom/blur.rastershader`, `Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods/All Games Blurred Bloom/blur.vs.glsl`, `Data/Sys/Load/GraphicMods/All Games Blurred Bloom/blur_horizontal.rastermaterial` … and 59 more
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.h`
- **Upstream intent:** Add a graphics-mod 'AfterEFB' hook that can return a post-processing Material. CustomResourceManager/MaterialResource/ShaderResource gain a UID-less post-processing material/shader kind: generated GLSL fullscreen-triangle VS/PS, FramebufferShaderGen passthrough GS when host_config.stereo, and an RGBA8 Utility pipeline. CustomPipelineAction is rewritten to supply that material for EFB targets. TextureCacheBase::CopyRenderTargetToTexture runs the material chain on EFB-to-texture copies through the new ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry, which ping-pongs with a pool texture of the same config and swaps texture/framebuffer. Also adds hot-reload watching of the Sys/User GraphicMods dirs and recursive (subfolder) metadata.json discovery. Ships the all-games 'Bloom Blurred' and 'DOF Blurred' mods, bloom definitions for 16 more games, Pandora's Tower and Sonic Colors definition fixes, and splits De Blob 1/2 into 'Bloom Blurred'/'Bloom Removal' subfolder mods.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** An opt-in renderer feature inside CopyRenderTargetToTexture, the EFB copy function XR modifies most, plus a new utility shader-pipeline kind. That rules out LOW: it is a behavioral VideoCommon EFB/shader change. It is not HIGH: (a) the whole-PR merge-tree (798f581c38^1..798f581c38) onto 5e9a304a65 is conflict-free, and greedy_xr is clean for all 12 commits. (b) Every runtime path is gated by GFX_MODS_ENABLE (default false in GraphicsSettings.cpp and Android BooleanSetting.kt) plus per-mod m_enabled=false, and GraphicsModManager::Initialize loads nothing unless bGraphicMods is set, so default VR rendering is byte-identical. (c) The new pass is stereo-aware by design: passthrough GS with tex0.z=layer, and the shipped blur shader reads layer=int(tex0.z). This matches XR's own UseGeometryShaderForEFBCopies policy (GS into non-multiview 2-layer texture-cache framebuffers). Residual risk: once a user enables Bloom/DOF Blurred, the pass rewrites stereo EFB-copy textures that XR features consume, so it needs VR verification on D3D11/D3D12/Vulkan/Quest.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** TextureCacheBase.cpp: 6ba2d2e081 renames OnXFB->BeforeXFB and OnEFB->BeforeEFB, and the EFB struct->PreEFB, in the pre-copy graphics-mod block (XR HEAD lines ~2527-2550, which XR did not modify). 1f72403ec7 inserts the AfterEFB/ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry block in the copy_to_vram path right after the CopyEFBToCacheEntry call, which XR wrapped in the VR EFB-clear if/else (vr_clear_efb_min_width / ElementsGroupManager::ShouldClearEFBCopy -> SetAndClearFramebuffer, else CopyEFBToCacheEntry with XR's vram_src_rect/vram_linear_filter). Verified with merge-tree: the block lands after the else-branch closing brace, so the material also runs on VR-cleared (transparent) copies, which is harmless. ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry is appended after DecodeTextureOnGPU, where XR has no code. It reallocates from entry->texture->GetConfig(), so layers stay 2 in OpenXR stereo and native dims are unchanged. XR consumers of stereo EFB copies therefore keep valid layers/dims: the Metroid palette/thermal fixes in ApplyPaletteToEntry, ApplyVRPreserveStereoEFBFix, and IsBoundEfbCopy for AutoNativeEfbEffects. TextureCacheBase.h only gains a MaterialResource forward decl and a private method, disjoint from XR's header edits (GetBoundTextureHash/Name, IsBoundEfbCopy, vram_source_rect param, BackupConfig Metroid fields). No XR code calls the renamed APIs (OnEFB/OnXFB/GraphicsModActionData::EFB/GetMaterialFromAsset; git grep at 5e9a304a65 finds only upstream's own uses). XR's CustomShaderCache GetPipelineSync edits are unrelated. FramebufferState is u64 in XR with multiview/FDM bits; the post-processing pipeline uses GetRGBA8FramebufferState (multiview=0), which matches non-multiview texture-cache framebuffers (VKFramebuffer only goes multiview for the current EFB).
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate the whole PR in landing order (or cherry-pick merge 798f581c38 with -m 1). It applies with no conflicts. Then take G137 6059045c59 (range-loop fix inside ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry). Keep XR's VR EFB-clear if/else intact and leave the AfterEFB block after it, not inside either branch. Build Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON) and the Quest flavor; no CMake/props changes are needed because no source files are added. Test plan: (1) Graphics Mods disabled (default): confirm unchanged VR output in MP1 thermal/MP2 dark visor and MPT. (2) Enable Graphics Mods plus 'Bloom Blurred' in a VR-profile bloom game (Sonic Unleashed RSVE8P, MPT R3ME01, SMG RMGE01/TP) on D3D11, D3D12, Vulkan PC and Quest Vulkan/GLES. Check that both eyes show blurred bloom with no right-eye black/garbage (passthrough GS must write layer 1), and that there are no Vulkan validation errors from the GS utility pipeline on 2-layer non-multiview pool framebuffers. (3) Combine with an XR Clear-EFB override to confirm the copy stays transparent. (4) Test 'DOF Blurred' once. If a backend lacks GS, layer 1 is left as pool garbage; disable the mod for that backend rather than changing XR code.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G137 (Core: Address `-Wrange-loop-construct` warning.)
- **Notes:** There are no Data/Sys/GameSettings changes, so ini_video_setting_change=false. The new graphics-mod definitions cover VR-profile titles Metroid Prime Trilogy (R3M: efb 320x224 fmt4 bloom targets, which do not collide with XR's MP visor palette candidates 640x448, 408x286 and 640x448/528) and Sonic Unleashed (RSV). They also cover Metroid Other M (R3O), which XR's IsMetroidPrimeTrilogyWii() also matches. All definitions are inert unless the user enables a Bloom/DOF mod. The custom_pipeline action JSON schema changes ('passes'/'pixel_material_asset' -> 'material_asset'). XR ships no such mods, and the old CustomPipelineAction::OnDrawStarted was already a no-op. 9cde551e29 starts wtr filesystem-watcher threads on the Sys and User GraphicMods dirs, but only when Graphics Mods is enabled. 6b7a9e6ac3 makes mod discovery recursive (it also affects the Qt and Android graphics-mod lists via jni/Cheats/GraphicsModGroup.cpp; no Android source changes). DolphinQt CMake syncs Data/Sys with copy_directory, which never deletes. So building between db72877520 and 306daff517 leaves stale 'De Blob*/metadata.json' files in Binary/x64/Release/Sys that the recursive scan will pick up as duplicates; clean that Sys copy. Later upstream groups that build on this PR: G137 6059045c59 (edits ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry) and G139 95dec13203 (std::move cleanup of the MaterialResource/ShaderResource constructors and the GraphicsModManager create_action lambda added or reshaped here). G327 9263f6b7c6 does not depend on it. The MaterialResource::Data accessors used by 1f72403ec7 (GetPipeline/GetUniforms/GetTextures/GetNextMaterial), plus ReadStringFromJson, FilesystemWatcher::Watch, DOLPHIN_SYSTEM_GRAPHICS_MOD_DIR and the DoFileSearch(string_view, string_view, bool) overload, all already exist at XR HEAD.
- **Commits:**
  - `6ba2d2e081` VideoCommon: update graphics mod action interface — **MEDIUM**: Interface groundwork for the feature. Renames OnEFB->BeforeEFB and OnXFB->BeforeXFB, adds the virtual AfterEFB(PostEFB*) hook and the PostEFB struct (GraphicsModActionData.h now includes Resources/MaterialResource.h), and a DecoratedAction forwarder. The TextureCacheBase.cpp hunk is a pure rename in the pre-copy graphics-mod block that XR left unmodified. Behavior-neutral on its own, but it only makes sense together with the rest of the PR.
  - `2d8f955851` VideoCommon: enhance 'CustomResourceManager' for post processing — **MEDIUM**: New post-processing material/shader kind in Resources/ (no XR overlap). Makes m_uid optional, adds generated GLSL fullscreen VS/PS, a passthrough GS when backend_geometry_shaders && stereo, and an RGBA8 Utility pipeline, and renames GetMaterialFromAsset->GetDrawMaterialFromAsset (only internal callers). New shader-pipeline creation in VideoCommon. It is only reached through mods.
  - `7bfd43eb1a` VideoCommon: update CustomPipelineAction to get a Material when an EFB is received — **MEDIUM**: CustomPipelineAction now reads 'material_asset' instead of 'passes' and returns a post-processing material from AfterEFB. The old draw path was already a no-op, and no XR code or data uses custom_pipeline. Needs the new hook and the material API.
  - `1f72403ec7` VideoCommon: optionally apply materials to EFBs — **MEDIUM**: The XR-overlapping core. Adds the AfterEFB material pass into CopyRenderTargetToTexture copy_to_vram (merge lands after XR's VR EFB-clear if/else) and the new ApplyMaterialToCacheEntry. That function draws into a pool texture of identical config (2 layers in stereo) and swaps texture/framebuffer, then recurses through next_material. It changes EFB-copy texture contents when a mod is enabled. Gated by bGraphicMods.
  - `9cde551e29` VideoCommon: watch the user and system graphics mods directories for modified assets — **MEDIUM**: GraphicsModManager::Load now watches the Sys and User GraphicMods dirs for asset hot-reload (wtr watcher threads, only when Graphics Mods is enabled). Uses only existing helpers. Textual context depends on the earlier GraphicsModManager.cpp edit.
  - `6b7a9e6ac3` VideoCommon: allow graphics mods to be added to sub folders — **MEDIUM**: GraphicsModGroupConfig::Load scans each mod dir recursively for metadata.json (enables subfolder mods) using the existing DoFileSearch(string_view, string_view, bool). Affects the Qt and Android graphics-mod lists. No XR overlap. Required by 306daff517.
  - `ace082af23` Data: provide a blur mod for games with bloom — **MEDIUM**: Data only: the all-games 'Bloom Blurred' mod (custom_pipeline with material_asset, horizontal->vertical blur materials, a layer-aware texelFetch shader). Without the engine commits it would load but fail. Off by default.
  - `db72877520` Data: add more bloom graphics mods — **MEDIUM**: Data only: bloom group definitions for 16 games, including VR-profile titles MPT R3M (320x224 fmt4) and Sonic Unleashed RSV, plus Metroid Other M R3O. Inert unless a Bloom mod is enabled. They work even with the existing Bloom Removal/Native Resolution mods. Kept at the group risk as part of the PR.

### G103 — PR #14550 — Improve NAND import progress dialog

- **Subsystem:** DiscIO NANDImporter + DolphinQt MainWindow (NAND import UI) + Android JNI WiiUtils
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-04 (merge `1ee93df287`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/jni/WiiUtils.cpp`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/NANDImporter.cpp`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/NANDImporter.h`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/MainWindow.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/MainWindow.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Give the NAND import a real progress bar with Cancel: - NANDImporter::UpdateCallback becomes bool(Step{Loading,Extracting}, int cur, int max), and returning true cancels. - ReadNANDBin reports per-block progress and can abort. - New ExtractFiles() counts FAT chain ends (0xFFFB) as the maximum. - ProcessEntry returns bool so cancel and errors propagate. - DolphinQt's OnImportNANDBackup shows a progress bar with a Cancel button instead of the elapsed-time label. - The Android JNI WiiUtils importNANDBin lambda is adapted to the new signature (returns false, no UI yet).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Applies cleanly alone and in greedy_xr and has nothing to do with VR. It is MEDIUM by rule 2: the only XR-modified file, DolphinQt/MainWindow.cpp, gets a behavior change. The hunk is confined to MainWindow::OnImportNANDBackup (~L1863-1900), away from XR's MainWindow hunks at ~L211, ~L694 ConnectToolBar, ~L1277 HideRenderWidget, ~L1404 ShowGraphicsWindow and ~L2024 ShowTASInput. It also edits Android jni WiiUtils.cpp, whose JNI entry importNANDBin is called from XR-modified MainPresenter.kt; the JNI signature is unchanged. Effort should be low.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** MainWindow.cpp: no textual or functional overlap with XR's render-widget, graphics-window or toolbar edits. NANDImporter.h/.cpp and jni/WiiUtils.cpp are not XR-modified. ImportNANDBin has no other callers in XR (MenuBar and WiiUpdate only use ExtractCertificates, which is unchanged). MainPresenter.kt:325 WiiUtils.importNANDBin(path) keeps the same API.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick f06aef4f83 as-is. Build Windows (Build-vs2022, VS2022, Qt 6.5.1, ENABLE_VR=ON) and questDebug. The code is VS2022-safe: std::ranges::count and std::ignore are fine, and the narrowing to u16 hits C4244, which the CMake build disables. Smoke-test Tools > Import BootMii NAND Backup on PC, including Cancel. Take the later NANDImporter hardening commits (3abadcd507, 2faa9760d3 path traversal, 29f1bc4d4c) after this one.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** The earlier MainWindow.cpp commit 7209cf87cb (G076, status bar) has no semantic link to this one.
- **Commits:**
  - `f06aef4f83` Improve NAND import progress dialog — **MEDIUM**: Behavior change in XR-modified MainWindow.cpp, in a function disjoint from XR's edits, plus the Android JNI callback update.

### G120 — PR #14500 — Replace `const std::vector&` arguments with `std::span`

- **Upstream intent:** A behavior-neutral signature modernization across 79 files. `const std::vector<T>&` parameters become `std::span<const T>`. It adds a `DHCPPacket::AddOption(initializer_list)` overload and an `HttpRequest::Post(string_view)` overload, and drops a few now-unneeded includes (`<vector>` and `Common/BitSet.h` from VertexShaderManager.h, `<map>` from ES.h, `<ranges>` from Volume.cpp).

The 5 XR-modified files are touched only in hunks that don't overlap XR's edits: the `VertexShaderManager::SetConstants` parameter type, `Config::SetIsoPaths`, `State.cpp` `GetEmptySlot` (not the STATE_VERSION line), the `ConfigStringChoice` constructors, and `WiimoteControllersWidget::OnBluetoothAdapterRefreshComplete`. The alone-on-XR conflicts (GeckoCodeConfig.h, DiscIO/Volume.cpp) are only missing context from G028 and G007, both of which were classified LOW. A grep of the full XR diff (including Source/Android) found no XR-added caller of any re-signatured function. XR also has no override of the changed virtuals (`GBAHostInterface::FrameEnded`, `DebugInterface::LoadWatchesFromStrings`, `NetPlayUI::ShowChunkedProgressDialog`, `WiiSave` `WriteFiles`). All `ConfigStringChoice` callers at XR HEAD are upstream callers that pass named vectors.

XR's edits are in `LoadProjectionMatrix`/`SetProjectionMatrix` (freelook restructure, `dirty=true`) and in the viewport block inside `SetConstants` (vertex rounding disabled for StereoMode::OpenXR). The body only range-iterates `textures`, and the sole caller, VertexManagerBase.cpp:1015, passes a `std::vector<std::string>`. VertexShaderManager.h (not XR-modified) drops `<vector>` and `Common/BitSet.h`. None of the XR-added lines in its includers (VertexManagerBase, GeometryShaderManager, BPFunctions, VertexLoaderManager, VideoBackendBase, VK/D3D vertex managers) use `std::vector` or `BitSet`, and no VR/* file includes the header. MainSettings.cpp: the `SetIsoPaths` hunk is disjoint from XR's hunks at about lines 55 and 510. State.cpp: the `GetEmptySlot` hunk is disjoint from XR's compression/`WaitForPendingSaves`/STATE_VERSION edits. ConfigChoice.h: XR edited the `ConfigChoiceMap` constructor (fallback_layer); upstream edits `ConfigStringChoice`. WiimoteControllersWidget.cpp/.h: disjoint from XR's OpenXR Wii Remote source combo code (`GetSourceFromCombo`/`IsConfigurableWiimoteSource`).
- **Suggested integration approach:** Auto cherry-pick f6a67aa6e7 after its textual prerequisites d2e381090c (G028) and 1d74321212/7b372db559 (G007). No XR adaptation is needed. Run a normal VS2022 build and a Quest build. The Android caller `Config::SetIsoPaths(JStringArrayToVector(...))` passes a temporary vector, which is legal for `span<const std::string>`.
- **Upstream dependencies:** d2e381090c (G028), 1d74321212 (G007), 7b372db559 (G007)
- **Notes:** The Volume.cpp hunk removes the `#include <ranges>` that G007 added. `std::ranges::replace_if` lives in `<algorithm>`, which is included transitively, and upstream CI (MSVC at the time) built it. Later commits in this range (G139, G157) have this commit in their history but apply without it, except where noted.
- **Commits:**
  - `f6a67aa6e7` Use more std::span arguments — **MEDIUM**: Signature-only span refactor. The XR-modified files are touched only in hunks clear of XR's edits; greedy_xr is clean; the alone conflicts come from missing G028/G007 context.
- **Adversarial-review downgrades:**
  - skeptic (xr) on `f6a67aa6e7`: LOW → MEDIUM: REFUTED: the classifier's 'no behavior changes' claim is wrong. The XR-overlap hunks themselves are neutral (verified below), but the commit adds a real lifetime regression that compiles cleanly and passes the unit tests. Two lambdas posted with QueueOnObject, which always uses Qt::QueuedConnection and so runs later on the GUI thread, now capture a non-owning std::span. Before the commit they copied the vector. (1) NetPlayDialog::ShowChunkedProgressDialog: [this,title,data_size,players] now captures std::span<const int> pointing at the function-local std::vector<int> players in NetPlayClient::OnChunkedDataStart (upstream NetPlayClient.cpp:548-550) and NetPlayServer::ChunkedDataThreadFunc (~2447-2475). The caller's vector is destroyed before the queued lambda runs, and ChunkedProgressDialog::show then calls Common::Contains(players, player->pid) on freed memory. That is a use-after-free on every NetPlay chunked-data transfer (save/SD sync). (2) GBAHost::FrameEnded: the lambda now holds a span into HW::GBA::Core::m_video_buffer, and GBAWidget::SetVideoBuffer reads it later on the GUI thread. The emulation side keeps rewriting that buffer (GBACore.cpp:459/726; resize/clear at 421-435; DoState at 704), and it is freed when the core is destroyed. Before, a snapshot was taken. The result is a data race with torn GBA frames, or a use-after-free at teardown. Upstream master 771fb154059c still has both lambdas unchanged, and nothing later in range fixes them (G139 95dec13203 only touches NetPlayDialog::show's m_nickname). A human should either cherry-pick with a 2-line local fix (capture owning copies, e.g. std::vector<int>(players.begin(), players.end()) and std::vector<u32>(video_buffer.begin(), video_buffer.end())) or accept the upstream bug on purpose. XR side, all checked and neutral: VertexShaderManager::SetConstants only range-iterates textures (XR line 413), and its only caller (VertexManagerBase.cpp:1015) passes a named local std::vector. No XR-added line in any of the 14 includers of VertexShaderManager.h uses std::vector or BitSet, so dropping <vector>/BitSet.h is safe; line 405 (std::vector<GraphicsModAction*>) is upstream code that upstream also compiles. SetIsoPaths and the ConfigStringChoice constructors consume the span synchronously and store nothing. XR-modified callers (Settings.cpp:461/473, EnhancementsWidget.cpp:148, GeneralWidget.cpp:62) pass named vectors. Android: the commit touches no Android files. The callers GameFileCache.cpp:55 (passes a temporary vector, which is fine for span<const T>), GeckoCheat.cpp:183 and PatchCheat.cpp:159 are not XR-modified, and no JNI signature changes. ConfigChoice.h and MainSettings.h have the same include set as upstream. It also needs G007/G028 context to apply.

### G122 — PR #14336 — VideoCommon: Invert interaction between Arbitrary Mipmap Detection and GPU Texture Decoding (the former disables the latter)

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon VideoConfig (GPU texture decoding gating) + DolphinQt Graphics panes + GameSettings INIs
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-12 (merge `b62bfe4b30`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/GameSettings/DAX.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GMS.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GZ2.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GZL.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RMG.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RZD.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SB4.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SOU.ini`, `Source/Core/Common/Config/Config.h`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/AdvancedWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/EnhancementsWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/EnhancementsWidget.h`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/GraphicsPane.h`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/HacksWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VideoConfig.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/AdvancedWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/EnhancementsWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/EnhancementsWidget.h`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/Graphics/HacksWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VideoConfig.h`
- **Upstream intent:** Make Arbitrary Mipmap Detection take priority. `VideoConfig::UseGPUTextureDecoding()` now also requires `!bArbitraryMipmapDetection`. The Qt panes invert the enable/disable relation: the GPU Texture Decoding checkbox greys out when mipmap detection is on, using the new `GraphicsPane::UpdateGPUTextureDecoding` signal. `EnhancementsWidget::ReadSetting` is replaced by a new `Config::Get(const Layer*, const Info<T>&)` helper in Common/Config/Config.h, and the dead `EnhancementsWidget::OnConfigChanged` is removed. `EnableGPUTextureDecoding = False` is removed from 8 game INIs that set ArbitraryMipmapDetection=True, because the code now enforces it.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** There is a real conflict in XR-modified EnhancementsWidget.cpp, confirmed with merge-tree. (1) In the constructor, upstream replaces the `UseGPUTextureDecodingChanged` connect right where XR added `connect(&Settings::Instance(), &Settings::ConfigChanged, this, &EnhancementsWidget::UpdateStereoscopyAvailability)`. (2) Upstream deletes `OnConfigChanged()`, into which XR inserted `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability()`. The commit also changes runtime behavior in VideoConfig.h (XR-modified, a config VR reads): `UseGPUTextureDecoding` gates the RAM-texture decode path and the XFB-from-RAM decode path in TextureCacheBase (XR lines about 1937 and 2131). It also touches XR-modified Qt graphics panes (HacksWidget, AdvancedWidget) and INIs of VR-profiled games. Small, obvious conflict plus a VideoCommon change with clear intent, so MEDIUM.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** EnhancementsWidget.cpp: conflict 1 is the constructor connect block (XR's `Settings::ConfigChanged` → `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability`). Conflict 2 is `OnConfigChanged()` (XR line about 413). That function is private and never connected at MB or XR HEAD, so XR's added call there was dead code; the live gating comes from XR's constructor connect and `OnBackendChanged`. XR's `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability` reads `Config::Get(GFX_VR_ENABLE_OPENXR)` and isn't touched by the `ReadSetting` → `Get(m_game_layer, ...)` rewrite. The `m_stereoscopy_box` rename merges cleanly. EnhancementsWidget.h: upstream removes the `ReadSetting`/`OnConfigChanged` declarations, while XR's `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability` declaration and `QGroupBox` forward declaration merge cleanly. HacksWidget.cpp: XR's 3-line VR comment above `OnBackendChanged(...)` merges cleanly. AdvancedWidget.cpp: XR's Command Buffers in Flight control is in a separate area. VideoConfig.h: the `UseGPUTextureDecoding` hunk merges cleanly; no VR code calls it. XR did not modify Data/Sys/GameSettings, and the GameSettingsVR profiles set neither ArbitraryMipmapDetection nor EnableGPUTextureDecoding.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick fdbc2883ef and immediately 8971b3b67a (G124, the upstream fixup that restores the initial enabled state; bug 14024). In EnhancementsWidget.cpp: (1) keep upstream's `Get(m_game_layer, ...)` texture-filtering lambda and its new `m_arbitrary_mipmap_detection` → `UpdateGPUTextureDecoding` connect, and also keep XR's `Settings::ConfigChanged` → `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability` connect. (2) Accept the deletion of `OnConfigChanged()` and its declaration, dropping XR's dead call inside it, while keeping XR's `UpdateStereoscopyAvailability` declaration and `m_stereoscopy_box` member. Take the INI hunks as-is. Then build on VS2022 and Quest. In the global Graphics window and the per-game GameConfigWidget, check that the Stereoscopy box still greys out when OpenXR is enabled and that the GPU Texture Decoding and Arbitrary Mipmap checkboxes enable and disable correctly.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G124 (VideoCommon: Fixup #14336)
- **Notes:** INI changes: removes `EnableGPUTextureDecoding = False` from DAX, GMS, GZ2, GZL, RMG, RZD, SB4 and SOU. VR-profiled ids affected: GMSE01/GMSE04/GMSP01, GZ2E01, GZLE01, RMGE01, SOUE01. The rest of the GZ2/GZL/RZD changes are whitespace in comments. This is not an EFB/XFB/copy/vertex-rounding key, and it is behavior-neutral given the new code gating, because those INIs keep ArbitraryMipmapDetection=True. The only change for those games is when a user INI sets ArbitraryMipmapDetection=False; GPU decoding then follows the global setting. G146's StereoMode-rename commits need this group's EnhancementsWidget.cpp context.
- **Commits:**
  - `fdbc2883ef` VideoCommon: Invert interaction between Arbitrary Mipmap Detection and GPU Texture Decoding (the former disables the latter) Split from #14293. It makes sense for a setting that changes visual output to have priority over a setting that barely makes any difference. — **MEDIUM**: Real XR conflict in EnhancementsWidget.cpp (constructor connect and deleted `OnConfigChanged`) plus a texture-decoding gating behavior change in XR-modified VideoConfig.h.

Both were verified to 3-way-merge cleanly onto XR's Settings.cpp in that order, without needing G139. Expected result on XR Windows with Qt 6.5.1: the QSS block is compiled out and nothing changes visually. On Linux builds with system Qt >= 6.6, spot-check VRPane and override-editor group boxes under the Fusion styles.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G140 (DolphinQt: Use custom style for `QGroupBox` only with Qt 6.6)
- **Notes:** If XR ever adopts upstream's Windows Qt 6.8.3 bump (G207, e87225beb2), this QSS becomes active on Windows; review VR pane layouts at that point.
- **Commits:**

MainWindow.cpp, Settings.cpp/.h, FileUtil `ScanDirectoryTree`, and ConfigChoice.h `ConfigComplexChoice` are all in hunks that don't overlap XR's VR additions.

### G145 — PR #14608 — GCAdapter: Automatically start and stop thread

- **Subsystem:** InputCommon/GCAdapter + Android JNI/Kotlin (NativeLibrary, Settings) + DolphinQt GamecubeControllersWidget
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-24 (merge `a94854309b`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/NativeLibrary.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/model/Settings.kt`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Config/GamecubeControllersWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/InputCommon/GCAdapter.cpp`, `Source/Core/InputCommon/GCAdapter.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/NativeLibrary.kt`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Move the lifecycle of the GC-adapter scan thread into GCAdapter.cpp. RefreshConfig(), which is already the Config-changed callback, now calls StartScanThread() or StopScanThread() depending on whether any SI port is SIDEVICE_WIIU_ADAPTER. StartScanThread/StopScanThread become file-static, UseAdapter() is removed (uses become s_is_adapter_wanted), and all external callers are deleted: Qt GamecubeControllersWidget::SaveSettings, Android Settings.saveSettings -> NativeLibrary.UpdateGCAdapterScanThread() and its JNI export in MainAndroid.cpp.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** It edits two XR-modified Android files (NativeLibrary.kt, jni/MainAndroid.cpp) and removes a JNI export, so rubric rule 6 makes it not LOW. The cherry-pick conflicts both alone and in greedy_xr in MainAndroid.cpp: upstream deletes `#include "InputCommon/GCAdapter.h"`, and XR inserted ControlReference.h/ControllerEmu.h/ControllerInterface.h before that line and InputConfig.h after it. The conflict is trivial. A merge-tree check (base cc01a60347^) shows every other hunk auto-merges and leaves no remaining UseAdapter/StartScanThread/StopScanThread/UpdateGCAdapterScanThread references. It also changes threading behavior: the scan thread is now started and joined from Config::OnConfigChanged callbacks, which run under s_callbacks_lock on whichever thread changed config. XR changes config at runtime (VR hotkeys via HotkeyScheduler and the Android HotkeyDispatcher thread; VR profile apply/clear in ConfigManager.cpp ClearAppliedVRSettings/DeleteKey), so those paths now also run GCAdapter RefreshConfig -> Start/StopScanThread. These calls do nothing unless the adapter-wanted state flips. The commit introduces the s_is_adapter_wanted data race that G147 fixes and makes the Android hotplug-callback double registration (IllegalStateException) that G263 fixes easier to hit. It is not HIGH: the change is small, touches no render/VR code, and no XR logic uses the removed API.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp: XR's include additions (lines ~62-67; ControlReference.h, ControllerEmu.h, ControllerInterface.h, InputConfig.h, needed by XR's Java_..._ApplyOpenXRControllerMapper) sit right around the removed GCAdapter.h include, which gives a textual conflict. The removed Java_org_dolphinemu_dolphinemu_NativeLibrary_UpdateGCAdapterScanThread (XR HEAD line ~710) is untouched upstream code; XR's own JNI (RequestOpenXRRecenter, Start/Get/Apply/StopOpenXRControllerMapper, Run() SetAndroidAppInfo/recenter/HotkeyDispatcher) never uses GCAdapter. NativeLibrary.kt: XR's edits (Activity/Intent/MainActivity imports, OpenXR external funs after StopEmulation, finishEmulationActivity relaunching MainActivity) are disjoint from the removed `external fun UpdateGCAdapterScanThread()` at ~line 303 and auto-merge. At 5e9a304a65, git grep finds UpdateGCAdapterScanThread/StartScanThread/StopScanThread/UseAdapter only in upstream-owned files (Settings.kt, GamecubeControllersWidget.cpp, MainAndroid.cpp, GCAdapter.cpp/.h). No XR-modified or XR-added Android file (quest flavor, QuestVrSettings, SettingsFragmentPresenter, etc.) references them. GCAdapter.cpp/.h, Settings.kt and GamecubeControllersWidget.cpp are not XR-modified, and XR's UICommon.cpp change (D_GAMESETTINGSVR_IDX dir) is unrelated to GCAdapter::Init/Shutdown.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick cc01a60347. In the MainAndroid.cpp include block, keep XR's four added InputCommon includes (ControlReference.h, ControllerEmu.h, ControllerInterface.h, InputConfig.h) and drop only `#include "InputCommon/GCAdapter.h"`; accept all other hunks unchanged. Afterwards `git grep -n "UpdateGCAdapterScanThread\\|GCAdapter::UseAdapter\\|GCAdapter::StartScanThread\\|GCAdapter::StopScanThread"` must return nothing. Integrate G147 (ac78e52cf9, makes s_is_adapter_wanted/s_config_rumble_enabled atomic) and G263 (05855a37f4, deregisters the hotplug callback when the scan thread exits) right after, as one unit. Build Windows (Build-vs2022, ENABLE_VR=ON) and Quest (build-quest.ps1). Smoke test: set a port to 'GameCube Adapter for Wii U' in Qt and in the Quest settings UI and check it is detected without a restart; switch back and check the thread stops; boot and stop a VR game and spam VR hotkeys to confirm no hitch or deadlock.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G147 (GCAdapter: Fix data races), G263 (GCAdapter: Deregister callback when scan thread exits)
- **Notes:** G054 (afc2920a5f, 0e8db4fa22), the earlier GCAdapter fixes, are NOT required: GCAdapter.cpp 3-way-merges onto XR HEAD without them. Keep landing order anyway. Downstream: G147 conflicts textually without this commit (that is its greedyConf=1). G160 (c73dde2193, 'Android: Remove HostThreadLock') deletes the HostThreadLock struct; if G145 were skipped, the surviving UpdateGCAdapterScanThread (which uses HostThreadLock) would break the Android build, so integrate G145 before G160. G131 (GCAdapter.java -> Kotlin) is unrelated. On Quest with no port set to the WiiU adapter the scan thread never starts, same as before.
- **Commits:**
  - `cc01a60347` GCAdapter: Automatically start and stop thread — **MEDIUM**: Edits XR-modified MainAndroid.cpp (trivial include-block conflict) and NativeLibrary.kt (auto-merges) and removes a JNI export no XR code uses. Moves scan-thread start/stop into the Config-changed callback (threading change). Needs follow-ups G147/G263.

### G152 — PR #14614 — GameSettings: Disable EFBToTexture for "Metroid Prime 2: Dark Echoes (Wii)" to fix the scan visor.

- **Subsystem:** GameSettings INI (Data/Sys/GameSettings) / Metroid Prime video hacks
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-27 (merge `77b778302c`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/GameSettings/G2M.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/G2ME01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/G2MP01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GM8.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GM8E01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/P2M.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R32.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R3I.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R3M.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RM3.ini`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Add R32.ini (R32J01 Wii de Asobu Metroid Prime 2) with EFBToTextureEnable=False (fixes the scan visor) and CPUCull=True. Add R3I.ini (R3IJ01 Wii de Asobu Metroid Prime) with CPUCull=True. Clean up comments and empty sections in the other Metroid INIs (G2M, G2ME01, G2MP01, GM8, GM8E01, P2M, R3M, RM3).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Rule 7: an EFB-copy setting change on a Metroid title, a flagship XR VR target. For R32 it newly forces EFB copies to RAM. XR's stereo EFB copies are 2-layer only in VRAM, and RAM-reloaded copies are mono. XR special-cases R32 in code: TextureCacheBase.cpp IsMetroidPrime2WiiStandalone feeds IsMetroidPrime2FullViewDarkTextureCandidate and IsMetroidPrime2DarkHighlightTextureCandidate, both requiring layers>=2. VertexManagerBase.cpp GetMetroidProfileForGameID maps R3I to Prime1Wii and R32 to Prime2Wii, and GetMetroidHydraHudSettings gates on R3I. CPUCull=True for R3I and R32 also matters in VR: XR's VertexLoaderManager cpu_cull_allowed_in_vr gate exists because CPU culling against the game frustum can drop geometry visible in the wider VR FOV. XR's GM8E01 and RM3E01 VR profiles set DisableCPUCull=True, but R3I and R32 have no VR profile. All edits to the VR-profiled files (G2M, G2ME01, GM8, GM8E01, P2M, R3M, RM3) only remove comments or empty sections, so their effective settings are unchanged. The commit applies cleanly.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.cpp: IsMetroidPrime2WiiStandalone (R32) and the MP2 dark-visor texture candidates (need stereo layers>=2). VertexManagerBase.cpp: GetMetroidProfileForGameID (R3I/R32) and GetMetroidHydraHudSettings (R3I). VertexLoaderManager.cpp: cpu_cull_allowed_in_vr / GFX_VR_DISABLE_CPU_CULL. GameSettingsVR GM8E01.ini and RM3E01.ini set DisableCPUCull=True, and there is no R3I or R32 VR profile. No file-level overlap: XR never modified Data/Sys/GameSettings.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick as-is (clean, data-only). Then VR device-test R32J01: scan, dark and echo visors in both eyes, now on the EFB-to-RAM path like G2M. With ShaderHunter debug logging on, the VR_PALETTE log shows whether the MP2 dark-visor candidates still see layers>=2. Also check R3IJ01 and R32J01 for peripheral geometry pop-out caused by CPUCull. If culling artifacts appear, the data-only remedy is a GameSettingsVR R3I/R32 profile with DisableCPUCull = True, mirroring GM8E01/RM3E01. No XR code change is needed.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **INI video-setting change:** yes (EFB/XFB/copy/vertex-rounding settings)
- **Notes:** The NPC MP2 game-ID prefix is R32; the brief's 'R2M' should read R32. IsMetroidVulkanPaletteFixGame and IsMetroidD3DPaletteFixGame exclude R32, so R32 never had XR's layered palette fix; only the dark-visor candidates include it. R3M.ini still forces EFBToTextureEnable, ImmediateXFB, XFBToTexture and DeferEFBCopies to False, unchanged. The new R32/R3I INIs do not set SafeTextureCacheColorSamples=512 the way G2M/GM8 do.
- **Commits:**
  - `b70e28a0f6` GameSettings: Disable EFBToTexture for "Metroid Prime 2: Dark Echoes (Wii)" to fix the scan visor and clean up the other Metroid Prime ini files. — **MEDIUM**: Forces EFBToTextureEnable=False plus CPUCull on R32 and CPUCull on R3I, both Metroid titles XR special-cases. The other file edits are cosmetic.

### G153 — PR #14617 — GameSettings: Set SafeTextureCacheColorSamples for "Sonic and the Secret Rings".

- **Subsystem:** GameSettings INI (Data/Sys/GameSettings) / texture cache accuracy
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-27 (merge `d47d5431a1`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/GameSettings/DSR.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RSR.ini`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Set SafeTextureCacheColorSamples=2048 for Sonic and the Secret Rings (DSRJ8P, RSR*) to fix glitching text in the ring customization menu; comment cleanup (RealWiiRemoteRepeatReports=False unchanged).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Rule 7 would call a texture-cache-accuracy change LOW. I raised it to MEDIUM because of a concrete XR interaction. RSRP8P has an XR VR profile whose enabled TextureElementOverrides use handling=screen to route dynamic text and number textures to the virtual screen by hash: $Numbers (577 hashes), $Text - Abilities (476), $Text (261), $Menu (10). Raising the sample count from 128 to 2048 changes which text-texture contents the cache detects (exactly the ring-menu text this PR fixes). Newly detected textures get new content XXH64 names (TextureCacheBase::GetBoundTextureHash uses texture_info_name) that are missing from XR's captured lists. EFB-copy textures use the fallback entry->hash from TCacheEntry::CalculateHash, which depends directly on the sample count, so those hashes change too. Result: possible VR-visible regression where some menu text renders in-world instead of on the virtual screen. The commit itself is clean and data-only.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Data/Sys/GameSettingsVR/RSRP8P.ini TextureElementOverride lists (1,329 texture= hashes). Hash source: TextureCacheBase::GetBoundTextureHash (content XXH64 from the dump name, with fallback to the sample-dependent entry->hash) consumed by VertexManagerBase and TextureElementManager::CaptureDrawTextures. No file-level overlap.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick as-is. Then test RSRP8P in VR: ring customization, ability and number text, and the HUD should stay on the virtual screen. If some text drops out, re-capture the missing hashes with XR's texture override hunter and add them to GameSettingsVR/RSRP8P.ini (data-only; no XR code change).
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** SafeTextureCacheColorSamples is texture-cache accuracy, not EFB/XFB/copy, hence ini_video_setting_change=false. 2048 samples adds CPU hashing cost, which is minor but more noticeable on Quest standalone. G244 (a0f514cf60) later edits RSR.ini (adds codes); keep landing order.
- **Commits:**
  - `dbb80ce296` GameSettings: Set SafeTextureCacheColorSamples for "Sonic and the Secret Rings" to fix glitching text in the ring customization menu. — **MEDIUM**: Changes texture-cache change detection for a VR-profiled game whose XR overrides are keyed on text-texture hashes.

### G154 — PR #14582 — GameSettings: Allow ImmediateXFB for a handful of games that seem fine with it.

- **Subsystem:** GameSettings INI (Data/Sys/GameSettings) / XFB presentation
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-27 (merge `ab6b30afe2`), 2 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/GameSettings/G6S.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GCP.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GFO.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GFW.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GHQ.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GKB.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GRK.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GTW.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GWL.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R3B.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RBW.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RED.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/WTX.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RO7.ini`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Remove the forced ImmediateXFBEnable=False from G6S, GCP (deleted), GFO, GFW, GHQ (deleted), GKB, GRK, GTW (deleted), GWL, R3B (deleted), RBW, RED (deleted) and WTX, because these games work with ImmediateXFB. For RO7 (Spyro: Eternal Night), also drop the forced EFBAccessEnable=True. Boilerplate cleanup and a few title fixes.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Rule 7: XFB setting change on a VR-profiled game. GHQ.ini (Simpsons Hit & Run) is deleted, and GHQE7D has an XR VR profile (camera_anchor texture override, viewport-signature element overrides, HideObject black bars). XR derives its VR pose and present path from ImmediateXFB: VideoConfig::VRLockHeadPosePerFrame() = !bImmediateXFB drives the GeometryShaderManager pose refresh, Present.cpp invalidation, BPStructs EFB-clear deferral and the OpenXRManager lock. On Quest, XR defaults ImmediateXFB to true (GraphicsSettings.cpp DEFAULT_IMMEDIATE_XFB under __ANDROID__&&ENABLE_VR, and QuestVrSettings.applyRecommendedDefaults writes GFX_HACK_IMMEDIATE_XFB=true). So on Quest, GHQE7D and the other 13 titles silently move from the deferred-XFB/XFB-pose-stamp path to immediate XFB. On PC this only affects users who enabled ImmediateXFB. c8dd2c9fc4 touches only RO7 (no VR profile) and is independent, so it is LOW.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Behavioral, not textual. GameSettingsVR/GHQE7D.ini profile. VideoConfig.h:491 VRLockHeadPosePerFrame() = !bImmediateXFB, consumed by GeometryShaderManager.cpp:286-291, Present.cpp:890-918, BPStructs.cpp:410-432 and OpenXRManager.cpp:3104. Quest default ImmediateXFB=true: GraphicsSettings.cpp:230-236 and QuestVrSettings.kt:244. XR never modified Data/Sys/GameSettings.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick c8dd2c9fc4 freely. Cherry-pick 75946bd385, then device-test Simpsons Hit & Run (GHQE7D) on Quest (ImmediateXFB now on by default) and on PC with ImmediateXFB on: check camera-anchor stability, the minimap element override, black-bar hiding and head-tracking judder. If it regresses, apply the commit but keep upstream's pre-change GHQ.ini (restore that one file during the cherry-pick). This is a data-only divergence with no XR code change.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G138 (GameSettings: Add comments with the reason for disabling Imm), G128 (GameSettings: Disable ImmediateXFB for "Who Wants To Be A Mi)
- **INI video-setting change:** yes (EFB/XFB/copy/vertex-rounding settings)
- **Notes:** None of the other titles (G6S, GCP, GFO, GFW, GKB, GRK, GTW, GWL, R3B, RBW, RED, WTX, RO7) has an XR VR profile or XR code references. Upstream tested them on stock Dolphin only. GKB keeps EFBToTextureEnable=False and DeferEFBCopies=False. RO7 losing EFBAccessEnable only reduces EFB readbacks. G138/G128 are the same author's ImmediateXFB audit series (review with the same Quest-default lens).
- **Commits:**
  - `75946bd385` GameSettings: Allow ImmediateXFB for a handful of games that seem fine with it. — **MEDIUM**: Deletes GHQ.ini's ImmediateXFBEnable=False for VR-profiled GHQE7D. On Quest (XR default ImmediateXFB=true) this flips its VR pose/present path.
  - `c8dd2c9fc4` GameSettings: The Legend of Spyro: The Eternal Night. — **MEDIUM**: Independent: RO7.ini only (no VR profile, not Metroid). XFB/EFB-access change on a non-VR title is LOW per rule 7 (noted).

### G164 — PR #14632 — Unconditionally lock in ControllerInterface::UpdateInput

- **Subsystem:** InputCommon/ControllerInterface core (device-update locking / threading)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-05-06 (merge `cf20cdabd4`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/InputCommon/ControllerInterface/ControllerInterface.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/InputCommon/ControllerInterface/ControllerInterface.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** ControllerInterface::UpdateInput() used m_devices_mutex.try_lock() and silently skipped the whole input update when another thread held the lock ('prefer outdated values over blocking UI or CPU thread'). Now it always blocks on the lock so no poll is ever dropped (fixes pipe inputs missing frames).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Applies clean (alone and greedy) and XR's edits in ControllerInterface.cpp are textually disjoint, but this is a threading/timing behavior change in InputCommon core plumbing (rubric caution area). The CPU thread calls UpdateInput at SI rate (Core/HW/SI/SI.cpp:566) and at Wiimote rate right after CoreTiming::Throttle (IOS/USB/Bluetooth/BTEmu.cpp:346); after this commit those calls wait whenever another thread is iterating all devices. XR added extra pollers: Android HotkeyDispatcher::Run (Source/Android/jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp:91, Host channel every 11 ms for the whole emulation session, on a thread deliberately lowered to nice 10) and OpenXRUtilitySession::DrawAndUpdate (VideoCommon/VR/OpenXRUtilitySession.cpp:322). Inside the now-blocking section, XR's OpenXRDevice::UpdateInput (InputCommon/ControllerInterface/OpenXR/OpenXR.cpp:62-64) takes OpenXRInputState::s_state_mutex, which OpenXRManager::SetControllers (OpenXRManager.cpp:2213) takes on the VR thread. On Quest this enables priority inversion (performance-core-pinned CPU thread waiting on a nice-10 thread holding m_devices_mutex) -> possible VR frame-pacing hitches. No deadlock path found: no XR code holds GetDevicesMutex()/population mutex around UpdateInput, and OpenXRInputState's mutex is a leaf.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** ControllerInterface.cpp: XR only adds '#ifdef ENABLE_VR #include OpenXR/OpenXR.h' and 'm_input_backends.emplace_back(ciface::OpenXR::CreateInputBackend(this))' in Initialize() — disjoint from the UpdateInput hunk. Behavioral overlap: XR-added UpdateInput callers HotkeyDispatcher.cpp:91 (Android, nice 10, 11 ms) and OpenXRUtilitySession.cpp:322; XR OpenXRDevice::UpdateInput snapshot copy under OpenXRInputState::s_state_mutex.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick 20718de37e (clean). Then device-test before accepting: Quest 3 standalone (Vulkan, HotkeyDispatcher active) with a Wii title (BT path, IR pointer) and a GC title (SI path, e.g., Metroid Prime) comparing CPU-thread frame times / XR pacing logs and pointer smoothness with vs without the commit; PC SteamVR D3D11+Vulkan with the Qt HotkeyScheduler (upstream 5 ms FreeLook+Host polling, unchanged). If Quest shows CPU-thread stalls, defer/skip this commit (keep upstream's previous try_lock behavior) rather than adapting XR code. Upside if fine: OpenXR controller snapshots are never skipped on SI/BT polls.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** 95dec13203 (G139) touched the same file earlier but is not needed (alone clean).
- **Commits:**
  - `20718de37e` Unconditionally lock in ControllerInterface::UpdateInput. — **MEDIUM**: Lock-semantics change (try_lock -> blocking) in XR-modified ControllerInterface.cpp affecting CPU-thread input polls that contend with XR-added low-priority pollers on Quest.

### G172 — PR #14448 — AudioCommon: Add individual Wiimote audio mixer

- **Subsystem:** AudioCommon Mixer/Cubeb, WiimoteEmu speaker, MainSettings, savestate, Qt AudioPane

- **Upstream intent:** Replaces the single Wiimote-speaker MixerFifo with 4 per-Wiimote FIFOs (Speaker learns its Wiimote index through SetWiimoteIndex in the Wiimote ctor). Each Wiimote's speaker audio can optionally be routed to its own cubeb output device, using new Main/Core keys WiimoteAudioRoutingEnabled, Wiimote{1-4}AudioOutputEnabled and Wiimote{1-4}AudioOutputDevice, plus CubebUtils::ListOutputDevices/GetOutputDeviceById, extra cubeb streams in CubebStream and an AudioPane UI group. Mixer::DoState now serializes 4 FIFOs, and STATE_VERSION goes 189 to 190.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** It touches 3 XR-modified files in caution areas, but in disjoint hunks. WiimoteEmu.cpp (WiimoteEmu plumbing) gets one SetWiimoteIndex line in the ctor. MainSettings.cpp/.h get additive keys after MAIN_TRIFORCE_IP_REDIRECTIONS. In State.cpp the greedy_xr conflict is only the STATE_VERSION line. Beyond the version line, however, it changes the savestate format (Mixer::DoState writes 4 wiimote FIFOs) and the public Mixer API (SetWiimoteSpeakerVolume and PushWiimoteSpeakerSamples gain a wiimote_index). It also has real audio prerequisites. Patch simulation on XR HEAD copies shows Mixer.cpp/.h apply only after f5dfb7e3d3 (G066) and e7e6c0990d (G083). The code also uses MixerFifo::PushSample and the 2-arg MixerFifo ctor introduced by G083. Default behavior is unchanged: with routing off, all 4 FIFOs are mixed into the main output. No XR code calls the changed Mixer APIs, and XR never modified AudioCommon, Speaker or AudioPane. On Android the default backend is OpenSL ES and the new cubeb streams require IsWii() and routing enabled. So this is MEDIUM rather than HIGH.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/Core/State.cpp: only the STATE_VERSION line conflicts (XR 181 'VR pane-remap GS constant' vs upstream 190 'PR 14448'). XR's other State.cpp edits (SaveOptions, compression_type, WaitForPendingSaves) are untouched. Source/Core/Core/Config/MainSettings.cpp/.h: XR's CPUThread default and AutoUpdate UpdateTrack edits are far from the additive block, which auto-merges. Source/Core/Core/HW/WiimoteEmu/WiimoteEmu.cpp: XR's edits are the SYSCONFSettings include, a blank line in BuildDesiredWiimoteState and the ir_read_size refactor in SendDataReport; upstream adds one line in Wiimote::Wiimote(). They are disjoint and do not interact with XR's IR-pointer raycast (Dynamics.cpp/Nunchuk.cpp). AudioPane uses ConfigStringChoice(vector<pair<QString,QString>>, Info<string>); XR HEAD's ConfigChoice.h has that overload.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate after f5dfb7e3d3 (G066), e7e6c0990d (G083) and f8fead401c (G115), in landing order. Resolve the State.cpp conflict as XR current+1 and keep XR's other State.cpp code. No other manual merge is expected. Build Windows (VS2022/Qt 6.5.1, cubeb ON) and Quest. Test a Wii game that uses the Wiimote speaker with routing off (audio identical to before) and on (per-device output via cubeb backend). Save and load a state; old states are rejected by the version bump, which is expected.
- **Upstream dependencies:** f5dfb7e3d3 (G066), e7e6c0990d (G083)
- **Notes:** f8fead401c (G115, Mixer WAV dump fix) is not textually required but lands between G083 and G172, so apply it in order. There are no later upstream follow-up fixes to this feature in range (only e222665eb0, mingw include casing). New config keys only, with no rename of existing keys.
- **Commits:**

It touches XR-modified WiimoteEmu.cpp, MainSettings and State.cpp in disjoint hunks (conflict is STATE_VERSION-only) and needs the G066/G083 mixer rework.

### G178 — PR #14659 — Fix aspect ratio cropping option

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon/Present (presenter rects / aspect ratio)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-05-25 (merge `25a6205ffb`), 2 commit(s)

- **Upstream intent:** Revert G176's Presenter logic, which broke Crop to Aspect Ratio, back to the original AdjustRectanglesToFitBounds, UpdateDrawRectangle rounding and CalculateOutputDimensions. Then reimplement Custom Crop: crop the XFB source rect (AdjustForCustomCrop) and scale the VI source aspect ratio (AdjustAspectRatioForCustomCrop in CalculateDrawAspectRatio). The cropped rect is used in UpdateDrawRectangle snapping, Present() render_source_rc, the ShowImage (no utility drawing) path and SetSuggestedWindowSize.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Behavioral change inside Presenter::Present(), right beside XR's #ifdef ENABLE_VR blocks. render_source_rc becomes AdjustForCustomCrop(m_xfb_rect) and is passed to RenderXFBToScreen. In XR's StereoMode::OpenXR branch of RenderXFBToScreen, that rect drives the desktop mirror and, in the legacy flow (GFX_VR_USE_XR_PACING_THREAD=false), BlitCurrentSourceToOpenXREyes, so eye images would be cropped and stretched over the full eye FOV. Three paths keep the uncropped m_xfb_rect: the default pacing-thread submit (SubmitOpenXRFrameFromCurrentSource), the Quest direct-to-HMD branch (replay_source_rc) and flat mode (BlitCurrentSourceToOpenXRFlat/SetFlatScreenAspect). VR behavior is therefore inconsistent when the default-off option is enabled. The default path is unchanged because GetCustomCrop returns 0 when bCropCustom is false. Hard dependency on G176.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Present.cpp, Present(): the modified render_target_rc/render_source_rc lines sit directly above XR's `else if (openxr_direct_to_hmd && vr_frame_started ...)` block. They feed RenderXFBToScreen's XR OpenXR branch (mirror view plus BlitCurrentSourceToOpenXREyes when the pacing thread is inactive). CalculateDrawAspectRatio now honors custom crop. Its only non-Present caller is Android jni MainAndroid.cpp:686 (upstream GetGameAspectRatio); no XR VR code calls it. AdjustRectanglesToFitBounds is restored to the original, so XR's direct-to-HMD call behaves exactly as at XR HEAD. Present.h: new public declarations; XR's additions are in the private #ifdef ENABLE_VR section, so they are disjoint.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Apply immediately after G176: ba15ddd602, then afd343ec9c. Both apply cleanly in sequence (verified with a sequential merge-file of all 4 commits onto XR Present.cpp). After integration, confirm that XR's VR submit, direct-to-HMD and flat paths still use m_xfb_rect, and that with Custom Crop on and UseXRPacingThread=false the eye images are cropped (expected upstream behavior). Record this as a follow-up VR policy question: should custom crop apply to the stereo eye images (no) or to the flat panel (maybe)? XR code needs no changes to integrate. Test the desktop mirror and VR with the pacing thread on/off.
- **Upstream dependencies:** a5059cbca81c (G176), 17b4b8fc07f9 (G176)
- **Handle together with:** G176 (Add screen crop feature)
- **Notes:** ba15ddd602 reverts only the Presenter-logic part of a5059cbca8+17b4b8fc07, and the two net to zero for AdjustRectanglesToFitBounds/UpdateDrawRectangle/CalculateOutputDimensions, so squashing G176+G178 is safe. afd343ec9c is clean even on its own (greedy clean) but will not compile without a5059cbca8's bCropCustom/iCropCustom* fields. The ba15ddd602 greedy conflict is only a cascade from skipping a5059cbca8/17b4b8fc07.
- **Commits:**

### G193 — PR #14668 — IOS/FS: Rewrite NAND savestating

- **Subsystem:** IOS/FS (Wii NAND) + savestate format
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-06-21 (merge `79322e77b0`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Core/IOS/FS/FileSystem.h`, `Source/Core/Core/IOS/FS/FileSystemCommon.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/IOS/FS/HostBackend/FS.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/IOS/FS/HostBackend/FS.h`, `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Security and accuracy rewrite. The Wii NAND subtree (/tmp, or / when TASing) was saved by touching host files directly (HostFileSystem::DoStateRead/DoStateWriteOrMeasure via BuildFilename/File::*), which allowed path traversal through a malicious savestate. It now goes through the guest FS API: new protected FileSystem::DoStateRead/DoStateWriteOrMeasure in FileSystemCommon.cpp (Delete/CreateDirectory/CreateFile/OpenFile/Read/Write/GetMetadata/ReadDirectory). Metadata (uid/gid/attr/modes) is now saved and NAND redirects are honored. Adds fmt::formatter<ResultCode> and bumps STATE_VERSION 190→191.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This changes the savestate format for every Wii game, including VR-profiled Wii titles (RMGE01, SOUE01, RMCE01, R3ME01...), beyond a plain STATE_VERSION bump, so rule 3 rules out LOW. Upstream shipped known regressions that later commits fix, so the PR must not be taken alone. G303 (8a90cbf638) fixes loading a state while guest files are open: without it, DoStateRead's Delete fails, SetVerifyMode is called, and the load fails and is undone. G344 (4fcd61620f, 093d3abe95) fixes loading while TASing ('/' root and the /wfs UID). G274's 682490c220 fixes a maybe-uninitialized warning in the new code and is G344's textual base. Textual prerequisite: 95dec13203 (G139) changed the signatures of the two deleted HostFileSystem functions to const std::string&. On XR HEAD alone the commit conflicts in FS.cpp/FS.h/State.cpp; after G139 only State.cpp conflicts (merge-tree verified). The State.cpp conflict is only the STATE_VERSION line. The required APIs (PointerWrap::DoEachElement/SetVerifyMode, Metadata.fst_index, ResultCode::ShortRead, std::expected Result) all exist at XR HEAD.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/Core/State.cpp: the only upstream hunk is STATE_VERSION 190→191, which conflicts with XR's `constexpr u32 STATE_VERSION = 181;  // Last changed for VR pane-remap GS constant`. No XR savestate logic (SaveOptions, WaitForPendingSaves, VR GS-constant state) is touched. XR never modified IOS/FS.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate as one unit, in order. (1) d35fe1b78b. If G139 (95dec13203) is not yet integrated, resolve the FS.cpp/FS.h conflicts by taking upstream's deletion of HostFileSystem::DoStateRead/DoStateWriteOrMeasure and their declarations; the only difference is the by-value vs const-ref parameter. Resolve State.cpp as XR current STATE_VERSION + 1. (2) 682490c220 (G274). (3) 8a90cbf638 (G303) with its FileSystemTest additions. (4) 4fcd61620f, then 093d3abe95 (G344). Skip 6376e0c1f1 in the G349 release merge, which duplicates 8a90cbf638. Test: save/load states in a Wii title with a VR profile, on PC and Quest, including XR's SaveOptions wait_for_completion flow; load a state while the game has NAND files open; run UnitTests Core/IOS/FS.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 95dec13203 (G139)
- **Handle together with:** G274 (Resolve various warnings), G303 (IOS/FS: Fix loading savestate when files are open), G344 (IOS/FS: Fix loading savestate while TASing)
- **Notes:** From G274 only 682490c220 ('FileSystemCommon: Resolve maybe-uninitialized warning') belongs to this chain. The release merge G349 carries a duplicate of the G303 fix (6376e0c1f1); pick only one of them. Existing Wii savestates are invalidated by the version bump, as with any bump.
- **Commits:**
  - `d35fe1b78b` IOS/FS: Rewrite NAND savestating — **MEDIUM**: Rewrites the NAND savestate serialization, a format change beyond the version line. It regresses until the G303/G344 follow-ups land, and its FS.cpp/FS.h context needs G139 (trivially resolvable deletions). The State.cpp conflict is only STATE_VERSION.

Alternatively, skip the intermediate steps and set the final 2609/0 values by hand when f84df02055 (G349) is reached. If XR keeps its own versioning, skip it, and then also skip 596d71fbba and f84df02055 (G349), whose hunks need this "2606" line as context or removed text.
