### G243 — PR #14665 — Core: Adjust emulated memory size automatically.

- **Subsystem:** Core memory sizing (DiscIO bi2 header, SConfig running-game metadata, Memmap, IOS RAM override, FifoPlayer, analytics) + Qt/Android setting descriptions
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-12 (merge `fc9fd6952a`), 6 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/DiscIO/Volume.h`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/VolumeDisc.cpp`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/VolumeDisc.h`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/VolumeWad.h`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.h`, `Source/Core/Core/DolphinAnalytics.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/FifoPlayer/FifoDataFile.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/Memmap.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/Memmap.h`, `Source/Core/Core/IOS/IOS.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/System.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/System.h`, `Source/UnitTests/Core/PowerPC/PageTableHostMappingTest.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Settings/AdvancedPane.cpp`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.h`
- **Upstream intent:** Reads the simulated memory size, a u32 at bi2.bin+0x04 (Volume::GetSimulatedMemorySize; VolumeDisc reads it from the game partition, VolumeWAD returns 0). Stores it in the SConfig running-game metadata via a new parameter on the private SetRunningGameMetadata, and includes it in `was_changed`. System::Initialize latches the value. When the user's RAM override is off, MemoryManager::Init now sizes MEM1 as clamp(simulated, 24 MiB, 64 MiB), with new MEM1_SIZE_MAX/MEM2_SIZE_MAX constants. FifoDataFile requires the RAM-override loader version when simulated > 24 MiB. The RAMOverrideForIOSMemoryValues early return also changes. Analytics gains `simulated-memory-size`, and the Qt tooltip and Android description are reworded. Retail games (0 or 24 MiB) see no change.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Not LOW, for four reasons. (1) ebcbb79f70 edits the XR-modified ConfigManager.cpp/.h and widens the `was_changed` predicate in SConfig::SetRunningGameMetadata. That is the function where XR calls ApplyGameVRConfigOverrides(game_id, revision) after adding the game INI layers. The hunks are disjoint and clean, but this is a behavior change in the per-game config and VR-profile load path. In practice it only fires if the IDs match but the simulated size differs, which isn't expected. (2) 0cab1731d6 changes MEM1 sizing on every boot and ships a known regression. Its IOS early return `!override \|\| simulated <= 24MiB` skips the IOS RAM-override setup when the user has the override on. Wii games then hit an invalid read; upstream's repro is RMCE01, which has an XR VR profile. 2039b5524e (G341) fixes this and must ship with it. (3) 0cab1731d6 alone conflicts in System.h's include block. XR HEAD still has `#include "VideoCommon/VideoEvents.h"` because G039 and G038 are not in. The conflict is trivial. (4) f9ef6c2151 edits the XR-modified Android res/values/strings.xml (rule 6), text only. greedy_xr is clean for all six commits.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** ConfigManager.h: upstream adds GetSimulatedMemorySize() (~line 68), the private SetRunningGameMetadata parameter and `u32 m_simulated_memory_size` (~119–135). XR added ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides() at ~92. Disjoint. ConfigManager.cpp: upstream's hunks sit at base ~154–226 (getter, callers, signature, was_changed, member assignment). XR's edits in SetRunningGameMetadata are at its end: ApplyGameVRConfigOverrides(...) after GenerateLocalGameConfigLoader, plus the new ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides(). The text is disjoint, but XR's VR per-game layer is applied only when `was_changed` is true, and upstream widens that condition. No XR code calls the private 5-argument overload; all callers are upstream's and the commit updates them. strings.xml: upstream's hunk is at ~403; XR's hunks are at 346/488/668/785/821/823. Disjoint. The resource name is unchanged, and the XR-modified SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt:1291 references R.string.enable_memory_size_override_description by that name, so there is no code impact. XR's CullingCodeFinder uses memory.GetRamSizeReal() as the FindFunctions range, so it follows the new size automatically. The memory-size check in State.cpp (XR-modified file, unchanged lines) still works. Memmap, IOS.cpp, System.h/.cpp, FifoDataFile, AdvancedPane, DolphinAnalytics and DiscIO are not XR-modified, and every include these commits rely on exists at XR HEAD.
- **Suggested integration approach:** 1. Integrate as one unit, in order: 24ad562871, ebcbb79f70, 03b1f2148b, 0cab1731d6, 594e5d2b56, f9ef6c2151. 2. Immediately add 2039b5524e (G341). Never ship 0cab1731d6 without it. 3. If 2aba231915 (G039) and f9c3f06f0a (G038) are not in yet, resolve System.h by keeping XR's `#include "VideoCommon/VideoEvents.h"` and adding `#include "Common/CommonTypes.h"`. Merge-tree shows this is the only conflict; every other hunk applies cleanly. 4. Verify the XR VR profile still applies exactly once per boot (VR_CONFIG log / ApplyGameVRConfigOverrides) for a GameCube disc (GM8E01), a Wii disc (RMCE01) and a WAD. 5. After G341, boot RMCE01 with Advanced > Emulated Memory Size Override on (64/128 MiB) and confirm there is no invalid read at 0x8019FE6C. 6. Save and load a savestate on a VR title, and build Quest (strings.xml). 7. If XR builds unit tests, note that PageTableHostMappingTest now calls SConfig::Init/Shutdown.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 2aba231915 (G039)
- **Handle together with:** G341 (Core: Fix RAM override setup in IOS.)
- **Notes:** The G039 dependency is textual only (System.h include context) and transitively involves f9c3f06f0a (G038). G341 was classified BLOCKED on this group in another batch. MEM1 only grows for discs whose bi2 asks for more than 24 MiB (debug builds such as the MKDD debug disc, memory-extending mods); the clamp never goes below 24 MiB. There is no STATE_VERSION bump; savestates for such games just carry a larger MEM1, which State.cpp already validates. FIFO logs from those games need the RAM-override loader version.
- **Commits:**
  - `24ad562871` Core: Add `Volume::GetSimulatedMemorySize()`. — **MEDIUM**: Adds the pure-virtual Volume::GetSimulatedMemorySize with VolumeDisc/VolumeWAD implementations in DiscIO (not XR-modified; no XR Volume subclasses). It is a prerequisite of the rest of the PR, so it is integrated with the unit.
  - `ebcbb79f70` Core: Add `SConfig::GetSimulatedMemorySize()`. — **MEDIUM**: Edits the XR-modified ConfigManager.cpp/.h: new getter, private-overload parameter, and a widened `was_changed` predicate in the SetRunningGameMetadata function that applies XR's VR game overrides. The hunks are disjoint and clean.
  - `03b1f2148b` DolphinAnalytics: Add game quirk for game simulated memory size. — **MEDIUM**: One analytics field in the non-XR file DolphinAnalytics.cpp; the AddData u32 overload exists at XR HEAD. It needs the SConfig getter from ebcbb79f70.
  - `0cab1731d6` Core: Adjust emulated memory size automatically. — **MEDIUM**: Changes MEM1 sizing at every boot and contains the IOS RAM-override regression that G341 fixes. Alone, it has a trivial System.h include conflict against XR HEAD (needs G039 context).
  - `594e5d2b56` DolphinQt: Update tool tip for Enable Emulated Memory Size Override. — **MEDIUM**: Tooltip text in AdvancedPane.cpp (not XR-modified). It describes the automatic sizing behavior, so it ships with 0cab1731d6.
  - `f9ef6c2151` Android: Update description for Enable Emulated Memory Size Override. — **MEDIUM**: Text-only change to the XR-modified Android strings.xml (rule 6) in a hunk disjoint from XR's strings. The resource name is unchanged, and it is referenced by the XR-modified SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt. It describes the new behavior.

### G248 — PR #14643 — Windows: Fix command line output not displaying when using Git Bash

- **Subsystem:** DolphinQt startup (Windows console attach in Main.cpp)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-12 (merge `cdaf67c6ce`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Main.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Main.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** At the top of main() on _WIN32, call AttachConsole(ATTACH_PARENT_PROCESS) and freopen CONOUT$ for stdout/stderr only when the process has no valid stdout and stderr handles. This keeps existing handles, so --help/--version output reaches Git Bash and redirected output is preserved, while cmd and PowerShell keep working as before.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Applies cleanly alone and in greedy_xr and is functionally unrelated to VR. However, it changes runtime behavior in a file XR modified (DolphinQt/Main.cpp), so rule 2, which requires behavior-neutral changes in XR-modified files, is not met. That makes it MEDIUM by the rubric; in practice it is a near-trivial review.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** XR's only Main.cpp edit (~line 286) gates the updater: if (AutoUpdateChecker::SystemSupportsAutoUpdates() && !Settings::Instance().IsBatchModeEnabled()). G248's hunk is the #ifdef _WIN32 console block at the start of main() (~line 122), so the hunks don't overlap and don't interact. XR has no other AttachConsole or CONOUT$ use in Source/Core.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick 1a62cdca0e as-is and confirm XR's updater guard line is untouched. Build with Build-vs2022 and test: 'Dolphin.exe --version' / '--help' from cmd, PowerShell and Git Bash; a normal double-click GUI launch; and a VR game launch to confirm startup is unaffected. e222665eb0 (G237, lowercased windows includes in the same file) is not needed.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** MEDIUM purely because of rubric rule 2. If XR policy treats non-overlapping, non-VR bug fixes in XR-touched files as auto-pickable, this one qualifies. Side benefit: OpenXR loader stderr diagnostics (XR_LOADER_DEBUG) become visible when Dolphin is launched from Git Bash.
- **Commits:**
  - `1a62cdca0e` Windows: Fix command line output not displaying when using Git Bash — **MEDIUM**: Windows console-attach logic change in XR-modified DolphinQt/Main.cpp. The hunk doesn't overlap XR's updater-guard edit and the commit applies cleanly, but it changes behavior, so rule 2 rules out LOW.

### G269 — PR #14436 — Loading custom textures using combination of elf/dol game ids and iso game ids.

- **Subsystem:** Core SConfig game metadata + custom texture / dynamic input texture directory lookup
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-22 (merge `b008142f72`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.h`, `Source/Core/InputCommon/DynamicInputTextureManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/HiresTextures.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/HiresTextures.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/ConfigManager.h`
- **Upstream intent:** When a title is booted from an ELF/DOL, remember that executable's generated ID (m_game_id_elf_dol). Custom-texture and dynamic-input-texture loading then tries '<elfid>-<gameid>' before '<gameid>' (GetGameIDsForTextures and GetTextureDirectoriesForFirstMatchingGameId). Generated dynamic-input textures are written under the preferred ID, and the OSD message changes to 'Preloading/Found N custom textures for <ids>'.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** The change is functionally isolated from VR. It does not change GetGameID() or m_game_id, so XR's per-game VR profile loading (ApplyGameVRConfigOverrides, keyed by m_game_id inside SetRunningGameMetadata) and XR's GetGameID() users (TextureCacheBase Metroid gates, ShaderCache, VertexManagerBase, CullingCodeFinder, VRConfigWidget) are unaffected. HiresTextures.cpp/.h and DynamicInputTextureManager.cpp are not XR-modified. It is MEDIUM because the merge-tree simulation shows 4 conflict regions in XR's heavily modified ConfigManager.cpp: (1, 2) the include block, where XR removed <Core/Core.h> and added <fstream>/<map>/<set>; (3) ResetRunningGameMetadata, whose context is G243's 6-argument SetRunningGameMetadata; (4) SetElfDolID added at the same spot where XR added ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides(). All are trivial keep-both resolutions. It also introduces a regression that G299 fixes (the OSD count is always 0 when prefetch is off).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** ConfigManager.cpp: XR's include changes, the anonymous-namespace VR per-game INI loader, the ApplyGameVRConfigOverrides call at the end of SetRunningGameMetadata, and the new SConfig::ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides() between SetRunningGameMetadata and OnESTitleChanged, which is the same insertion point as upstream's SetElfDolID. ConfigManager.h: XR added ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides elsewhere; it auto-merges.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick with manual resolution in ConfigManager.cpp. (a) Keep XR's include set and add a single <fmt/format.h>. (b) In ResetRunningGameMetadata, use the 6-argument call only if G243 ebcbb79f70 is integrated (otherwise keep XR's 5-argument call), then add SetElfDolID(""). (c) Keep both ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides() and SetElfDolID(). Take G299 (f5be1c30e5) right after. Verify that a per-game VR profile (e.g. GM8E01 from GameSettingsVR) still applies on disc boot, that ELF/DOL boot works, and that custom textures load. HiresTextures.h now includes <fmt/ranges.h> for fmt::join, which XR's fmt 12.0.0 provides.
- **Upstream dependencies:** ebcbb79f70 (G243)
- **Handle together with:** G299 (VideoCommon: Fix custom texture count in OSD message)
- **Notes:** The dependency on ebcbb79f70 is only textual/signature (the trailing simulated-memory-size argument in ResetRunningGameMetadata). Drop that argument if G243 is skipped.
- **Commits:**
  - `83dc4685aa` waterfall loading of textures via id to prefer elfid-gameid when launched via elf; fallback to gameid if no match — **MEDIUM**: Small, obvious conflicts in XR-modified ConfigManager.cpp (include block; insertion next to XR's ReloadGameVRConfigOverrides; G243 context in ResetRunningGameMetadata). It does not change the game-ID semantics XR's VR config overrides rely on.

### G275 — PR #14752 — AboutDialog: Report the SDL version being used

- **Subsystem:** DolphinQt AboutDialog + DolphinQt CMake link dependencies
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-29 (merge `bc6688c0aa`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/AboutDialog.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Show 'Using SDL x.y.z' in the About dialog, using SDL_GetVersion and SDL_VERSIONNUM_* from <SDL3/SDL_version.h>. To get the include path and symbols, SDL3::SDL3 is added unconditionally to dolphin-emu's PRIVATE target_link_libraries.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Touches XR-modified Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt. The hunk doesn't overlap XR's edits and applies cleanly alone and in greedy_xr, but it is a build-dependency change rather than a behavior-neutral one, so rules 2 and 5 rule out LOW. The unconditional include and link also break ENABLE_SDL=OFF builds. Upstream fixed that in d5e3c2bcfc (G289), which touches the XR-modified top-level CMakeLists.txt (global add_definitions(-DHAVE_SDL3)) and InputCommon/CMakeLists.txt (drops the PUBLIC HAVE_SDL3=1 on inputcommon), so the two should be integrated as a pair. XR's real build configs are fine with G275 alone. Windows Build-vs2022 has ENABLE_SDL=ON with vendored Externals/SDL, on the same submodule commit (a962f40bbb) as MB, so the SDL3::SDL3 target and the SDL3 version API exist. Quest/Android doesn't build DolphinQt, and ENABLE_SDL defaults OFF on Android.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt: XR adds VR sources to add_executable (VRPane, VRConfigWidget, Shader/Elements/TextureElement override and HideObject editors, ShaderHunter/CullingCodeFinder widgets, HotkeyVR, OpenXRWiimoteConfigSessionController) and a dolphin-emu-sys-copy custom target in the WIN32 block. Upstream only appends SDL3::SDL3 to the dolphin-emu target_link_libraries list (~line 458, after imgui/implot), so they don't overlap. AboutDialog.cpp is untouched by XR. The only HAVE_SDL3 consumer is upstream's ControllerInterface.h:35, and G289 widens the define's scope (global instead of inputcommon PUBLIC), so XR's OpenXR ControllerInterface code still sees CIFACE_USE_SDL.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick d7615aa0b0, then d5e3c2bcfc (G289) right after. G289 conflicts alone only because it needs G275 and is clean in greedy_xr. Re-run CMake for Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON), build, and check that About shows the Qt 6.5.1 and SDL versions; confirm SDL controllers still enumerate. Not needed for Quest (DolphinQt isn't built). If G289 is deferred, G275 alone still builds every XR configuration, but desktop builds with ENABLE_SDL=OFF break.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G289 (AboutDialog: Fix compilation with ENABLE_SDL=OFF)
- **Notes:** Later upstream G277 (SDL bump to 3.4.12) is independent of this group. No toolchain change: SDL is already a static dependency of inputcommon on Windows, so linking it into dolphin-emu adds nothing new.
- **Commits:**
  - `d7615aa0b0` AboutDialog: Report the SDL version being used — **MEDIUM**: Adds an SDL3 include/link to dolphin-emu via XR-modified DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt (no overlapping hunks, clean). It is a build-dependency change and needs follow-up d5e3c2bcfc (G289) for ENABLE_SDL=OFF builds.

### G276 — PR #14757 — CMake: Improve our version checking

- **Subsystem:** Build (top-level CMake + CMake/DolphinLibraryTools.cmake) / toolchain gating
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-29 (merge `3c4659ce34`), 3 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `CMakeLists.txt`, `CMake/DolphinLibraryTools.cmake`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** f98c974a0d simplifies the compiler minimum-version check and its message, keeping the thresholds GCC 12 / Clang 15 / AppleClang 14.0.3 / MSVC 19.32. 13238340ae moves CMAKE_MODULE_PATH and the support includes to right after project(). It also adds dolphin_check_toolset_version() with hard FATAL_ERROR gates: MSVC toolset >= 143 (VS2022) and Xcode >= 14.3. 24361f49d1 adds dolphin_check_std_version(), which uses configure-time try_compile to gate libstdc++ >= 12 and libc++ >= 150000.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is a toolchain-enforcement change to XR-modified CMakeLists.txt. It puts new configure-time FATAL_ERROR gates in front of both XR builds, so it is not LOW (rule 5). Both builds pass the gates today: XR's Build-vs2022 uses MSVC 19.44.35215 (toolset 143), and NDK r29 has clang 21 with _LIBCPP_VERSION 210000. 13238340ae conflicts in greedy_xr only because its hunk context comes from three G273 commits. Hunk 1 needs 808a82d5dd (removes the CMAKE_VERSION<3.25 LINUX block after project()) and c7b621ad08 (adds the VS_STARTUP_PROJECT line). Hunk 3 needs 3d9f46cd64 (removes the LINUX_LOCAL_DEV option block just above the old includes). Greedy skipped 3d9f46cd64 because of its own DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt conflict. 24361f49d1 then conflicts in DolphinLibraryTools.cmake only because 13238340ae was skipped. I verified this with 3-way simulations. The whole PR applied to the merge's first parent plus XR's CMakeLists edits is clean. With 3d9f46cd64's hunk reverted, 13238340ae conflicts exactly at the LINUX_LOCAL_DEV block.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** CMakeLists.txt: XR's relative `set(CMAKE_USER_MAKE_RULES_OVERRIDE "CMake/FlagsOverride.cmake")` (line 16) is about six lines above hunk 1's context, so there is no overlap. Nothing touches XR's option(ENABLE_VR), the Qt6.5.1 CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH block, the ENABLE_VR/OpenXR loader block or CPACK_PACKAGE_CONTACT. The moved includes (DolphinLibraryTools, DolphinDisableWarningsMSVC, GNUInstallDirs, CheckSymbolExists…) now come earlier, but XR's blocks only use them later, so they still work. XR did not modify CMake/DolphinLibraryTools.cmake. The new checks add a few try_compile probes to both the VS2022 and the Quest (VS-bundled CMake 3.31 + Ninja via Gradle) configure steps.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Best: integrate after G273 (808a82d5dd, c7b621ad08, 3d9f46cd64); then all three commits apply cleanly. If G273 is skipped or only partly applied: apply f98c974a0d (clean). Then resolve 13238340ae by hand: insert `list(APPEND CMAKE_MODULE_PATH ...)` plus the include block right after project(dolphin-emu), and delete the old copy below the LINUX_LOCAL_DEV option block. Then apply 24361f49d1, which is clean once 13238340ae is in. Keep MSVC_toolset_min_version=143 and MSVC_min_version=19.32. XR builds with VS2022 only, so never raise these to 145/19.5x (VS2026). To validate, reconfigure Build-vs2022 with 'Visual Studio 17 2022' and ENABLE_VR=ON: expect 'Using MSVC Toolset 143' and no FATAL_ERROR. Then run Source/Android/build-quest.ps1: the configure step must pass the LLVM_libc++ check.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 808a82d5dd (G273), c7b621ad08 (G273), 3d9f46cd64 (G273)
- **Handle together with:** G273 (CMake: Replace the Visual Studio project)
- **Notes:** The upstream target still has toolset 143 / MSVC 19.32 / libc++ 150000. G314 (ed25d5649b) enforces VS2026 separately, via an _MSC_FULL_VER #error in Source/PCH/pch.h, not through these CMake thresholds. f98c974a0d on its own is message-only, but commits 2 and 3 build on its text, so the PR is treated as one unit. XR desktop-Linux builds would now also require libstdc++ >= 12.
- **Commits:**
  - `f98c974a0d` CMake: Update compiler version checking — **MEDIUM**: Keeps the same thresholds and only reshapes the message and logic, so it is harmless alone and applies cleanly. Rated MEDIUM because it is the base of the toolchain-gating PR that 13238340ae builds on.
  - `13238340ae` CMake: Enforce minimum toolset versions — **MEDIUM**: Adds a hard MSVC toolset >= 143 gate (VS2022 passes) and moves the includes. Greedy conflict comes only from context in the G273 commits; hand resolution is trivial.
  - `24361f49d1` CMake: Enforce minimum stdlib versions — **MEDIUM**: Adds configure-time libstdc++/libc++ gates. On Quest the check runs and passes (NDK r29 libc++ 210000). It needs dolphin_check_toolset_version and the reorganized block from 13238340ae.

### G283 — PR #14770 — AchievementApprovedHash: Automatically generate hash using CMake

- **Subsystem:** Build system (CMake build-time code generation) / RetroAchievements approved-INI list hash
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-29 (merge `c863abc0d3`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `CMake/GenerateAchievementHash.cmake`, `Source/Core/Common/Crypto/SHA1.cpp`, `Source/Core/Common/Crypto/SHA1.h`, `Source/Core/Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h`, `Source/Core/Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h.in`, `Source/Core/Core/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/UnitTests/Core/PatchAllowlistTest.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Stop hand-maintaining ACHIEVEMENT_APPROVED_LIST_HASH. The checked-in Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h is deleted and generated at build time from Data/Sys/ApprovedInis.json via add_custom_command running CMake/GenerateAchievementHash.cmake (file(SHA1) + configure_file of the new AchievementApprovedHash.h.in). A new constexpr Common::SHA1::StringToDigest parses the hex into a constinit Digest. DigestToSource and the copy-paste hint in PatchAllowlistTest are removed.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** It touches no XR-modified file and no VR behavior, but rubric rule 5 applies: it changes what both builds do. The Windows build (Build-vs2022, VS2022 CMake generator) and the Quest build (Gradle -> top-level CMakeLists, CMake 3.22.1+) gain a new build-time generation step. The output lands in ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR} and is found through include_directories("${PROJECT_BINARY_DIR}/Source/Core"), which is still present at XR HEAD line 853. Checks done: VS2022 MSVC 14.44 compiled and linked the new constexpr StringToDigest plus constinit header under Dolphin's /std:c++latest /W4 /WX /Zc:* flag set, and the digest equals the old hard-coded one. The raw-file SHA1 of ApprovedInis.json (e6cdd785...fccc at the parent) equals the runtime picojson serialize() hash, because the file is stored canonically (one line, 0 newlines); XR's autocrlf=true checkout hashes identically. Hard prerequisite: a108fb849f (G096), since XR HEAD still has APPROVED_LIST_HASH inline in AchievementManager.h. A clean cherry-pick also needs the hash-bump commits G151/G158/G209/G239; without them there is a trivial modify/delete conflict on the header and a conflict in PatchAllowlistTest.cpp.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** none. XR never modified Source/Core/Core/CMakeLists.txt, CMake/, Common/Crypto/SHA1.*, AchievementManager.*, Data/Sys/ApprovedInis.json or Source/UnitTests. The only indirect effect is the new generation step in the Build-vs2022 and Quest native CMake builds.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate after G096 (a108fb849f) and the ApprovedInis hash-bump commits c96d1b7b4a (G151), 3944310308 (G158), 915b839598 (G209) and a5f6ba81fa (G239); that order simulated clean. If any were skipped, resolve the modify/delete by DELETING Source/Core/Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h, and take upstream's PatchAllowlistTest.cpp. A leftover source-tree copy would shadow the generated header, because include_directories(Source/Core) (line 610) comes before the binary-dir include. Then reconfigure Build-vs2022 and run build-quest.ps1 (assembleQuestDebug) to confirm the 'Generating AchievementApprovedHash.h' step runs on both. Optionally run tests --gtest_filter=PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes. Land this before G325/G332/G353: those later commits edit ApprovedInis.json without updating any hash header, so without G283 XR's hard-coded hash goes stale and RetroAchievements rejects the approved list at runtime.
- **Upstream dependencies:** a108fb849f (G096), c96d1b7b4a (G151), 3944310308 (G158), 915b839598 (G209), a5f6ba81fa (G239)
- **Handle together with:** G096 (AchievementManager: APPROVED_LIST_HASH quality of life impro)
- **Notes:** Merge commit c863abc0d3, with no later upstream follow-up fixes. Of the dependencies, only G096 is semantic; G151/G158/G209/G239 are needed only to avoid the trivial header/test conflict. Upstream had already removed the MSBuild files (DolphinLib.props is absent at 55c015e6ac). XR still has DolphinLib.props on disk but builds only via CMake; G096's props hunk would then reference a header that G283 deletes, which is harmless for XR. The custom-command arguments are unquoted and the script uses unquoted file(SHA1 ${JSON_FILE}), which is fine for XR's space-free source and build paths. ApprovedInis.json must stay canonical (no pretty-printing, no trailing newline): if a hand-resolved conflict reformats it, the build-time raw hash will differ from the runtime serialize() hash.
- **Commits:**
  - `55c015e6ac` AchievementApprovedHash: Automatically generate hash using CMake — **MEDIUM**: A self-contained build-system change that adds build-time generation of AchievementApprovedHash.h for both the Windows and Quest CMake builds (rubric rule 5). The C++ side was verified to compile under VS2022 with /W4 /WX. It needs G096 (a108fb849f) semantically, and the ApprovedInis hash-bump commits for a clean cherry-pick. It must land before the post-G283 ApprovedInis edits (G325/G332/G353).

### G291 — PR #14779 — Make sure CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT is set

- **Subsystem:** Build system (top-level CMakeLists.txt, macOS)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-30 (merge `e463c64b7a`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Before project(), default the cache variable CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT to `macosx`. This makes AppleClang use the SDK sysroot so that /usr/local/include is no longer searched ahead of the -isystem Externals include paths, which caused odd include clashes on macOS.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** For XR's platforms this is a functional no-op: CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT is read only by CMake's Apple/Darwin logic and is ignored by the VS2022 generator, the Android NDK toolchain and Linux. It does not apply cleanly, though. Both alone_on_xr_head and greedy_xr conflict in the XR-modified top-level CMakeLists.txt. A read-only merge-tree simulation shows the conflict comes only from adjacency. Upstream inserts its 3 lines directly above `set(CMAKE_USER_MAKE_RULES_OVERRIDE ...)`, and XR changed that exact line to the relative path "CMake/FlagsOverride.cmake" in XR commit 2d41ce7ac4 ('qt6'). Rule 1 fails because the conflict is not a STATE_VERSION line, so it is MEDIUM (small, obvious conflict).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** CMakeLists.txt line 16 at XR HEAD is `set(CMAKE_USER_MAKE_RULES_OVERRIDE "CMake/FlagsOverride.cmake")`. XR commit 2d41ce7ac4 changed it from upstream's absolute `${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/CMake/FlagsOverride.cmake`. That line is the trailing context of upstream's hunk, so the two edits conflict because they are adjacent. There is no interaction with XR's other CMake edits: the ENABLE_VR option (line 96), the Qt6.5.1 CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH block (~493-499), the OpenXR add_subdirectory block (~664-688) and CPACK_PACKAGE_CONTACT.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick dbbe5c403e and resolve the one conflict by keeping both sides. Keep XR's `set(CMAKE_USER_MAKE_RULES_OVERRIDE "CMake/FlagsOverride.cmake")` line exactly as it is. After `set(CMAKE_OSX_DEPLOYMENT_TARGET "11.0.0" CACHE STRING "")` (XR line 14), insert upstream's 2 comment lines, then `set(CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT macosx CACHE STRING "")`, then a blank line. Do not bring in upstream's `${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}` version of the FlagsOverride line with this pick. Check that a Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON) configure and the Quest assembleQuestDebug CMake configure behave the same as before.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** There is no dependency on G273: the hunk's leading context does not include the cmake_minimum_required/CMP0141 lines that 808a82d5dd (G273) changed, and no later in-range commit touches CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT. Separate observation, not part of this integration: XR's relative FlagsOverride path quietly undoes upstream acb18a58cf ('Make the path given to CMAKE_USER_MAKE_RULES_OVERRIDE absolute'). It came in through contributor commit 2d41ce7ac4 ('qt6', PR #5 qt6-10-fix), and the maintainer may want to review it on its own.
- **Commits:**
  - `dbbe5c403e` Make sure CMAKE_OSX_SYSROOT is set — **MEDIUM**: The change only matters on macOS, but it conflicts at an adjacent line with XR's edit to the FlagsOverride line in CMakeLists.txt. The conflict is trivial and is resolved by keeping both sides.

### G295 — PR #14776 — Replace functional cast with `static_cast`

- **Subsystem:** Core: savestate API (State.cpp/.h), plus NetPlay/Movie/ARDecrypt/LibusbUtils/FreeLook/WiiUtils cast cleanup
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-01 (merge `3c63788e01`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Core/ARDecrypt.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/FreeLookManager.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/LibusbUtils.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/Movie.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/NetPlayClient.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/NetPlayServer.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/State.h`, `Source/Core/Core/WiiUtils.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/State.h`
- **Upstream intent:** A cosmetic cleanup that replaces functional casts (u8(x), int(x), u32(x)) with static_cast in 9 files. In State it also changes slot types from int to u32: SlotWithTimestamp::slot, the GetEmptySlot return type, MakeStateFilename, GetInfoStringOfSlot, GetUnixTimeOfSlot, Save and Load. Behavior does not change.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is a real conflict, not the STATE_VERSION line, with XR's savestate API extension from XR commit 677aa34050 ('Added ARMode by Clodo76'). That commit added State::SaveOptions {compress, wait_for_completion}, Save(Core::System&, int slot, SaveOptions options = {}), SaveAs(..., SaveOptions) and WaitForPendingSaves(), which Culling Code Finder uses. A read-only merge-tree onto XR HEAD conflicts in two places. The first is State.h, at the Save/Load declarations. The second is State.cpp, at the Save definition, plus the GetEmptySlot signature line when f6a67aa6e7 (G120, the std::span parameter) has not been applied. greedy_xr still conflicts in both files. The rubric says any State.cpp/State.h conflict other than STATE_VERSION is not trivial, so this is MEDIUM. The other 7 files are not XR-modified, and their changes are behavior-neutral casts. The conflict itself is small and obvious.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** State.h: XR's `struct SaveOptions`, `void Save(Core::System& system, int slot, SaveOptions options = {});`, `SaveAs(..., SaveOptions options = {})` and `WaitForPendingSaves()`. Upstream rewrites the Save/Load lines. State.cpp: XR's `void Save(Core::System& system, int slot, SaveOptions options)` forwards options to SaveAs. The LoadLastSaved and SaveFirstSaved hunks auto-merge with XR's WaitForPendingSaves() and its `SaveAsFromCore(system, MakeStateFilename(*slot), {})` call. XR callers still pass int: VideoCommon/CullingCodeFinder.cpp:1018 `State::Save(system, m_scan_config.savestate_slot, {.compress=false,.wait_for_completion=true})` and :1319 State::Load; Android jni MainAndroid.cpp:489/503/516 (jint); jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp:185/190; DolphinQt MainWindow.cpp:1533-1577. All of them compile through implicit int to u32 conversion. MSVC /W4 /WX has C4245 disabled and C4365 off by default, and the clang build has no -Wsign-conversion. No JNI signature changes.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick a5552e612c and resolve by hand. In State.h use `void Save(Core::System& system, u32 slot, SaveOptions options = {});` and `void Load(Core::System& system, u32 slot);`, and keep XR's SaveOptions, SaveAs(..., SaveOptions) and WaitForPendingSaves() unchanged. In State.cpp use `void Save(Core::System& system, u32 slot, SaveOptions options) { SaveAs(system, MakeStateFilename(slot), options); }`. For GetEmptySlot, take the `std::optional<u32>` return type and keep whichever parameter type the tree already has: XR's `const std::vector<SlotWithTimestamp>&`, or `std::span<const SlotWithTimestamp>` if f6a67aa6e7 (G120) is already in. Leave STATE_VERSION alone. Then build Build-vs2022 and Quest (assembleQuestDebug). Run the XR unit test State.SaveOptionsDefaultsMatchExpectedCompressionBehavior, and smoke-test slot save/load hotkeys (Qt and Android) and a Culling Code Finder reference-savestate capture and reload.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** The State hunks have a context-only overlap with f6a67aa6e7 (G120). With G120 applied, only the Save/Load conflict remains. This is a purely cosmetic commit, and no later in-range upstream commit touches State.cpp/State.h (checked with --full-history). If the integrator would rather not churn XR's savestate API, skipping G295 has no downstream consequences, and so does applying only the 7 non-State files.
- **Commits:**
  - `a5552e612c` Replace functional cast with `static_cast` — **MEDIUM**: A cosmetic cast cleanup, but its int to u32 slot signature change in State.h/State.cpp conflicts with XR's SaveOptions additions to Save() (XR 677aa34050). This is a non-STATE_VERSION State conflict, and it needs the hand resolution described in the group.

### G296 — PR #14768 — Better validation and handling of GCZ

- **Subsystem:** DiscIO (GCZ CompressedBlob reader/writer)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-01 (merge `ad7e793548`), 4 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/DiscIO/CompressedBlob.cpp`, `Source/Core/DiscIO/CompressedBlob.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Security hardening against malicious GCZ files. It masks uncompressed_flag in GetBlockCompressedSize and rejects uncompressed blocks of the wrong size and compressed blocks larger than the zlib buffer, which fixes two heap overflows. It bounds-checks block_num. It validates the header, sizes and block pointers at load (new Initialize(), ValidateBlockPointers() and m_valid; Create() returns nullptr for invalid files). It also renames CompressedBlobHeader::data_size to disc_size and widens m_data_offset from int to u64.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** XR never modified CompressedBlob.*, and this is an isolated DiscIO fix whose content would be LOW. It is MEDIUM rather than LOW for three reasons. (1) feeb36bf07, and therefore 4782a04c20, does not apply to XR HEAD by itself: its CompressedBlob.h hunk context contains the constructor declaration `CompressedBlobReader(File::DirectIOFile file, std::string filename);`, which 95dec13203 (G139) introduced. G139 is a 140-file clang-tidy by-value/const-ref sweep that touches many XR-modified files (CoreTiming, ShaderCache, CustomShaderCache, TextureCacheBase, ControllerInterface, Qt), so it is likely MEDIUM and not auto-picked. A read-only merge-tree confirms that declaration is the only conflict. greedy_xr is clean for all four commits only because G139 applied there. (2) feeb36bf07 alone breaks the MSVC /WX build: `m_data_offset = header_size` is a size_t to int conversion (C4267 is not disabled) until 4782a04c20 lands, so the 4 commits must go in as one unit. (3) The rubric's rule 4 would otherwise make it BLOCKED, but the dependency is only textual context and can be resolved trivially without G139, so it is not BLOCKED.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** none. There are no XR-modified files. The only external user is DiscIO/Blob.cpp:233 (`CompressedBlobReader::Create`), which is unchanged. Nothing at XR HEAD references CompressedBlobHeader::data_size, which is renamed to disc_size.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Pick a3740b5f71, ffb0e2c594, feeb36bf07 and 4782a04c20 in order as one unit, and do not build in between on MSVC. If 95dec13203 (G139) is already integrated, all four apply cleanly. Otherwise, feeb36bf07 conflicts only in CompressedBlob.h's private section. Keep XR's `CompressedBlobReader(File::DirectIOFile file, const std::string& filename);`, add `bool Initialize();` and `bool ValidateBlockPointers() const;` after it, and take the rest of upstream (disc_size rename, member default initializers, m_valid). The .cpp auto-merges and keeps `m_file_name(filename)`. After that, 4782a04c20 applies cleanly. Later, when handling the release merge G349, treat its copies 2f5232fbf4, 8f1e33a5f4, 4eb90c7314 and b6d8da24b3 as already applied, since they are identical diffs. To test, load an existing .gcz on PC and on Quest, convert an ISO to GCZ (ConvertToGCZ now writes disc_size) and boot it, and confirm that a truncated GCZ is rejected cleanly.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 95dec13203 (G139)
- **Handle together with:** G349 (Merge branch 'release-prep-2609')
- **Notes:** The G139 dependency is context-only (the constructor parameter type). If the G139 classifier makes it LOW, so that it is auto-picked before this group, G296 can be downgraded to LOW, because all four commits then apply cleanly. G296 is the master copy. The release-prep-2609 merge (G349) carries identical cherry-picked duplicates of all four commits. No later in-range commit touches CompressedBlob.* except those duplicates.
- **Commits:**
  - `a3740b5f71` GCZ: Don't trust GetBlockCompressedSize — **MEDIUM**: Adds uncompressed_flag masking and bounds on read_size (fixing two heap overflows) and drops the memset. It applies cleanly alone on XR HEAD, and on its own it would be LOW (DiscIO only). It is rated MEDIUM because it ships as a unit with feeb36bf07, which needs uncompressed_flag.
  - `ffb0e2c594` GCZ: Don't trust block_num either — **MEDIUM**: Adds a `block_num >= m_header.num_blocks` guard in GetBlock. Its hunk context is a3740b5f71's read_size line. It is clean alone on XR, and part of the unit.
  - `feeb36bf07` GCZ: validate while loading — **MEDIUM**: Adds load-time validation (Initialize/ValidateBlockPointers/m_valid) and the data_size to disc_size rename. On XR without G139, the only conflict is the constructor declaration context in CompressedBlob.h. Alone it breaks the MSVC /WX build (C4267 on int m_data_offset = size_t) until 4782a04c20.
  - `4782a04c20` GCZ: use 64-bit for m_data_offset — **MEDIUM**: Changes m_data_offset from int to u64 and fixes the MSVC error that feeb36bf07 introduced. It modifies a line that feeb36bf07 added.

### G302 — PR #14802 — Enable Fallthrough warnings

- **Subsystem:** Build system (MSVC/gcc/clang warning flags) + code hygiene in Common/Hash, Common/GL/GLExtensions, IOS Bluetooth passthrough (BTReal), Triforce MagneticCardReader, Qt debugger widgets
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-08 (merge `f42c349c32`), 7 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Common/Hash.cpp`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLExtensions/GLExtensions.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/MagCard/MagneticCardReader.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Debugger/MemoryViewWidget.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/IOS/USB/Bluetooth/BTReal.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/Debugger/CodeViewWidget.cpp`, `Source/CMakeLists.txt`, `CMakeLists.txt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Make implicit switch fallthrough a diagnosed condition on every compiler. Intentional fallthroughs get [[fallthrough]] (GetMurmurHash3 tail mixing, the GL/GLES version cascade in InitExtensionList) and missing breaks are added (MagneticCardReader, MemoryViewWidget, and BTReal, which drops a spurious 'IOCtlV: Unknown request' error log). Ctrl+F in CodeView/MemoryView no longer also forwards to QWidget::keyPressEvent. Then -Wimplicit-fallthrough is enabled for gcc/clang (Source/ scope) and /w15262 (MSVC C5262 at level 1, fatal under Dolphin's /WX).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** The first six commits are behaviour-neutral or tiny fixes in files XR never modified, and all apply cleanly on XR HEAD (merge-tree verified), so they are LOW. The group is MEDIUM only because of 94eceb35e8. It edits the XR-modified root CMakeLists.txt and conflicts textually: hunk 1 strips trailing whitespace from the `set(libc++_min_version ...)` line, which only exists after 24361f49d1 (G276). It also adds a Windows toolchain policy. Combined with XR's existing `/WX` (root CMakeLists l.253), `/w15262` makes any unannotated fallthrough a hard error in every target that inherits the root options. That includes XR-only code that upstream CI never compiles: `openxr_loader` (Externals/OpenXR is added at root l.~687 after the MSVC block, without dolphin_disable_warnings; Build-vs2022 openxr_loader.vcxproj = Level4 + TreatWarningAsError) and Externals/imgui/backends/imgui_impl_vulkan.cpp, which XR compiles inside videovulkan. The actual breakage risk is low (see notes), but rules 1, 2 (not a behaviour-neutral build change) and 5 (Windows toolchain flag) fail for that commit. It is not HIGH: the change is 2 lines, the conflict is trivial, and the result was checked.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Only the root CMakeLists.txt overlaps (94eceb35e8). XR's edits there: FlagsOverride path (l.16), ENABLE_VR option (l.96), Qt6.5.1 CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH (l.493-499), the OpenXR loader block (l.664-688, add_subdirectory(Externals/OpenXR) after the MSVC flags block, not shielded by dolphin_disable_warnings), and CPACK contact (l.~907). Upstream's hunks (version-check block l.~63, MSVC 'Additional warnings' block l.~273) do not touch those lines, but the new flag also reaches openxr_loader (incl. bundled jsoncpp) and XR's VR sources. No other overlap: Hash.cpp, GLExtensions.cpp, MagneticCardReader.cpp, BTReal.cpp, CodeViewWidget.cpp, MemoryViewWidget.cpp and Source/CMakeLists.txt are untouched by XR, and no XR-added code references the debugger widgets. Indirect: GetMurmurHash3 is s_texture_hash_func (texture-cache hashes that XR texture overrides / Shader Hunter key on), but [[fallthrough]] does not change codegen, so hash values are unchanged. GLExtensions feeds XR's OGL OpenXR backend, and the annotations do not change it.
- **Suggested integration approach:** 1) Cherry-pick 4a10a9c634, 1c4208e1fb, ca33c38afd, 7de31b291e, a6cf98332b and 02bffba368 as-is; each applies cleanly on XR HEAD. 2) Apply 94eceb35e8 strictly after the five annotation/break commits; without them MSVC /WX fails in Hash.cpp, GLExtensions.cpp, MagneticCardReader.cpp, MemoryViewWidget.cpp, BTReal.cpp and CodeViewWidget.cpp. If G276 (24361f49d1) is already integrated, it applies cleanly. Otherwise keep XR's version-check block unchanged (hunk 1 is only a trailing-whitespace strip on a G276 line) and keep the added `/w15262 # Unannotated fallthrough between switch labels` line in the MSVC 'Additional warnings' add_compile_options block. 3) Re-configure and do a full Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON) build. Expected clean; the spots to watch are openxr_loader (jsoncpp json_reader.cpp:214/1115) and videovulkan (imgui_impl_vulkan.cpp). If anything in XR-only code trips C5262, do not patch XR code to fit: defer 94eceb35e8 alone (it is diagnostics policy with zero runtime effect) and keep the other six. 4) Optionally run a Quest build (gradlew app:assembleQuestDebug) for 02bffba368; at most it adds warnings. 5) Tell XR developers that once 94eceb35e8 lands, any new unannotated switch fallthrough in VR code breaks the Windows build.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** Verification (read-only on the repo; probe compiles went only to the scratchpad): (1) A heuristic fallthrough scanner (scratchpad ftscan.py) was validated on this PR. It flags exactly the 6 pre-fix sites, and at 94eceb35e8 only macro or 32-bit-#else false positives. At XR HEAD (2245 C/C++ files under Source/) the only extra findings compared with upstream 94eceb35e8 are those same 6 sites. All 178 switches / 1494 labels in the 251 XR-added or modified files parsed with zero findings, also with the noreturn whitelist disabled, and no XR-added macro contains switch/case. (2) MSVC 19.44.35215 (VS 17.14, the compiler in Build-vs2022) probe with /w15262: it flags a plain statement, if-without-else, a for-loop, and falling into `default: break;`. It does NOT flag `if (c) { ...; break; } default:`. (3) openxr_loader: the only matches are jsoncpp json_reader.cpp:214 and :1115 (`} // Else, fall through...`), which is that unflagged pattern. A real cl compile of json_reader.cpp with the loader flags + /W4 /WX /w15262 exits 0. The loader's own sources are clean. (4) Other /WX targets in Build-vs2022 with code outside Source/: libcurl_object, FatFs, spng_static, libminiupnpc-static (XR lacks the dolphin_disable_warnings that upstream added for miniupnpc by 94eceb35e8) and SDL_uclibc are C TUs (default/stdc11), and MSVC emits C5262 for C only under /std:clatest (probed). gtest/gmock, the glslang stub and 595 Qt moc/rcc outputs (incl. XR panes) scan clean. imgui_impl_vulkan.cpp has no switch. (5) Quest/Linux: check_and_add_flag uses directory-scoped add_compile_options (Source/ only), and the only -Werror is -Werror=format. Remaining risk is XR's own future code only. No INI, no Android files, not a duplicate or revert. No later in-range upstream commit touches these flags or adds [[fallthrough]].
- **Commits:**
  - `4a10a9c634` Add fallthroughs to GetMurmurHash3 — **MEDIUM**: Adds [[fallthrough]] to the 64-bit GetMurmurHash3 tail switch and drops one blank line. Codegen and hash values are unchanged (relevant because it is s_texture_hash_func, which XR texture overrides key on). Hash.cpp is not XR-modified; clean alone and greedy.
  - `1c4208e1fb` Add fallthroughs to OpenGL InitExtentionList — **MEDIUM**: Annotation-only [[fallthrough]] in the GLES/GL version cascade of InitExtensionList. The extension table used by XR's OGL OpenXR backend is unchanged. GLExtensions.cpp is not XR-modified; clean.
  - `ca33c38afd` Add missing breaks (no behaviour change) — **MEDIUM**: Adds `break;` before `default: break;` in MagneticCardReader::Command_33_ReadData (Triforce) and MemoryViewWidget::UpdateDispatcher; no behaviour change. merge-tree confirms that on XR HEAD's older (MB) MagneticCardReader the break lands at the end of `case 2:`. Neither file is XR-modified.
  - `7de31b291e` Fix missing break in BluetoothRealDevice — **MEDIUM**: In BluetoothRealDevice::IOCtlV, an unknown INTRMSG endpoint now breaks instead of falling into default, which removes a spurious 'Unknown request' error log. XR never touched IOS Bluetooth passthrough (its IR pointer work is in WiimoteEmu). Clean.
  - `a6cf98332b` Fix fallthough in CodeView/MemoryView widgets — **MEDIUM**: In the CodeViewWidget/MemoryViewWidget keyPressEvent handlers, Ctrl+F now returns after emitting search instead of also calling QWidget::keyPressEvent. XR did not modify these Qt debugger widgets and no XR code references them. Clean alone; its MemoryViewWidget hunk is independent of ca33c38afd's.
  - `02bffba368` Enable implicit fallthrough warnings for gcc/clang — **MEDIUM**: Adds check_and_add_flag(IMPLICIT_FALLTHROUGH -Wimplicit-fallthrough) for gcc/clang in Source/CMakeLists.txt (not XR-modified). It is scoped to Source/ (Externals/OpenXR unaffected) and produces warnings only: the Quest/Linux builds have no -Werror apart from -Werror=format. The clang-equivalent scan of all XR-HEAD Source/ code (incl. Source/Android/jni) finds nothing beyond the 5 annotated sites. This is a soft dependency on the annotation commits (without them it only adds warnings).
  - `94eceb35e8` Enable fallthrough warnings for msvc++ — **MEDIUM**: Adds /w15262 to the MSVC block of the XR-modified root CMakeLists.txt. Under XR's /WX this makes C5262 an error in all targets that inherit the root options, including XR-only openxr_loader (unshielded) and imgui_impl_vulkan.cpp in videovulkan. Checked clean: no fallthroughs in XR code, jsoncpp's pattern is not flagged by MSVC 19.44 (real compile passes), and C externals are immune. Conflict on XR HEAD: hunk 1 is a whitespace-only strip of the libc++_min_version line introduced by 24361f49d1 (G276), which is droppable; hunk 2 merges cleanly. Hard dependency on the 5 annotation commits.

### G320 — PR #14718 — Android: Make vibration length variable

- **Subsystem:** Android input (Kotlin ControllerInterface + native Android backend) + Core.cpp rumble reset
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-27 (merge `4f8af23db5`), 2 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/input/model/ControllerInterface.kt`, `Source/Core/InputCommon/ControllerInterface/Android/Android.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/Core.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Core/Core.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** (1) Android rumble now starts a 10 s one-shot and is cancelled through a new @Keep @JvmStatic ControllerInterface.cancelVibration(Vibrator), called from native AndroidMotor::SetState when the state drops below 0.5, replacing fixed 100 ms pulses. (2) Remove the '#if defined(__LIBUSB__)' guard around GCAdapter::ResetRumble() in Core.cpp so GC adapter rumble is also reset on Android.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** b67188cc44 adds a new Kotlin->native JNI entry point that native code looks up in the InputBackend constructor (GetStaticMethodID; if the Kotlin side is missing, backend init on Quest throws), changes Android rumble behavior, and textually needs G240 (f5d225691e/424269ed4b; ControllerInterface.kt alone conflict). ControllerInterface.kt itself is not XR-modified, but the Kotlin ControllerInterface object is referenced by XR-modified EmulationActivity.kt (dispatchKeyEvent/dispatchGenericMotionEvent, which are untouched), so rule 6 is not cleanly met. 82e576a183 edits XR-modified Core.cpp: the static ResetRumble() hunk is disjoint from XR's Core.cpp edits (OpenXR thread registration, PinEmulationThreadToPerformanceCore, CpuThread/FifoPlayerThread hooks) but is a behavior change on Android/Quest (GCAdapter::ResetRumble now runs on every pause/stop), so it is not behavior-neutral. Hence MEDIUM.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Core.cpp: XR's edits (includes ~L44/L95, RegisterCurrentThreadWithOpenXR/PinEmulationThreadToPerformanceCore helpers ~L124-150, CpuThread ~L363, FifoPlayerThread ~L456/L527) do not touch static ResetRumble(). Android: ControllerInterface.kt and Android.cpp are not XR-modified; no XR code calls vibrate/cancelVibration; XR-modified EmulationActivity.kt uses only ControllerInterface.dispatchKeyEvent/dispatchGenericMotionEvent. Quest VR controller rumble uses OpenXR haptics (OpenXRInputState), not Android Vibrators.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate after G240 (f5d225691e, 424269ed4b) and preferably after G226 (64a73e5636); both commits then apply cleanly (greedy clean). Review: the Android GCAdapter::ResetRumble path only writes a rumble-off payload under s_write_mutex and sets s_write_happened (no JNI, safe with no adapter attached). Test questDebug and a minified questRelease (XR release uses proguard-android.txt + proguard-rules.pro; @Keep keeps cancelVibration exactly like the existing vibrate) with a Bluetooth gamepad that has a vibrator: rumble stops on in-game off, pause, stop and exit, and there is no NoSuchMethodError at InputBackend init.
- **Upstream dependencies:** f5d225691e (G240), 424269ed4b (G240)
- **Handle together with:** G240 (Android: Set USAGE_MEDIA for rumble), G226 (Android: Fix mixup between Vibrator id and index)
- **Notes:** The two commits are independent (different files and subsystems); both kept MEDIUM: commit 1 for the new JNI coupling, Android behavior and G240 dependency, commit 2 for the behavior change in XR-modified Core.cpp.
- **Commits:**
  - `b67188cc44` Android: Make vibration length variable — **MEDIUM**: New native-called @Keep Kotlin method plus Android rumble behavior change; requires G240's ControllerInterface.kt context.
  - `82e576a183` Core: Remove ifdef for call to GCAdapter::ResetRumble — **MEDIUM**: Disjoint 2-line hunk in XR-modified Core.cpp, but it enables GCAdapter::ResetRumble on Android/Quest pause/stop (not behavior-neutral).

### G322 — PR #14829 — Android: Fix Crash when loading Skylanders file

- **Subsystem:** Android app: EmulationActivity NFC figure pickers (Skylanders portal / Infinity Base)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-29 (merge `9b50b88d2e`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/activities/EmulationActivity.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/infinitybase/ui/FigureSlotAdapter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/skylanders/ui/SkylanderSlotAdapter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/activities/EmulationActivity.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- **Upstream intent:** Switch the Skylander and Infinity figure file pickers from ActivityResultContracts.GetContent() to OpenDocument(). OpenDocument uses ACTION_OPEN_DOCUMENT, which grants write access to the URI that C++ opens read-write through ContentHandler.openFd(). GetContent did not, which caused the #13956 crash. The commit also replaces the `!!` force-unwraps in the requestSkylanderFile callback with null checks and a new 'Failed to load Skylander figure' toast (string `skylander_load_failed`). It skips slot updates when createSkylander returns -1. The adapters now call launch(arrayOf("*/*")).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This cannot be LOW under rubric rule 6: it touches two XR-modified Android files, EmulationActivity.kt (XR +61) and values/strings.xml (XR +175). Rule 2 also fails because the change is not behavior-neutral (the picker intent action changes). Otherwise it is as safe as a MEDIUM gets. The hunks are fully separate from XR's. A merge-tree simulation onto 5e9a304a65 (merge-base b1e360e1e4^) is clean: XR's QuestVrSettings import is kept, and the new string lands at strings.xml:1109 next to `invalid_skylander`, far from XR's VR string blocks. No XR-modified or XR-added Android file uses requestSkylanderFile or requestInfinityFigureFile; only the upstream SkylanderSlotAdapter and FigureSlotAdapter do, and XR didn't change them. At XR HEAD, SkylanderConfig.loadSkylander returns Pair<Int?,String?>? and createSkylander returns Pair<Int,String>, which matches what the new null and -1 checks expect. There is no JNI or native change, and android.widget.Toast is already imported. The commit adds `import org.dolphinemu.dolphinemu.utils.ContentHandler`, which is unused. It resolves to XR's ContentHandler.java; upstream only converts that class to Kotlin later, in G245, with the same fully qualified name. The unused import only produces a Kotlin warning, because no allWarningsAsErrors is set (XR has lint abortOnError=false).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** EmulationActivity.kt: XR added the QuestVrSettings import right below upstream's new ContentHandler import line (no conflict). XR also added stopRequestedForFinish/stopEmulationForActivityFinish() in onStop/onDestroy, the menu_emulation_recenter_vr → MENU_ACTION_RECENTER_OPENXR (NativeLibrary.RequestOpenXRRecenter) overlay item, and prepareQuestLaunchSettings/startEmulationActivity (VR intent category + finish()) in the companion launch helpers. None of these touch the requestSkylanderFile/requestInfinityFigureFile launchers or the createSkylander callback that upstream edits (lines ~104-152). strings.xml: XR's VR string hunks sit at MB lines ~344-1000, while upstream adds one string after `invalid_skylander` (MB line 935). The quest flavor has singleTask plus the immersive start mode for EmulationActivity. The picker is launched the same way as before (only the intent action changes), so XR's immersive behavior is unaffected.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick b1e360e1e4 unchanged; it applies cleanly on XR HEAD with no prerequisites. Build both flavors (gradlew app:assembleStandardDebug and app:assembleQuestDebug, or Source/Android/build-quest.ps1). Optional device smoke test on Quest: in immersive EmulationActivity, open the Skylanders portal and use Load/Create figure. Confirm that DocumentsUI opens, the figure loads, and figure writes no longer crash. Do the same for Infinity Base.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** The unused, non-alphabetical ContentHandler import is an upstream quirk and harmless. Nothing in the diff relates to rendering or input. The earlier upstream commits that touch the same files (G223/G235/G243/G309/G317) are not prerequisites, because the commit applies cleanly on XR HEAD by itself.
- **Commits:**
  - `b1e360e1e4` Android: Use OpenDocument for NFC figures and fix crash (Fixes #13956) — **MEDIUM**: Only touches the Skylander/Infinity picker launchers and callback in XR-modified EmulationActivity.kt, plus one new string in XR-modified strings.xml. The hunks are separate from XR's, the merge-tree result on XR HEAD is clean, and there is no JNI change. It is MEDIUM only because rule 6 does not allow LOW when an XR-modified Android file is touched.

### G339 — PR #14850 — DolphinNoGUI: Default to OFF

- **Subsystem:** Build system (top-level CMake options)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-09-13 (merge `9611279be5`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Stop building the DolphinNoGUI frontend (dolphin-emu-nogui) by default. Builders now opt in with -DENABLE_NOGUI=ON.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** It is a one-line default change, `option(ENABLE_NOGUI ... ON)` to OFF, in the XR-modified CMakeLists.txt at XR line 85. The hunk and its context are separate from XR's edits: the ENABLE_VR option at line 96 is 11 lines away, and it applies cleanly alone and in greedy_xr. The change is not behavior-neutral, though: a fresh Windows or Linux configure stops building dolphin-emu-nogui, which falls under rule 5's 'changes what the Windows build does'. So it is not LOW. It is a fork-policy choice. In practice it has almost no effect on XR. XR never modified Source/Core/DolphinNoGUI, and no XR script or Dockerfile mentions NoGUI (the Dockerfile builds `--target dolphin-emu`). Android already forces NoGUI off (CMakeLists.txt:448-450). The existing Build-vs2022/CMakeCache.txt keeps ENABLE_NOGUI:BOOL=ON, because option() never overrides a cached value.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** The only overlap is the option(ENABLE_NOGUI) line in CMakeLists.txt (XR line 85), which XR itself never edited. XR's CMakeLists edits are elsewhere: the FlagsOverride line 16, option ENABLE_VR line 96, the Qt6.5.1 prefix block ~493-499, the OpenXR loader block ~664-688 and CPACK_PACKAGE_CONTACT ~907. XR does not use or modify the NoGUI frontend.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Once the maintainer confirms XR does not need to ship dolphin-emu-nogui, cherry-pick 14e3c61d62 as-is. There are no conflicts and no XR code needs to change. To keep NoGUI, skip the commit or configure with -DENABLE_NOGUI=ON. Existing build dirs are unaffected until their cache entry is removed.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** The change only takes effect in fresh configure dirs. Build-vs2022 currently has ENABLE_NOGUI:BOOL=ON cached. It has no effect on the Quest build.
- **Commits:**
  - `14e3c61d62` DolphinNoGUI: Default to OFF — **MEDIUM**: Flips a build option default (NoGUI OFF) in XR-modified CMakeLists.txt. It applies cleanly and does not touch XR's edits, but it changes which targets a fresh Windows configure builds, so the maintainer should accept it knowingly.

### G340 — PR #14832 — DolphinQt: Properly define QtPaths

- **Subsystem:** Build system: Windows CMake / bundled-Qt deployment (windeployqt post-build)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-09-17 (merge `a96b5da856`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/qtpaths.bat.in`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/target_qt.conf.in`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Fix Windows cross-compilation, where windeployqt deployed the host's Qt DLLs. The commit defines DOLPHIN_HOST in the root CMakeLists and computes DOLPHIN_QT_ROOT, DOLPHIN_QT_HOST_ROOT and DOLPHIN_QT_TARGET_SPEC. It generates target_qt.conf and a qtpaths.bat shim from two new .in templates, makes windeployqt and qtpaths REQUIRED, and passes --qtpaths=<shim> to windeployqt.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Rule 5: this is a build-tooling change to XR's Windows post-build deploy step, in two XR-modified files. It conflicts both alone and in greedy_xr in Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt. Upstream's rewritten top block hardcodes Externals/Qt/Qt6.8.3/{x64,ARM64}, strings that come from the Qt bump e87225beb2 (G207). XR has Qt6.5.1 on those lines, plus a stray leading blank line. Taken verbatim, DOLPHIN_QT_ROOT and the target_qt.conf Prefix would point to a missing Qt6.8.3 folder, and the windeployqt POST_BUILD step would fail. The fix is simple and was verified: Qt 6.5.1's windeployqt has --qtpaths and its qtpaths accepts -qtconf. I ran windeployqt 6.5.1 in --dry-run mode with the shim adapted to Qt6.5.1 against Binary/x64/Release/Dolphin.exe. It deployed the same set as the current XR invocation (Qt6Core, Gui, Svg, Widgets plus qwindows, qsvg, qsvgicon, qwindowsvistastyle), with the same pre-existing warnings. The root CMakeLists.txt hunk (the DOLPHIN_HOST block after the unsupported-platform check) auto-merges and is disjoint from XR's edits.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** CMakeLists.txt: XR adds the ENABLE_VR option (~L96), a `list(PREPEND CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH .../Qt6.5.1/x64)` for ENABLE_QT AND MSVC (~L493-499), the OpenXR add_subdirectory block (~L664-688) and the CPACK contact. Upstream only inserts a DOLPHIN_HOST detection block around L235, which is disjoint and auto-merges. Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt: XR adds a leading blank line, keeps the Qt6.5.1 prefix paths in the `if (MSVC)` block, adds VR sources, and adds the dolphin-emu-sys-copy target plus the $<TARGET_FILE_DIR> Sys copy. Upstream replaces that top block with an `if (WIN32)` block that hardcodes Qt6.8.3 roots, which is a direct conflict with XR's Qt 6.5.1 pin. The windeployqt hunk (L576-594 at XR) gains the qtpaths shim and auto-merges.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Keep XR on Qt 6.5.1 and do not take G207. You can skip this group entirely, since XR builds x64 on an x64 host and gets no benefit. Or cherry-pick e9c5b57d88 and resolve DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt this way: take upstream's new `if (WIN32)` block, but set DOLPHIN_QT_X64_ROOT and DOLPHIN_QT_ARM64_ROOT to Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64 and Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/ARM64. Keep XR's root Qt6.5.1 PREPEND and XR's Sys-copy changes. Add qtpaths.bat.in and target_qt.conf.in unchanged. Then reconfigure Build-vs2022 (ENABLE_VR=ON) and build Release. Check that the post-build windeployqt step succeeds and that Binary/x64/Release contains the Qt6*.dll files and QtPlugins/{platforms,styles,imageformats,iconengines}.
- **Upstream dependencies:** e87225beb2 (G207)
- **Handle together with:** G207 (Update bundled Windows Qt to 6.8.3), G293 (windeployqt: Disable uneeded DLLs)
- **Notes:** The dependency on G207 is only textual (the Qt6.8.3 path strings), and the adapted version works on 6.5.1. Cross-batch warning: 6ec57bcc5d (G293) adds `--no-system-dxc-compiler` to the same windeployqt command. Qt 6.5.1's windeployqt rejects that option ('Unknown option', exit 1, verified), so the flag must stay out while XR is pinned to 6.5.1. G340's own hunk does not contain that line.
- **Commits:**
  - `e9c5b57d88` DolphinQt: Properly define QtPaths — **MEDIUM**: Windows build/deploy tooling change. It conflicts with XR's Qt6.5.1 pin in DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt and needs a manual 6.8.3-to-6.5.1 path substitution (verified to work with 6.5.1 windeployqt and qtpaths).

## High-risk commits requiring advanced review

Large or architectural changes that hit DolphinXR's rendering/OpenXR paths, the Windows/Quest toolchain, or heavily XR-extended Android code. These need design decisions and device testing. Integrate each as a unit, following the suggested strategy.

13 groups, 69 commits.

### G063 — PR #14476 — Android: Use version catalog and update dependencies

- **Subsystem:** Android build tooling (Gradle wrapper, AGP, Kotlin, R8/ProGuard, dependency catalog)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-03-22 (merge `6608bca694`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/benchmark/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/gradle.properties`, `Source/Android/gradle/libs.versions.toml`, `Source/Android/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar`, `Source/Android/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties`, `Source/Android/gradlew`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`
- **Upstream intent:** Move all plugin and dependency versions into the new gradle/libs.versions.toml and update the toolchain: - Gradle 9.2.0 to 9.4.1 (wrapper jar and gradlew regenerated). - AGP 8.13.1 to 9.1.0, using AGP's built-in Kotlin (drops the org.jetbrains.kotlin.android plugin and the kotlin{jvmTarget} blocks). - Kotlin and the serialization plugin 2.2.21 to 2.3.20. - compileSdkVersion replaced by compileSdk=36; buildFeatures.resValues=true. - Release switches from proguard-android.txt to proguard-android-optimize.txt, which turns R8 optimization on. - android.nonFinalResIds=false removed. - Benchmark module compile/targetSdk 34 to 36, managedDevices.allDevices. - Dependency bumps: core-ktx 1.18.0, lifecycle 2.10.0, kotlinx-serialization-json 1.10.0, swiperefreshlayout 1.2.0, baselineprofile/benchmark-macro 1.5.0-alpha04.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is a rule-5 toolchain change (AGP major 8 to 9, plus Gradle, Kotlin and R8 config) on the Quest build XR ships. It is textually clean: merge-tree onto XR HEAD keeps XR's flavors and build types. It is still HIGH because: 1. It hard-depends on 57a8a78d06 (G041, minSdk 24). Lifecycle 2.10.0 raised its minSdk to 23 (AndroidX release notes). XR still has minSdk=21 because G041 conflicts on Readme.md, so the manifest merger would fail. 2. proguard-android-optimize.txt turns on R8 optimization for XR's minified questRelease. XR's ENABLE_VR native code (MainAndroid.cpp GetEmulationActivity(), called in Run() before OpenXRManager::SetAndroidAppInfo) looks up NativeLibrary.getEmulationActivity through JNI GetStaticMethodID. That is an upstream @JvmStatic getter with no @Keep that upstream native code never calls, so R8 can inline and drop it. The result would be a VR boot failure in release APKs only; upstream hit the same kind of failure in G068. 3. AGP 9.1.0's bundled R8 crashes in minifyReleaseWithR8, so it needs 336f604b3a (G067) or the AGP bump in aeb3fec03a (G246). 4. Nothing has tested AGP 9's new DSL, built-in Kotlin and baselineprofile 1.5.0-alpha04 against XR's extra 'device' flavor dimension and releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable build types, nor Kotlin 2.3.20 on XR's Quest Kotlin sources.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts: - XR added `flavorDimensions += "device"` right after buildFeatures. Upstream adds resValues=true inside buildFeatures; the lines are adjacent but merge cleanly. - XR added productFlavors standard/quest (buildConfigField IS_QUEST, ndk.abiFilters, externalNativeBuild.cmake.arguments -DENABLE_VR=OFF/ON, quest applicationIdSuffix and manifestPlaceholders). - XR added build types releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable. They use initWith(release), so they inherit the new optimize ProGuard file. - XR removed the defaultConfig abiFilters. - Upstream's hunks (plugin aliases, compileSdk, kotlin{} removal, ProGuard file, dependency aliases) sit outside XR's lines. JNI: Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp GET_EMULATION_ACTIVITY_METHOD resolves to NativeLibrary.getEmulationActivity. XR's build-quest.ps1 and build-quest-release.ps1 stay compatible: JDK 17, NDK 29.0.14206865 is kept, and gradlew.bat launches the wrapper with -jar.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Treat this as a fork-policy decision and integrate as one unit: 1. 57a8a78d06 (G041): resolve Readme.md and take minSdk 24, which is fine for Quest. 2. 08f598f439 (G046): keep rule for the androidx.core.util.Pair constructor used in JNI_OnLoad. 3. e878174235 (this commit). 4. 336f604b3a (G067), unless continuing to aeb3fec03a (G246). 5. 9085d649dc (G068) if G058 is taken. Then validate: - build-quest.ps1 (assembleQuestDebug), build-quest-release.ps1 (assembleQuestRelease with R8), and assembleStandardDebug. - A Gradle sync of :benchmark, to check baseline-profile variant matching with the 'device' dimension. - In the questRelease output (mapping/usage.txt or dexdump), check that NativeLibrary.getEmulationActivity survived R8. Boot a VR game from the release APK on Quest. If the method was stripped, the integration must add a keep rule for that JNI entry point. Expect the first build to download AGP 9.1 and Gradle 9.4.1-bin.

- **Handle together with:** G041 (Android: Raise minSdk to 24 (Android 7.0)), G046 (Android: Add ProGuard keep rules for classes and methods ref), G067 (Android: Pin R8 9.1.34 to fix release minify crash)

Removing nonFinalResIds=false is safe: no Java `case R.` switches exist at XR HEAD.
