Needs minSdk 24 (G041), the R8 pin (G067 or G246), and verification of the XR JNI getEmulationActivity path in the minified release.

### G079 — PR #14512 — VideoCommon: ignore zero-stride EFB/XFB copies

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon/BPStructs (EFB copy / XFB copy dispatch in BPWritten, BPMEM_TRIGGER_EFB_COPY)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-03-27 (merge `14965a5ec0`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/BPStructs.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/BPStructs.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Treat EFB copies whose destination stride is 0 as no-ops. Both the EFB-to-texture and EFB-to-XFB paths are wrapped in `if (destStride != 0)`, so a zero-stride copy no longer calls CopyRenderTargetToTexture, disables bbox, triggers after_frame_event, or calls ImmediateSwap/FakeVIUpdate. Only the requested clear still runs.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Conflicts in BPStructs.cpp, both alone and in greedy order, because it re-indents the entire XFB-copy branch where XR inserted its VR frame-boundary logic. The git auto-merge is a trap, verified with merge-tree. It places XR's head-pose-lock block outside the XFB branch, inside the new destStride scope where `system` is out of scope. A naive 'fix' there would re-latch head pose after every EFB-to-texture copy, causing judder, and the remaining conflict region needs XR's VR blocks re-inserted into upstream's re-indented code. Semantically this is an EFB/XFB change in XR's most sensitive path: zero-stride XFB copies would stop driving XR's frame-boundary work and per-frame resets. Rubric: EFB/XFB change plus a non-trivial conflict, so HIGH.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** The XFB branch of BPStructs.cpp at XR HEAD contains: (1) the VR frame-region latch, VR::ConsumeVRPresentationSourceRegion / VR::NotifyVRXFBCopyRegion plus the extra `vram_source_rect` argument to CopyRenderTargetToTexture (cinematic-bar fix and viewport classifier); (2) the #ifdef ENABLE_VR block with VR::g_openxr->StampXFBPose(destAddr) and g_framebuffer_manager->RequestVRClearEFB(), before ImmediateSwap (deferred-XFB pose stamps and the deferred VR EFB clear); (3) the head-pose lock after the ImmediateXFB/FakeVIUpdate block: LocateViews, GeometryShaderManager::InvalidateVRHeadPose and InvalidateControllerAnchorCache with a 5 ms debounce; (4) ClearScreen(..., PE_copy.copy_to_xfb != 0), which sits outside the guarded section and is unaffected (the extra parameter is unused in XR's ClearScreen). Indirect effects: after_frame_event drives XR's VertexManagerBase::OnEndFrame (ShaderHunter, ElementsGroupManager, CullingCodeFinder, TextureElementManager and HideObjectEngine OnFrameEnd; GeometryShaderManager::OnEndFrame; CommitCameraAnchorFrame; MetroidElementClassifier::ResetFrame). ElementsGroupManager::ShouldClearEFBCopy's pending flag is consumed per EFB-to-texture copy. Zero-stride copies stop taking part in both.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not accept git's merge result. Start from XR HEAD's BPStructs.cpp and wrap the whole existing `if (PE_copy.copy_to_xfb == 0) {...} else {...}` in `if (destStride != 0) { ... }`. Keep every XR VR block in its current order inside the XFB else-branch: frame-region latch, then CopyRenderTargetToTexture(..., vram_source_rect), then after_frame_event, then StampXFBPose/RequestVRClearEFB, then ImmediateSwap/FakeVIUpdate, then the head-pose lock. Leave the `if (PE_copy.clear)` ClearScreen section outside the guard. Accept upstream's view that zero-stride XFB copies are not frames, so they skip all VR boundary hooks, which matches not presenting them. Optionally add a temporary VR log for zero-stride copies to see whether any VR-profiled game issues them. Build both platforms, then device-test frame-boundary behavior: no judder with Lock/ATW, deferred XFB present, the VR EFB clear, and Camera/Controller Anchor. Use MP1/MP Trilogy, Mario Galaxy, MKDD and Mario Golf with VR_VIEWPORT logging.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** greedy_mb is clean, so there is no upstream prerequisite. Later upstream BPStructs.cpp edits are e4349ae179 (G333) and its revert 3e95d0fc16 (G337), a net-zero pair that does not depend on G079. XR has not implemented this fix already: HEAD has no destStride guard.
- **Commits:**
  - `51e488e84a` VideoCommon: ignore zero-stride EFB/XFB copies — **HIGH**: Re-indents and guards XR's heavily modified XFB-copy branch (pose stamp, deferred EFB clear, head-pose lock, frame-region latch). Git misplaces XR's head-pose-lock block during auto-merge, and the change alters XR's frame-boundary semantics for zero-stride copies.

### G144 — PR #14612 — Remove GLX support

- **Subsystem:** Common/GL context creation (GLX/EGL) + OGL backend
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-22 (merge `d7b7c47453`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Common/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLContext.cpp`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLContext.h`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLInterface/GLX.cpp`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLInterface/GLX.h`, `Source/Core/VideoBackends/OGL/OGLMain.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLContext.h`, `Source/Core/Common/GL/GLInterface/GLX.h`, `Source/Core/VideoBackends/OGL/OGLMain.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Delete `GLContextGLX` (GLInterface/GLX.cpp/.h) and the libGL link, so EGL (`GLContextEGLX11`) is the only X11 path. Drop the `prefer_egl` parameter from `GLContext::Create(wsi, stereo, core, prefer_gles)` and update the OGLMain.cpp callers. This prepares for OpenGL GPU/adapter selection via EGL.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** OpenXR-adjacent GL-context change that conflicts with an XR design dependency. XR's Linux OpenXR OpenGL binding requires GLX: `OGLOpenXR::CreateSessionGL()` (`#elif HAVE_X11`) includes Common/GL/GLInterface/GLX.h and `<GL/glx.h>`, rejects any context whose `NativeContextType` isn't GLX, and fills `XrGraphicsBindingOpenGLXlibKHR` from XR-added `GLContextGLX` getters (`GetDisplay`/`GetFBConfig`/`GetDrawable`/`GetContext`). After this commit the Linux ENABLE_VR build fails (missing header). Even with that fixed, GL VR on desktop Linux would be impossible: EGL contexts are rejected, and SteamVR's Linux GL path uses the Xlib/GLX binding. Textually it is a modify/delete conflict on XR-modified GLX.h plus a conflict on XR's OGLMain.cpp `GLContext::Create(gl_wsi, ..., true, false, prefer_gles)` line, which became a 5-argument call that no longer compiles. This is a fork-policy/design decision, so HIGH.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** GLX.h: XR added `NativeContextType::GLX` and the `Display*`/`GLXFBConfig`/`GLXDrawable`/`GLXContext` getters used by OGLOpenXR.cpp (lines about 262-281), and upstream deletes the file. GLContext.h: XR's `NativeContextType` enum and the `GetNativeContextType()` virtual sit next to upstream's `Create()` signature change. OGLMain.cpp: XR's `Initialize()` builds `gl_wsi` (Android headless for VR) and both XR `Create()` calls pass `prefer_egl=false`. Root CMakeLists.txt states "OpenGL (GLX) is always built on desktop Linux, so VR remains usable without Vulkan". Windows (WGL) and Quest (EGL/GLES) are functionally unaffected, but they share the OGLMain.cpp call sites.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not cherry-pick; record it as intentionally skipped (fork policy) while XR supports OpenGL VR on desktop Linux. Later upstream groups don't depend on it: G206 ec0c4b72ff and G233 3f5460a5ff edit distant lines of Common/CMakeLists.txt, and G297/G302 touch EGLX11/GLExtensions. If upstream parity is wanted later, first add an EGL-based Linux OpenXR GL binding (for example XR_MNDX_egl_enable / `XrGraphicsBindingEGLMNDX`, which Monado supports, keeping the Xlib path for SteamVR). Then apply G144 and resolve it by removing XR's GLX getters and the `NativeContextType::GLX` branch, dropping the `prefer_egl` `false` argument from XR's two OGLMain.cpp `Create()` calls, and updating the root CMake comment. Verify on Linux X11 with both SteamVR and Monado.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** Upstream's stated goal (OpenGL adapter selection via EGL) is not in this range. G297 (EGLX11 forbids EGL_PLATFORM=wayland) is independent and remains applicable whether or not this group is taken.
- **Commits:**
  - `fc47091592` Remove GLX support — **HIGH**: Removes the GLX context class that XR's Linux OpenXR GL binding requires; conflicts with XR-modified GLX.h (modify/delete) and OGLMain.cpp.

### G160 — PR #14628 — Android: Remove HostThreadLock

- **Subsystem:** Android JNI (MainAndroid host-call serialization / threading)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-05-01 (merge `eb44b64c9e`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/jni/ActivityTracker.cpp`, `Source/Android/jni/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Android/jni/Host.cpp`, `Source/Android/jni/Host.h`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/jni/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Delete jni/Host.h and Host.cpp (the HostThreadLock global mutex) and every use in MainAndroid.cpp and ActivityTracker.cpp. Core no longer needs a single host thread; this is a follow-up to 068947e, which is already in MB.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** XR builds on HostThreadLock. XR-added jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp includes jni/Host.h and takes the lock at 8 sites: play/pause Core::SetState, Core::Stop for HK_STOP and for the 3-second return-to-menu hold, ResetButton_Tap, SaveScreenShot, DoFrameStep, and State::Load/Save slots. XR-added JNI NativeLibrary_RequestOpenXRRecenter also takes it. XR's Run() places VR and hotkey lifecycle work inside host_identity_guard: OpenXRManager::SetAndroidAppInfo, RequestRecenter, s_hotkey_dispatcher.Start/Stop and ClearAndroidAppInfo. A merge-tree onto XR HEAD conflicts in the include block and in the Run() tail. Even after resolving those, 11 HostThreadLock uses remain while the header is deleted, including UpdateGCAdapterScanThread, which only cc01a60347 (G145) removes. The result is a Quest/Android build break. Dropping Run()'s lock also changes how XR's background hotkey thread serializes with boot and shutdown, which is an XR design decision.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** MainAndroid.cpp (XR +221): the RequestOpenXRRecenter guard (~L295), the OpenXR controller-mapper JNI functions, and Run() VR/hotkey lifecycle code inside host_identity_guard. Conflicts are at L83-87 (XR's #include "jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.h" next to Host.h) and at L824-830 (XR's ENABLE_VR ClearAndroidAppInfo before host_identity_guard.Unlock()). jni/CMakeLists.txt auto-merges: upstream drops Host.cpp/.h and XR adds Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp/.h. XR-added jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp depends on the deleted header.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate only after G145 (cc01a60347). Recommended: apply it partially. Take upstream's guard removals in MainAndroid.cpp and ActivityTracker.cpp and the Run() lock/unlock removal, but do NOT delete jni/Host.h, Host.cpp or their CMakeLists entries; keep them as the header XR's HotkeyDispatcher.cpp and RequestOpenXRRecenter include. Resolve the conflicts as follows: in the include block keep both jni/Host.h and jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.h; in the Run() tail keep the ENABLE_VR ClearAndroidAppInfo block and drop host_identity_guard.Unlock(). Whether XR's own guards should go is a separate XR decision. The alternative is to skip G160 entirely: the greedy simulation shows G171 and G223 apply without it. Device-test on Quest: hotkey pause/stop and save/load state, the 3-second return-to-menu hold, recenter, Stop during boot, and surface destroy while booting.
- **Upstream dependencies:** cc01a60347 (G145)
- **Handle together with:** G145 (GCAdapter: Automatically start and stop thread)
- **Notes:** Small diff (-72 lines) that cannot be merged as-is. Upstream's rationale (Core has been thread-agnostic since 068947e, which XR already has) suggests dropping the lock is safe on upstream paths; XR's remaining HotkeyDispatcher guards would then only serialize among themselves.
- **Commits:**
  - `c73dde2193` Android: Remove HostThreadLock — **HIGH**: Deletes a header that XR-added HotkeyDispatcher.cpp and RequestOpenXRRecenter use. It conflicts in XR's Run() VR/hotkey lifecycle and changes the locking XR's Quest hotkey thread relies on. It also needs G145.

### G180 — PR #14631 — Android: Convert DirectoryInitialization to Kotlin

- **Subsystem:** Android app startup (user/Sys/GPU-driver directory init, Sys asset extraction)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-05-25 (merge `9b4534328f`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/activities/UserDataActivity.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/utils/DirectoryInitialization.java`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/utils/DirectoryInitialization.kt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/utils/DirectoryInitialization.java`
- **Upstream intent:** Java->Kotlin port of utils/DirectoryInitialization. It also drops API that upstream no longer uses (getSysDirectory, sysPath, EXTRA_STATE) and changes getGameListCache(Context) to getGameListCache(); UserDataActivity.kt is updated accordingly.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Modify/delete conflict on XR-modified DirectoryInitialization.java. XR's version re-extracts Sys when the extracted Sys/GameSettingsVR is missing. It also versions extraction with getSysDirectoryVersion() = GetGitRevision() + '-GameSettingsVR-' + Arrays.hashCode(sorted asset list), so updated per-game VR profiles actually reach the Quest. The Kotlin port keys only on the git revision, which would silently stop VR profile refreshes. The port also removes getSysDirectory() and sysPath, which XR-added QuestGameOverrideSettings.kt:58 and QuestGameVrConfigSettings.kt:259 call, so the build breaks. Everything else is compatible. Public API stays @JvmStatic, so Java AfterDirectoryInitializationRunner, GamePropertiesDialog.java and EmulationActivity.kt compile, and the nested DirectoryInitializationState keeps its binary name. JNI SetSysDirectory and SetGpuDriverDirectories stay as @JvmStatic private external. Dependencies exist: Java PermissionsHandler, ThemeHelper constants, TvUtil.isLeanback and core-ktx edit{}. Upstream never changed the Java file between MB and this commit.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** DirectoryInitialization.java: XR adds getSysDirectoryVersion() and the isMissingGameSettingsVr check in extractSysDirectory, and sets sysPath for getSysDirectory(). XR-added QuestGameOverrideSettings.kt and QuestGameVrConfigSettings.kt read Sys/GameSettingsVR INIs through DirectoryInitialization.getSysDirectory(). The UserDataActivity.kt change applies cleanly (not XR-modified). XR-modified StartupHandler.java, EmulationActivity.kt and MainActivity.kt call APIs that remain.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Take 7ef2906351's DirectoryInitialization.kt and its UserDataActivity.kt change, delete the Java file, then port XR's behavior into the Kotlin object. (1) Add a private lateinit var sysPath assigned before SetSysDirectory(sysPath), plus @JvmStatic fun getSysDirectory(): String with the same areDirectoriesAvailable IllegalStateException guard. (2) In extractSysDirectory, compute version = GetGitRevision() + "-GameSettingsVR-" + (assets.list("Sys/GameSettingsVR") ?: emptyArray()).sortedArray().contentHashCode(). On IOException, fall back to an empty array and Log.warning. contentHashCode equals Java Arrays.hashCode, so existing installs will not re-extract. Also re-extract when !File(sysDirectory, "GameSettingsVR").isDirectory. Then grep for getSysDirectory and getGameListCache callers, build assembleQuestDebug and assembleStandardDebug, and device-test a fresh install and an APK upgrade: Sys/GameSettingsVR must be present and refreshed, and the Quest per-game VR settings screens must load the sys INIs.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** This is the only conversion in the batch that overwrites XR logic. The G180 port calls TvUtil.isLeanback and PermissionsHandler.* in forms that work whether G167 and G058 are integrated or not.
- **Commits:**
  - `7ef2906351` Android: Convert DirectoryInitialization to Kotlin — **HIGH**: Deletes XR-modified Java, losing the GameSettingsVR extraction versioning silently. It also removes getSysDirectory(), which XR Quest settings code calls, breaking the build. A manual port of XR logic into Kotlin is required.

- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Move the bundled Windows Qt from 6.5.1 to 6.8.3. Externals/Qt goes 495517af2b -> 306289108d; the new tree deletes Qt6.5.1/ and adds Qt6.8.3/{x64,ARM64}. The commit points DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH and QtCompile.props at Qt6.8.3 and deploys styles/qmodernwindowsstyle instead of qwindowsvistastyle.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** A Qt version bump is a toolchain change that is explicitly not LOW, and the build constraints pin XR to Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1. The commit applies cleanly as text (XR only added a blank line at the top of DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt), but it creates a silent semantic conflict. XR's root CMakeLists.txt l.493-499 (XR commit 2d41ce7ac4 'qt6') PREPENDs Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64 with the comment 'locked to 6.5.1'. The new gitlink removes that directory, so XR's pin dangles, and stale Build-vs2022 CMakeCache entries (Qt6*_DIR, WINDEPLOYQT_EXE) break incremental builds. The bump also swaps the toolkit and default Windows style under XR's ~12.7k lines of custom Qt UI: VRPane/VRConfigWidget, Shader/Texture/Elements override editors, ShaderHunter/CullingCodeFinder widgets and OpenXR mapping dialogs. This is a fork design decision.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt l.1-8: the textual merge is clean (XR only prepended an empty line). The real overlap is XR's root CMakeLists.txt l.493-499 `if (ENABLE_QT AND MSVC) list(PREPEND CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH .../Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64)` from 2d41ce7ac4, which would point at a deleted directory. Every XR DolphinQt surface is affected by the style/toolkit change. XR's DolphinQt additions use no Qt private/QPA APIs or QT_VERSION checks (grep of the XR diff), so compile risk is low. The QtCompile.props part is irrelevant because XR does not use VS props.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not auto-pick; the maintainer must decide. Option A, the default under the current build constraints: skip G207, and also skip or adapt its dependents. Those are G293 (6ec57bcc5d adds windeployqt --no-system-dxc-compiler, which Qt 6.5.1's windeployqt does not support, verified via --help, so the post-build deploy would fail), G340 (e9c5b57d88 rewrites the Qt6.8.3 lines) and the Qt6.8.3 QT_HOST_PATH in G273's c1261e7406 (DolphinToolchain.cmake). Upstream's Qt code stays QT_VERSION_CHECK-guarded (6.5/6.6/6.7), so skipping does not block later UI commits. Option B, migrate: apply G207 together with G293 and G340, then `git submodule update Externals/Qt` (306289108d is present locally; its manifest says it was built with VS2022 Community, so the toolchain stays compatible). As part of the migration the maintainer revisits XR's own Qt6.5.1 pin in the root CMakeLists.txt. Then delete Build-vs2022/CMakeCache.txt, rebuild, confirm windeployqt deploys styles/qmodernwindowsstyle, and run a full light/dark UI pass on XR's Qt surfaces, VR config and hotkey/mapping windows.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Handle together with:** G293 (windeployqt: Disable uneeded DLLs), G340 (DolphinQt: Properly define QtPaths)
- **Notes:** G273 (c1261e7406 DolphinToolchain.cmake QT_HOST_PATH, d2833fb788 deleting QtCompile.props) also assumes Qt6.8.3. G130/G140 (QGroupBox Fusion style, guarded for Qt >= 6.6) interact with the style change. XR's Qt 6.10 GuiPrivate logic (PR #5 qt6-10-fix) is untouched by 6.8.3.
- **Commits:**
  - `e87225beb2` Update bundled Windows Qt to 6.8.3 — **HIGH**: Qt 6.5.1 -> 6.8.3 toolchain bump against XR's explicit Qt6.5.1 pin (root CMakeLists.txt, 2d41ce7ac4), which it silently breaks; this is a fork design decision.

### G223 — PR #14647 — Android netplay

- **Subsystem:** Android app (Jetpack Compose UI, settings models), Android JNI (jni/NetPlay, IDCache JNI_OnLoad, MainAndroid.cpp RunNetPlay), Gradle/Compose dependencies, Core NetPlayCommon helper
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-06-28 (merge `e2ab15aa23`), 46 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/ui/NetplaySetupActivity.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/ui/main/MainPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/ui/theme/DolphinTheme.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/menu/menu_game_grid.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`, `Source/Android/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/gradle/libs.versions.toml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/model/ConnectionRole.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/model/ConnectionType.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/model/NetplaySetupViewModel.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/Netplay.kt`, `Source/Android/jni/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Android/jni/NetPlay/Netplay.cpp`, `Source/Android/jni/NetPlay/NetPlayUICallbacks.cpp`, `Source/Android/jni/NetPlay/NetPlayUICallbacks.h`, `Source/Android/jni/AndroidCommon/IDCache.cpp`, `Source/Android/jni/AndroidCommon/IDCache.h`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/NativeLibrary.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/fragments/EmulationFragment.kt`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/ui/NetplaySetupScreen.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/model/NetplayViewModel.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/netplay/ui/NetplayActivity.kt` … and 20 more
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/NativeLibrary.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/model/BooleanSetting.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/model/IntSetting.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/fragments/EmulationFragment.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/ui/main/MainPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/menu/menu_game_grid.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`, `Source/Android/jni/CMakeLists.txt`, `Source/Android/jni/MainAndroid.cpp`
- **Upstream intent:** Adds Android NetPlay (join and host) as a new Jetpack Compose UI: a setup screen and a lobby with players, chat and a game picker; buffer, host-input-authority and network-mode controls; traversal/UPnP join info; save-transfer and game-digest progress; desync and connection-lost handling. It is backed by a JNI layer (jni/NetPlay/Netplay.cpp and NetPlayUICallbacks implementing NetPlay::NetPlayUI, with IDCache JNI_OnLoad lookups of NetplaySession and Player) and a new NativeLibrary.RunNetPlay boot path that boots with the host's BootSessionData. NetPlay keys are exposed as Int/Boolean/String settings. The core gains NetPlay::GetExternalIPAddress() (eb02680cb6), which replaces the duplicated copies in Qt and in the server.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** HIGH for four reasons. (1) Rule 5: it changes the toolchain and dependencies of the Quest Gradle build. It introduces Jetpack Compose: the org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose plugin in the root and app build.gradle.kts, buildFeatures.compose, the Compose BOM (2025.04.00, then 2026.04.01 in df5e7a9767), material3, material3-adaptive, material-icons-extended, activity-compose, runtime-livedata, ui-tooling(-preview), coil-compose, lifecycle-livedata-ktx, and a top-level kotlin compilerOptions flag (-Xannotation-default-target=param-property). All of it is written against gradle/libs.versions.toml, which does not exist at XR HEAD. That file comes from e878174235 (G063, HIGH: version catalog plus AGP 9), which itself needs 57a8a78d06 (G041, minSdk 24). (2) Hard native compile prerequisite: from 1464fdfc28 on, NetPlayUICallbacks overrides NetPlayUI::ShowChunkedProgressDialog(..., std::span<const int>). XR HEAD still declares const std::vector<int>& (the change is f6a67aa6e7, G120), so libmain, the Quest build included, fails to compile without G120. (3) Rule 6: it is a large Android feature (46 commits, +6185/-1632) that edits XR-modified Android files: app/build.gradle.kts, MainPresenter.kt, menu_game_grid.xml, strings.xml, NativeLibrary.kt, EmulationFragment.kt, jni/MainAndroid.cpp, jni/CMakeLists.txt, IntSetting.kt and BooleanSetting.kt. It also adds about 25 JNI_OnLoad class/field/method lookups that run on every Quest app start. (4) It collides with XR's Quest launch flow and with netplay determinism (see xr_overlap_details), so it needs a product decision, not just a merge. The intermediate commits are not runnable on XR on their own. 766374434c up to 06cc09cf09^ look up the Kotlin static field GameFileCacheManager.gameFileCache in JNI_OnLoad; that field comes from 301ca5fcb8 (G149), but XR still has the Java class with sGameFileCache. The result is a NoSuchFieldError at every app start until 06cc09cf09 removes the lookup. Release-type variants also fail to compile without b1d81af9cb (G225).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** app/build.gradle.kts: XR added flavorDimensions 'device', productFlavors standard and quest (IS_QUEST buildConfigField, per-flavor ndk abiFilters, -DENABLE_VR=OFF/ON) and the releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable build types, and removed the cmake abiFilters. G223 adds the compose plugin alias, buildFeatures.compose, a top-level kotlin compilerOptions block and the Compose dependency block, all in post-G063 catalog form. Its debugImplementation(ui-tooling-preview) also breaks compilation of XR's releaseNoMinify, releaseDebuggable and questRelease variants until G225 is applied. MainPresenter.kt and menu_game_grid.xml: XR added menu_quest_controller_presets and showQuestControllerSetupDialog; upstream adds menu_netplay leading to NetplaySetupActivity.launch. Disjoint, auto-merges. NativeLibrary.kt: XR added the OpenXR recenter/mapper externs and the Quest relaunch-MainActivity branch in finishEmulationActivity. Upstream adds external RunNetPlay(paths, riivolution, bootSessionDataPointer) after RunSystemMenu. Disjoint. EmulationFragment.kt: XR added the QuestVrSettings import and the surfaceView.alpha mirror toggle. Upstream adds a netplay branch in the emulation thread (NetplayManager.activeSession, stopGame collector calling stopEmulation, desync Toasts, RunNetPlay). Disjoint. jni/MainAndroid.cpp: the new JNI RunNetPlay calls Run(env, paths, riivolution, BootSessionData) and so reaches XR's static Run(env, BootParameters, riivolution). That function calls OpenXRManager::SetAndroidAppInfo, recenters on launch, starts and stops s_hotkey_dispatcher and calls ClearAndroidAppInfo, so netplay games boot in VR on Quest. The include of jni/NetPlay/NetPlayUICallbacks.h sits next to XR's added includes and auto-merges. jni/CMakeLists.txt: adds NetPlay/Netplay.cpp and NetPlayUICallbacks.cpp/.h beside XR's Input/HotkeyDispatcher entries. Auto-merges. IntSetting.kt and BooleanSetting.kt: XR added GFX_COMMAND_BUFFERS_IN_FLIGHT and set the ImmediateXFBEnable default to BuildConfig.IS_QUEST. Upstream only appends NETPLAY_* entries at the end of the enums. A merge-tree of the whole PR onto XR HEAD conflicts in exactly five files: app/build.gradle.kts, root build.gradle.kts, libs.versions.toml (modify/delete), Settings.kt and BooleanSetting.kt (the last two only on context lines from G086 and G006). Behavioral collision 1: XR's EmulationActivity.startEmulationActivity (Quest with isOpenXrImmersiveEnabled) adds the VR category and NEW_TASK\|CLEAR_TOP, then calls activity.finish() on the caller. NetplayActivity launches games through EmulationActivity.launch(this, path, false), so on Quest NetplayActivity is finished. NetplayViewModel.onCleared() then calls netplaySession.close(), which releases the native client and server and sets NetplayManager.activeSession to null. That races EmulationFragment's activeSession?.isLaunching check, so the session is lost or the game boots offline. XR's finishEmulationActivity also relaunches MainActivity when EmulationActivity is the task root, so the user lands in the game list, not the lobby. Desync Toasts are not visible inside an immersive session. Behavioral collision 2 (determinism): NetPlay syncs MAIN_VI_OVERCLOCK(_ENABLE) through NetPlayConfigLoader. XR's VideoInterfaceManager::RefreshConfig, via GetEffectiveVBIOverrideFactor(), replaces that factor with values from GFX_VR_FORCED_VBI_FREQUENCY or GFX_VR_AUTO_VBI_FROM_HMD whenever GFX_VR_ENABLE_OPENXR is set (default true). Those GFX keys are not synced, so a VR peer with a forced VBI rate runs a different VI rate from non-VR peers and desyncs.
- **Suggested integration approach:** 1) Do not cherry-pick commits one by one. Integrate the PR as one squashed unit, and only after its prerequisites: 57a8a78d06 (G041), then e878174235 (G063) with its own R8/AGP follow-ups as triaged (G067/G246), then f6a67aa6e7 (G120), then bedb283d93 (G165). Take 301ca5fcb8 (G149) first only if you must keep the commits unsquashed. 2) Land b1d81af9cb (G225) right after it: ui-tooling and ui-tooling-preview become implementation, which is required for XR's release, releaseNoMinify, releaseDebuggable and questRelease variants. Land 0bca7edf20 (G321) as well. 6825a4a954 (G238, UPnP) is optional. Then G242, G250, G309 and G323. 3) Resolve app/build.gradle.kts by keeping XR's flavorDimensions, productFlavors (IS_QUEST, abiFilters, ENABLE_VR cmake args) and the releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable build types, and adding upstream's compose plugin alias, compose=true, the kotlin compilerOptions flag and the Compose dependency block. For Settings.kt and BooleanSetting.kt, only append SECTION_INI_NETPLAY and NETPLAY_USE_UPNP. If G063 stays deferred, the only alternative is a manual port with explicit coordinates and org.jetbrains.kotlin.plugin.compose at XR's Kotlin 2.2.21. Check the minSdk and AGP requirements of the 2026.04 Compose BOM against XR's minSdk 21 and AGP 8.13.1 before doing that. 4) Make the Quest product decision before shipping. Either hide menu_netplay in the quest flavor (MainActivity already toggles Quest-only menu items) or make the netplay launch survive XR's startEmulationActivity finish() and new-task path. Document that VR Forced VBI and Auto-VBI-from-HMD must be off for netplay. 5) Verify the following. Build standardDebug, questDebug and questRelease; under R8, every NetplaySession and Player member resolved in IDCache JNI_OnLoad must keep its @Keep (b52ffa3e8b, 22863b3703). Cold-start the Quest APK and check that JNI_OnLoad does not throw. Build the Windows Qt client (NetPlayDialog now calls GetExternalIPAddress). Join and host from a phone. If netplay stays enabled on Quest, run one Quest netplay session against a desktop peer.

The PR does not need c73dde2193 (G160, HostThreadLock removal), because 068947e (Core: Remove IsHostThread) is already in XR. eb02680cb6 is marked LOW: it is a behavior-neutral refactor of the core NetPlay files (NetPlayCommon, NetPlayServer, Qt NetPlayDialog), none of which XR modified. Removes the GameFileCacheManager JNI_OnLoad lookup (ending the G149 dependency) and renames the IDCache Netplay accessors to NetplaySession; XR code only uses IDCache::GetNativeLibraryClass, so nothing XR uses is affected.

Touches XR-modified MainAndroid.cpp (RunNetPlay formatting, moves the NetPlayUICallbacks.h include after Core/System.h) and the Qt NetPlayDialog.cpp include order introduced by eb02680cb6. ### G246 — PR #14728 — Android: Update dependencies and build tools

- **Subsystem:** Android build tooling (AGP, Gradle wrapper, Kotlin, NDK, compile/target SDK, version catalog) + GCMemcardUtils NDK-30 name clash
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-13 (merge `990b421233`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/benchmark/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Android/gradle.properties`, `Source/Android/gradle/libs.versions.toml`, `Source/Android/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar`, `Source/Android/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties`, `Source/Android/gradlew`, `Source/Android/gradlew.bat`, `Source/Android/settings.gradle.kts`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/GCMemcard/GCMemcardUtils.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`
- **Upstream intent:** Raises compileSdk/targetSdk 36 to 37 (app and benchmark) and ndkVersion 29.0.14206865 to 30.0.15729638. Updates AGP 9.1.0 to 9.2.1 and Gradle 9.4.1 to 9.6.1 (wrapper jar, gradlew and gradlew.bat regenerated; adds retries properties). Updates Kotlin 2.3.20 to 2.4.0, coroutines and serialization to 1.11.0, compose-bom to 2026.06.01, core-ktx to 1.19.0, lifecycle to 2.11.0, material to 1.14.0, benchmark-macro to 1.5.0-alpha07 and uiautomator to 2.4.0. Removes the `kotlin { compilerOptions { -Xannotation-default-target=param-property } }` block and the temporary R8 9.1.34 buildscript pin. Raises Gradle MaxMetaspaceSize from 512m to 1g. Renames GCS_MAGIC to GCS_FILE_MAGIC in GCMemcardUtils.cpp to avoid NDK 30's arm64 asm/sigcontext.h GCS_MAGIC macro.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** A rule 5/6 toolchain change to XR's shipping Quest build, stacked on HIGH work. (1) It needs four earlier upstream changes: - the version catalog libs.versions.toml (missing at XR HEAD) and the AGP 9 migration from e878174235 (G063, HIGH); - the R8 pin it deletes, from 336f604b3a (G067); - minSdk 24 from 57a8a78d06 (G041). The greedy conflict is exactly XR's `minSdk = 21` against upstream's `minSdk = 24` context, because G041 was skipped on a Readme.md conflict; - Compose lines from c716c32ce8, f10022f2dc and df5e7a9767 (G223, Android netplay). (2) ndkVersion 30.0.15729638 contradicts XR's Source/Android/build-quest.ps1, which hard-codes ANDROID_NDK_HOME=android-ndk-r29 (XR's documented NDK r29 toolchain). The Quest build fails unless XR's scripts change. NDK 30 also brings new libc++ and kernel headers that XR's native OpenXR code and the patched Externals/OpenXR loader have never been compiled against. (3) targetSdk 37 changes runtime behavior of the quest flavor. Kotlin 2.4.0 and AGP 9.2.1 are untested with XR's 'device' flavor dimension, the quest/standard flavors and the releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable build types.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts at XR HEAD still uses the pre-G063 form: `compileSdkVersion = "android-36"`, `ndkVersion = "29.0.14206865"`, `minSdk = 21`, plugin ids with the 2.2.21 serialization plugin, `kotlin { compilerOptions { jvmTarget } }`, and the root build.gradle.kts pins AGP 8.13.1. XR's own additions are flavorDimensions "device", productFlavors standard/quest (IS_QUEST buildConfigField, abiFilters, -DENABLE_VR=OFF/ON, the .quest applicationIdSuffix and questSupportedDevices placeholder), the releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable build types, and removal of the defaultConfig abiFilters. Upstream's hunks (compileSdk/ndkVersion, targetSdk, removal of the kotlin{} freeCompilerArgs block) don't touch XR's blocks, but all of their context comes from G063, G041 and G223. XR's build-quest.ps1 and build-quest-release.ps1 pin JDK 17 and NDK r29. settings.gradle.kts, gradle.properties, the wrapper, gradlew/gradlew.bat, benchmark and GCMemcardUtils.cpp are not XR-modified. I grepped XR's native code for the other NDK sigcontext *_MAGIC names (ZA/ZT/FPMR/POE/TPIDR2/SVE/ESR/EXTRA/FPSIMD); none appear. GCS_MAGIC appears only in GCMemcardUtils.cpp.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Don't cherry-pick standalone. Make it part of a deliberate Android toolchain migration (fork-policy decision): 1. Take G041 (minSdk 24), then G063 with G046 and G067. 2. Either take G223 (Android netplay), or drop by hand the compose-bom, compose and kotlin{} hunks that depend on it. 3. Apply aeb3fec03a. Resolve build.gradle.kts by taking upstream's compileSdk 37, targetSdk 37 and ndkVersion lines while keeping XR's flavors and build types. 4. Either update build-quest.ps1 and build-quest-release.ps1 to NDK 30.0.15729638, or keep ndkVersion 29.0.14206865 in XR and defer the NDK bump. 5. The GCMemcardUtils.cpp GCS_MAGIC to GCS_FILE_MAGIC rename is self-contained and harmless; it can be taken on its own at any time and is mandatory for any NDK 30 build. 6. Install SDK platform 37 and confirm JDK 17 still satisfies AGP 9.2.1 and Gradle 9.6.1. 7. Validate assembleQuestDebug, assembleQuestRelease (R8; make sure the JNI lookup of NativeLibrary.getEmulationActivity survives minification), assembleStandardDebug and a :benchmark sync. 8. Device-test Quest: VR boot through the OpenXR loader on both Vulkan and GLES, controllers, and passthrough.
- **Upstream dependencies:** e878174235 (G063), 57a8a78d06 (G041), 336f604b3a (G067), c716c32ce8 (G223), f10022f2dc (G223), df5e7a9767 (G223)
- **Handle together with:** G041 (Android: Raise minSdk to 24 (Android 7.0)), G063 (Android: Use version catalog and update dependencies), G067 (Android: Pin R8 9.1.34 to fix release minify crash)
- **Notes:** G067's R8 pin becomes net-zero once this lands; another batch already noted that. G225 (b1d81af9cb) touches the same file but not these hunks, so it is not a prerequisite. The only C++ change is the GCMemcardUtils.cpp rename, which is behavior-neutral.
- **Commits:**
  - `aeb3fec03a` Android: Update dependencies and build tools — **HIGH**: Bumps AGP, Gradle, Kotlin, NDK 30 and SDK 37 on XR's Quest build. It requires the version catalog and AGP 9 work (G063, HIGH), minSdk 24 (G041) and the Compose lines (G223). It conflicts with XR's minSdk 21, and its NDK 30 pin contradicts XR's r29 build scripts.

### G273 — PR #14475 — CMake: Replace the Visual Studio project

- **Subsystem:** Build system (CMake / MSBuild removal / Android Gradle cmake version / runtime file layout)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-24 (merge `707d3c7a73`), 11 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Externals/Bochs_disasm/Bochs_disasm.vcxproj`, `Externals/Bochs_disasm/exports.props`, `Externals/FatFs/FatFs.vcxproj`, `Externals/FatFs/exports.props`, `Externals/FreeSurround/FreeSurround.vcxproj`, `Externals/FreeSurround/FreeSurround.vcxproj.filters`, `Externals/FreeSurround/exports.props`, `Externals/LZO/LZO.vcxproj`, `Externals/LZO/exports.props`, `Externals/SDL/SDL3.vcxproj`, `Externals/SDL/exports.props`, `Externals/SFML/SFML.vcxproj`, `Externals/SFML/exports.props`, `Externals/bzip2/bzip2.vcxproj`, `Externals/bzip2/bzip2.vcxproj.filters`, `Externals/bzip2/exports.props`, `Externals/cpp-ipc/cpp-ipc.vcxproj`, `Externals/cpp-ipc/exports.props`, `Externals/cpp-optparse/cpp-optparse.vcxproj`, `Externals/cpp-optparse/exports.props`, `Externals/cubeb/exports.props`, `Externals/cubeb/msvc/cubeb.vcxproj`, `Externals/cubeb/msvc/cubeb.vcxproj.filters`, `Externals/curl/curl.vcxproj`, `Externals/curl/exports.props` … and 112 more
- **XR-modified files touched:** `.gitignore`, `CMakeLists.txt`, `Readme.md`, `Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts`, `Source/Core/Common/FileUtil.cpp`, `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **Upstream intent:** Make CMake the only build system. It deletes every .sln/.vcxproj/.props/.targets and make_scmrev.h.js, then unifies the runtime layout on all platforms. Output goes to $<1:${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/Binaries>, so VS multi-config stops adding per-config subdirs. PDBs go to <build>/pdb, translations to Languages/<lang>/dolphin-emu.mo, and Sys/COPYING/Licenses/qt.conf/build_info are symlinked (or copied) next to the exe on Windows and Linux. LINUX_LOCAL_DEV is removed, and the Linux install dir changes from sys to Sys. It also raises the CMake minimum to 3.25 (including the Android Gradle cmake version), disables Windows install() targets, sets the VS startup project, errors out on Windows generic builds, and replaces CMakeSettings.json with CMakePresets.json + CMake/DolphinToolchain.cmake (VS 18 2026/v145 and Ninja presets, cross-compiling).
- **Why not integrated automatically:** HIGH as a unit, even though no emulation or VR code changes. (1) Rule 5: it removes the MSBuild build system and raises toolchain minimums (CMake 3.25 for the Windows/Linux builds and for the Gradle externalNativeBuild). (2) 6da4bc38e1 moves XR's VR exe from <src>/Binary/x64/Release/Dolphin.exe (the verified OutDir of Build-vs2022's dolphin-emu.vcxproj) to Build-vs2022/Binaries/Dolphin.exe. That is a fork workflow decision, and any launcher pointing at the old path silently runs a stale build. (3) 3d9f46cd64 really conflicts with XR's Windows Sys deployment in DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt (the always-run dolphin-emu-sys-copy target and the $<TARGET_FILE_DIR> POST_BUILD copy). That deployment is the only way Data/Sys/GameSettingsVR VR profile defaults reach File::GetSysDirectory() at runtime. Developer Mode is off on the dev machine: I ran the upstream helper and it failed with 'A required privilege is not held', then fell back to a copy. The commit also removes LINUX_LOCAL_DEV, which XR's Linux VR build dirs use (Build-linux-vr/-ubuntu-vr/-arch-vr all have LINUX_LOCAL_DEV=ON). Resolving the conflict wrongly either breaks Linux Sys or stops Windows Sys syncing. (4) c1261e7406's presets require VS 18 2026/v145, and its toolchain file assumes Qt 6.8.3 (e87225beb2, G207, HIGH). XR cannot adopt either (VS2022 + Qt6.5.1 only), and the commit also conflicts with XR's Readme (5 hunks) and .gitignore. Other commit levels: d2833fb788, 20bded8066, a30aa5cf36, fa2a250f01, 808a82d5dd and fa83b0fe49 are MEDIUM; 8366609fc1 is LOW; c7b621ad08 is BLOCKED on 808a82d5dd.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt: XR's if(WIN32) block (XR HEAD L527-549) adds the always-run custom target dolphin-emu-sys-copy (GLOB Data/Sys, remove_directory + copy_directory into $<TARGET_FILE_DIR:dolphin-emu>/Sys) and changes the POST_BUILD Sys copy to $<TARGET_FILE_DIR> (a workaround for VS appending /Release). 3d9f46cd64 rewrites exactly this block, giving one conflict hunk (verified with git merge-file). XR's added VR source lists (VRPane, VRConfigWidget, ShaderHunter, override widgets, HotkeyVR) do not overlap. a30aa5cf36 (gettext block) and fa83b0fe49 (final install line) are disjoint and clean. CMakeLists.txt: XR edits are the FlagsOverride relative path (L16), option ENABLE_VR (L96), the MSVC CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64 (L493-499), the OpenXR/ENABLE_VR block (L664-688) and CPACK_PACKAGE_CONTACT. Upstream's hunks (cmake_minimum/CMP0141/LINUX L1-28, VS_STARTUP_PROJECT after project(), LINUX_LOCAL_DEV L150/L392, output dir L183-195, PDB dir, ENABLE_GENERIC guard, install sys->Sys) are disjoint, and all are clean in greedy. Source/Core/Common/FileUtil.cpp: XR's D_GAMESETTINGSVR_IDX line in RebuildUserDirectories (L844) is disjoint from fa2a250f01's CreateSysDirectoryPath rewrite. However, XR loads VR defaults via File::GetSysDirectory()+GameSettingsVR (ConfigManager.cpp:75, ElementsGroupManager.cpp:32, HideObjectEngine.cpp:132, DolphinQt VRConfigWidget.cpp:97/600), so Sys resolution and deployment directly gate VR profiles. Source/Android/app/build.gradle.kts: XR's flavorDimensions, standard/quest productFlavors (-DENABLE_VR), releaseNoMinify/releaseDebuggable and the removed cmake abiFilters are disjoint from the version="3.22.1+" line, so the change is clean. .gitignore: XR's EOF 'DolphinXR dev artifacts' block gives an append/append conflict. Readme.md: XR's +171 lines of VR build docs (Build-vs2022 VS 17 2022 -DENABLE_VR=ON, openxr_loader target, Linux VR, Docker/.deb, Quest flavor) conflict in 5 hunks. VS project files: XR never modified them; it only has copies under docs/, which are ignored. Not overlapping: Externals/OpenXR. Its static loader inherits the genex output/PDB dirs harmlessly, and jsoncpp's own CMAKE_*_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY settings are never add_subdirectory'd.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate the whole PR as one manual step after 271a140715 (G261). (1) d2833fb788: resolve every modify/delete conflict by deleting the file (git rm). Nothing XR-specific is lost. XR's Readme line 'Use the solution file Source/dolphin-emu.sln' becomes stale. (2) Apply 20bded8066, a30aa5cf36 and fa2a250f01, then 3d9f46cd64 and 6da4bc38e1 together. 3d9f46cd64 alone would symlink Sys into Binary/x64 while the exe is in Binary/x64/Release. For the 3d9f46cd64 conflict, take upstream's restructured blocks: the 'if (NOT APPLE AND NOT ANDROID)' symlink-or-copy for COPYING/Licenses and Sys (needed for XR's Linux builds, since LINUX_LOCAL_DEV is gone), and the 'if (WIN32)' block for qt.conf/build_info/manifest/OUTPUT_NAME/windeployqt. For Windows Sys, choose exactly one writer. If the fork owner wants Data/Sys edits (e.g. GameSettingsVR) to keep syncing on every build without Developer Mode, keep XR's always-run dolphin-emu-sys-copy target. It resolves to Build-vs2022/Binaries/Sys after 6da4bc38e1, so drop the redundant XR POST_BUILD copy and exclude WIN32 from upstream's Sys symlink line. Otherwise rely on upstream's symlink and enable Developer Mode. (3) Apply 808a82d5dd, 8366609fc1, fa83b0fe49 and c7b621ad08 (c7b621ad08 is trivial once 808a82d5dd is in). (4) Defer or skip c1261e7406 until XR adopts VS2026 and Qt 6.8.3 (G314/G207). No later upstream commit needs CMakePresets.json or DolphinToolchain.cmake. If it is taken anyway: union .gitignore (XR block + 4e55d2518a's .cache/clangd + CMakeUserPresets.json), hand-merge Readme keeping all XR VR sections, and drop the .sln and LINUX_LOCAL_DEV instructions. (5) Verify: fresh configure of Build-vs2022 (VS 17 2022 -A x64 -DENABLE_VR=ON), build dolphin-emu, and check that Build-vs2022/Binaries has Dolphin.exe, Qt6*.dll, QtPlugins, qt.conf, Sys/GameSettingsVR and Languages/<lang>/dolphin-emu.mo. Run unittests (PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes should now find Sys). Launch a game with a VR profile (e.g. GM8E01) and confirm the profile loads (VR_CONFIG log). Build Quest via build-quest.ps1 (VS-bundled CMake 3.31.6 meets 3.25.0+). Reconfigure the Linux VR dirs and confirm Binaries/Sys is created. Reinstall any Linux .deb (sys->Sys). Tell the user the exe and PDB locations moved (Build-vs2022/Binaries, Build-vs2022/pdb), and update shortcuts and the build-dirs-vr memory note.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 271a140715 (G261), e87225beb2 (G207)
- **Handle together with:** G276 (CMake: Improve our version checking), G340 (DolphinQt: Properly define QtPaths)
- **Notes:** e87225beb2 (G207, Qt 6.8.3, HIGH) matters only for c1261e7406, whose DolphinToolchain.cmake sets QT_HOST_PATH to Externals/Qt/Qt6.8.3. The toolchain also sets CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME (cross-compiling), so even the Ninja presets cannot configure XR's Qt6.5.1 build. The VS presets need VS2026, which matches ed25d5649b (G314, HIGH). The merge 707d3c7a73 tree equals the branch tip c1261e7406, so there are no extra merge-only changes. Later upstream groups build on this PR: G276's 13238340ae hunks need 808a82d5dd, c7b621ad08 and 3d9f46cd64, and G340 rewrites the same DolphinQt WIN32 deploy block. Positive side effects of 6da4bc38e1 for XR: (a) Today windeployqt, qt.conf, COPYING and build_info go to Binary/x64 while Dolphin.exe is built into Binary/x64/Release (its Qt DLLs there are hand-copied, dated Feb 23). (b) tests.exe runs from Binary/x64/Tests/Release while the Sys copy goes to Binary/x64/Tests, which is exactly why PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes fails in tests-baseline-run.log (cannot write ...Tests\Release/Sys). (c) Build/ (ENABLE_VR=OFF) and Build-vs2022 stop clobbering the same Binary/x64/Release/Dolphin.exe. Downsides: Release, RelWithDebInfo and MinSizeRel now share one Dolphin.exe; .dockerignore excludes /Binary/ but not Build-vs2022/ (the context was already large). The user has .vs/ProjectSettings.json (CMakeSettings 'Release' config) and .vscode, so VS2022 Open Folder and VS Code CMake Tools would switch to the unusable presets if c1261e7406 is taken. The Android SDK only has cmake 3.22.1; scripted Quest builds put VS2022's CMake 3.31.6 first on PATH, and an existing .cxx cache shows C:/Program Files/CMake 4.2.3 being picked. XR's Dockerfile uses ubuntu:26.04, whose CMake is well above 3.25. No GameSettings INI is touched.
- **Commits:**
  - `d2833fb788` Remove Visual Studio Projects and Solutions in favor of CMake — **MEDIUM**: Deletes 123 MSBuild files (-11908 lines): all .sln/.vcxproj/.props/.targets, Languages.vcxproj, SCMRevGen.vcxproj and make_scmrev.h.js. XR builds only via CMake and never modified these files outside docs/. XR's VR sources exist only in the CMake lists, so the .sln is already unusable for XR, and there is no functional effect. It is not LOW because it removes a build system (rule 5). Conflicts are modify/delete only. Alone: cpp-ipc.vcxproj (d75794b774 G236), glslang.vcxproj (bb41867ffe G208), DolphinLib.props (G008/G033/G059/G075/G086/G096/G206/G233), DolphinQt.vcxproj (7209cf87cb G076, 4e55d2518a G159), UnitTests.vcxproj (3f5460a5ff G233), Base.Dolphin.props (36f45dce44 G008), QtCompile.props (e87225beb2 G207). In greedy only DolphinQt.vcxproj conflicts, because G159 was skipped. Resolve each by deleting the file.
  - `20bded8066` CMake: PDBs outside of Binaries — **MEDIUM**: Sets CMAKE_PDB_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY $<1:${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/pdb>, so XR's Windows PDBs move from Binary/x64/<Config>/ to Build-vs2022/pdb/. Debugging still works because the PDB path is embedded in the exe. The hunk is disjoint from XR's CMakeLists edits, but it changes Windows build outputs (rule 5). Alone it conflicts only because its context is the 'if(ENABLE_CCACHE)' block added by 271a140715 (G261, LOW); greedy is clean. The OpenXR static loader is unaffected.
  - `a30aa5cf36` CMake: Unify translation directories — **MEDIUM**: Moves Windows translations from <out>/Languages/<lang>.mo to <out>/Languages/<lang>/dolphin-emu.mo. Translation.cpp (not XR-modified) now loads that path on all non-Apple platforms, with a DATA_DIR/../locale fallback on Linux, and the Linux msgmerge step is dropped. The gettext hunk in XR-modified DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt is disjoint and clean. It is a runtime-layout change. Until 6da4bc38e1, VS writes the .mo files to Binary/x64/Languages while the exe sits in Binary/x64/Release, whose hand-copied old-style Languages/<lang>.mo files would no longer match. Taken alone, the non-English UI breaks, so integrate it with 6da4bc38e1.
  - `fa2a250f01` CMake: Unify sys directories — **MEDIUM**: Rewrites CreateSysDirectoryPath(). Apple and Android are unchanged. All other platforms, Windows included, use <exe>/Sys if it exists, else DATA_DIR "Sys" (DATA_DIR is defined for every CMake build). The Linux install dir moves from ${datadir}/sys to ${datadir}/Sys. XR's FileUtil.cpp edit (D_GAMESETTINGSVR_IDX, L844) is disjoint and clean. However, XR's GameSettingsVR defaults are read through File::GetSysDirectory() (ConfigManager.cpp:75, ElementsGroupManager.cpp:32, HideObjectEngine.cpp:132, VRConfigWidget.cpp:97/600), so this changes the path VR profiles take. Existing Linux .deb installs from XR's Dockerfile+cpack must be reinstalled. It is a prerequisite of 3d9f46cd64.
  - `3d9f46cd64` CMake: Remove LINUX_LOCAL_DEV and symlink required files — **HIGH**: Real conflict in XR-modified DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt. Upstream replaces XR's WIN32 Sys handling (the always-run dolphin-emu-sys-copy target plus a POST_BUILD copy to $<TARGET_FILE_DIR:dolphin-emu>/Sys) with a NOT APPLE AND NOT ANDROID POST_BUILD symlink-or-copy (new CMake/DolphinSymlinkOrCopy.cmake) of Sys/COPYING/Licenses to ${CMAKE_RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY}, plus qt.conf and build_info on WIN32. It also deletes the LINUX_LOCAL_DEV option and define, which XR's Linux VR build dirs set to ON. Verified on the dev box: Developer Mode is off, so the helper warns and re-copies only when POST_BUILD runs. Before 6da4bc38e1, the link lands in Binary/x64/Sys rather than next to the exe in Binary/x64/Release, so it must be integrated together with 6da4bc38e1. The resolution is a design decision that decides whether GameSettingsVR defaults reach the runtime. Keeping only XR's side leaves Linux with no Sys. Keeping only upstream's side stops Data/Sys syncing on Windows builds that don't relink.
  - `6da4bc38e1` CMake: Unify output directory — **HIGH**: Sets CMAKE_RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIRECTORY to $<1:${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/Binaries> on every platform. XR's VR exe moves from <src>/Binary/x64/Release/ to Build-vs2022/Binaries/, and the non-VR Build/ gets its own Binaries/. The patch is clean, and XR's uses of the variable are genex-safe (POST_BUILD commands; UnitTests' string(APPEND .. /Tests)). It fixes XR's per-config split: windeployqt, qt.conf and COPYING currently go to Binary/x64 while the exe is in /Release, and tests.exe runs without Sys, which explains the baseline PatchAllowlist failure. It is still a fork workflow decision: shortcuts and the documented Binary/x64/Release path would silently keep launching a stale exe, and Release/RelWithDebInfo now share one Dolphin.exe. Integrate with 3d9f46cd64.

Raises cmake_minimum_required from 3.20 to 3.25, drops the CMP0141 and LINUX<3.25 shims (no-ops with XR's CMake 4.2.3/3.31.6), and changes the Android externalNativeBuild cmake version from 3.22.1+ to 3.25.0+ in XR-modified app/build.gradle.kts. That hunk is disjoint from XR's flavors and build types, and clean. The Android SDK has only cmake 3.22.1, which would now be rejected. build-quest.ps1 and build-quest-release.ps1 prepend VS2022's CMake 3.31.6, and an existing .cxx cache already shows C:/Program Files/CMake 4.2.3 being picked, so the scripted Quest builds pass. Android Studio builds without that PATH need CMake >= 3.25 visible to AGP. The Linux hosts and the ubuntu:26.04 Docker image are fine.

The hunk is disjoint from all XR edits, and the commit is clean alone and in greedy. XR never builds generic on Windows, and the Quest gradle -DENABLE_GENERIC line is commented out, so XR's Windows and Quest builds are unchanged (rule 5 small self-contained CMake fix).

The only XR-modified file is DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt, where the hunk (else() -> elseif (NOT WIN32) at the end) is disjoint and clean. Linux install/CPack (XR's Docker .deb flow) is unchanged. Windows INSTALL/PACKAGE targets, which exist in Build-vs2022, would stop shipping the executables. I found no XR use of them, but the change is not behavior-neutral, so it is not LOW.
