It is disjoint from XR edits and would otherwise be LOW. Alone it conflicts in CMakeLists.txt because its context requires 808a82d5dd's removal of the 'if (CMAKE_VERSION VERSION_LESS "3.25")' block after project(). Greedy is clean.
  - `c1261e7406` CMake: Introduce Presets, replacing Settings — **HIGH**: Adds CMakePresets.json and CMake/DolphinToolchain.cmake, deletes CMakeSettings.json, and edits .gitignore and Readme.md. The VS presets hardcode the 'Visual Studio 18 2026' generator and v145 toolset (XR is VS2022-only). Every x64/arm64 preset uses the toolchain, which sets CMAKE_SYSTEM_NAME (cross-compiling) and QT_HOST_PATH=Externals/Qt/Qt6.8.3 (e87225beb2, G207, which XR does not take; XR pins Qt6.5.1), so no preset can configure XR. Deleting CMakeSettings.json changes VS2022 Open Folder behavior (the user's .vs/ProjectSettings.json uses its 'Release' config), and VS Code CMake Tools would switch to presets. Conflicts: .gitignore EOF append/append (XR block + 4e55d2518a G159 clangd lines) and Readme.md (5 hunks vs XR's VR build docs). No later upstream commit needs these files, so it can be deferred.

### G314 — PR #14800 — Require VS2026; Update Redist WinUpdater Pulls

- **Subsystem:** Windows build toolchain (MSVC PCH version gate) + WinUpdater
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-20 (merge `38e70fda65`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Common/build_info.txt.in`, `Source/Core/WinUpdater/Platform.cpp`, `Source/PCH/pch.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Upstream intent:** Make VS2026 (MSVC >= 19.51.36252) the required Windows compiler through the #error check in Source/PCH/pch.h. Also switch the updater's VC++ redist download (VCToolsUpdateURL in build_info.txt.in and VCToolsUpdateURLDefault in WinUpdater/Platform.cpp) from aka.ms/vs/17/release to the new aka.ms/vc14 link, and refresh the comment block for the 14.51 redist.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This commit breaks the build on XR's required toolchain. XR builds only with VS2022, and Build-vs2022/CMakeFiles/4.2.3/CMakeCXXCompiler.cmake records MSVC 19.44.35215, so _MSC_FULL_VER is 194435215, below the new 195136252 minimum. XR's MSVC CMake build compiles pch.h: Source/CMakeLists.txt runs `if (MSVC) add_subdirectory(PCH)`, build_pch compiles pch.cpp with /Ycpch.h, and every use_pch target force-includes it with /FIpch.h. The new `#error Please update your build environment to the latest Visual Studio 2026!` would therefore stop every Windows build. The commit applies cleanly (alone and in greedy_xr), so an automatic cherry-pick would break the build without any conflict to warn about. Rule 5 explicitly excludes VS2026 requirements, and adopting one is a fork-level toolchain decision, so this is HIGH.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** There is no textual overlap: XR never modified Source/PCH/pch.h, Source/Core/WinUpdater/Platform.cpp or Source/Core/Common/build_info.txt.in. The conflict is with XR's VS2022-only build environment (Build-vs2022, generator 'Visual Studio 17 2022', MSVC 19.44.35215).
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do NOT auto-apply. By default, skip ed25d5649b entirely while XR stays on VS2022. If the updater changes are wanted: run `git cherry-pick -n ed25d5649b`, restore Source/PCH/pch.h to XR's version (keep `_MSC_FULL_VER < 193632532` and the 'Visual Studio 2022' message), then commit. The remaining VCToolsUpdateURL/VCToolsUpdateURLDefault switch to aka.ms/vc14 and the build_info comment change are harmless for VS2022-built binaries, because the v14 redist is backward compatible with 14.4x. Take the pch.h hunk only if XR decides separately to move to VS2026 ('Visual Studio 18 2026' generator, which would also change the Build-vs2022 workflow).
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** Upstream's CMake minimums are still MSVC_min_version 19.32 and MSVC_toolset_min_version 143, set by G276 (13238340ae/f98c974a0d). After G314, pch.h is therefore upstream's only VS2026 check, and CMake configure would not reveal the break. No later in-range commit touches pch.h, build_info.txt.in or WinUpdater. A quick grep found no VS2026-only STL headers (<flat_map>/<flat_set>) at the upstream target. Later upstream commits may still assume MSVC 19.51+, so integrators should compile-test on VS2022. The earlier commit e222665eb0 (G237) touched the same files but is not a textual or semantic prerequisite (alone-apply is clean).
- **Commits:**
  - `ed25d5649b` Require VS 2026, update WinUpdater redist — **HIGH**: The new #error check in pch.h requires MSVC 19.51.36252 (VS2026), so XR's VS2022 build (MSVC 19.44.35215) fails. It applies cleanly, which is why it must not be auto-picked.

### G317 — PR #14742 — Android: Add global settings search

- **Subsystem:** Android settings framework (SettingsActivity/Fragment/Presenters, layouts)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-08-23 (merge `474fa6e6f2`), 2 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/model/view/SettingsItem.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/model/view/SettingsSearchResult.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsActivity.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsActivityPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsActivityView.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsAdapter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsFragment.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsFragmentView.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/viewholder/SettingViewHolder.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/viewholder/SettingsSearchResultViewHolder.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/utils/ThemeHelper.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/anim/anim_settings_search_pop_in.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/anim/anim_settings_search_pop_out.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/color/settings_search_outline.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/drawable/ic_search.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/layout/activity_settings.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/layout/fragment_settings.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/layout/list_item_search_result.xml`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsActivityPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsFragment.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/java/org/dolphinemu/dolphinemu/features/settings/ui/SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt`, `Source/Android/app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml`
- **Upstream intent:** Adds a global search to the Android settings. SettingsFragmentPresenter lazily builds a search index by walking every menu from MenuTag.SETTINGS: it follows SubmenuSetting entries recursively and SingleChoice menu-tag actions, on Dispatchers.IO under a Mutex. loadSettingsList is refactored into tryBuildSettingsList(targetMenuTag, extras, updateWarnings), which temporarily swaps menuTag. The controllerNumber/controllerType/serialPort1Type fields are replaced by menuExtras, and wildcard imports by explicit ones. SettingsActivityPresenter gains search state and getMenuTagAction()/getMenuTagActionExtras(). SettingsFragment gains the search UI, debounce, and scroll-to/highlight of a result. SettingsFragmentView.onSettingChanged now takes a SettingsItem. New layouts, animations, drawables and 4 strings. The second commit is a reformat.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is a large rewrite (+1342/-703) of exactly the Android settings framework XR extended most: SettingsFragmentPresenter +1313, SettingsActivityPresenter +34, SettingsFragment +39, MenuTag +53. A simulated apply on XR HEAD gives 8 conflict hunks in SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt, 2 in SettingsActivityPresenter.kt and 1 in SettingsFragment.kt; the reformat commit then conflicts in 7 files. Semantic hazards: (1) About 30 XR MenuTag cases (OPENXR, QUEST_VR_*, QUEST_VR_CONFIG_*, QUEST_* overrides, HOTKEYS*) must move into tryBuildSettingsList, otherwise opening them throws 'Unimplemented menu'. (2) The explicit import lists replace the wildcards that XR relies on for QuestVrSettings, QuestGameOverrideSettings, QuestGameOverrideEnabledSetting, QuestGameVrConfig{Boolean,Float,Int}Setting, OpenXRControllerMapperSetting and similar; without re-adding them the build fails. (3) XR's per-game revision plumbing (ARG_REVISION via fragmentExtrasWithRevision, the ARG_REVISION Bundle in SettingsFragment submenus) and the OpenXR Wii Remote value 3 in onMenuTagAction/hasMenuTagActionForValue are rewritten into getMenuTagAction(). If they are not ported, search navigation opens per-game Quest VR menus with revision 0, and OpenXR Wii Remote mapping menus become unreachable and unsearchable. (4) Building the index runs every menu builder, including XR's Quest builders that read per-game INIs and the EGLHelper probe, on a background thread. (5) It is textually tied to earlier upstream settings commits (crop, debug and GBA settings, the EGLHelper rename, API-23 guard removal). It also needs G352: activity_settings.xml crashes on API 28 and below, and XR's minSdk is 21.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt: XR's `when (menuTag)` additions in loadSettingsList, the revision field/onCreate, the wildcard imports, and the addTopLevelSettings Quest submenus. SettingsActivityPresenter.kt: fragmentExtrasWithRevision() and the `value == 1 \|\| value == 3` (OpenXR Wii Remote) logic in onMenuTagAction/hasMenuTagActionForValue. SettingsFragment.kt: the ARG_REVISION Bundle in the submenu showSettingsFragment call, and the titles map entries for the XR MenuTags. strings.xml: disjoint insertion.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not cherry-pick mechanically. First settle its textual predecessors (G231, G235, G176, G142, G086, G098, G043), or port by hand against XR's versions. Apply 893be0996e + 93706022d5 + b5748b81d6 (G352) as one unit on a branch: - SettingsFragmentPresenter: take upstream's structure, then re-add every XR MenuTag case to tryBuildSettingsList. The hotkey category builder uses menuTag, which is fine because tryBuildSettingsList swaps menuTag. Keep the revision field, read ARG_REVISION from menuExtras, and re-add explicit imports for all XR model/activity classes. - SettingsActivityPresenter: implement getMenuTagAction with `isWiimoteMenu && (value == 1 \|\| value == 3)`, and wrap every returned extras (and showSearchResult) with fragmentExtrasWithRevision. - SettingsFragment: keep XR's titles entries and the ARG_REVISION submenu Bundle, and pass ARG_REVISION in search navigation extras. - Decide whether the dynamic per-game Quest override lists should be indexed. Test on Quest: global and per-game settings, searching for a VR setting and for a per-game VR Config setting (check the right revision INI is edited), GC/Wii controller menus including the OpenXR Wii Remote, and in-game settings during emulation. Also test on an API ≤ 28 device if generic Android builds matter.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 1800f5cf51 (G231), db77b117d9 (G235), a5059cbca8 (G176), 4aaecbb148 (G142), 5167adc8df (G086), 26583fd026 (G098), fd20322f5f (G043)
- **Handle together with:** G352 (Fix android settings crash on API level 28 and lower)
- **Notes:** The search-index warm-up constructs EGLHelper, which makes an EGL context current on a Dispatchers.IO thread, whenever the SETTINGS screen opens, including in-game on Quest while the GLES/Vulkan OpenXR session runs. Upstream does the same, but verify there is no interference with XR's native EGL/OpenXR contexts; eglBindAPI is per-thread. The reformat commit is meaningless without the first commit.
- **Commits:**
  - `893be0996e` Android: Add global settings search — **HIGH**: Restructures the XR-extended settings presenters/fragment (11 conflict hunks in 3 XR files). XR menus, revision plumbing, the OpenXR Wii Remote value 3 and wildcard-import-dependent XR classes must all be ported.
  - `93706022d5` Android: Reformat settings search files — **HIGH**: Reformat of the files the first commit just rewrote (including XR-modified SettingsActivityPresenter.kt/SettingsFragment.kt). Only applies as a unit with 893be0996e.

### G327 — PR #14837 — VideoCommon: separate out reinterpret and palette texture creation from rendering

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon TextureCacheBase (EFB-copy palette conversion and format reinterpretation)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-09-01 (merge `a1e636d72c`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/TextureCacheBase.h`
- **Upstream intent:** Split each GPU conversion into an allocation step and a rendering step. ApplyPaletteToEntry becomes CreatePaletteEntryWithOffset (allocate, upload TLUT, insert into m_textures_by_address) plus a public static RenderPaletteEntry (pipeline lookup, uniforms, utility draw). ReinterpretEntry likewise becomes CreateReinterpretEntry plus a public static RenderReinterpretEntry. All three call sites are updated (DoPartialTextureUpdates and two in GetTexture). This prepares future texture refactors.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** It rewrites the exact function that holds XR's flagship Metroid Prime stereo palette fix. XR's ApplyPaletteToEntry (TextureCacheBase.cpp L346-L392) chooses the layered palette pipeline (ShaderCache::GetPaletteConversionPipeline(tlutfmt, layered), with mono fallback) for MP1 640x448 thermal sources and MP2 408x286 dark-visor highlight sources. The choice depends on the D3D/Vulkan fix flags and the game-ID gates, and logs VR_PALETTE. Merge-tree shows a single conflict: XR's whole block against upstream's deleted pipeline lookup in CreatePaletteEntryWithOffset. Taking upstream's side compiles and silently restores the mono pipeline, bringing back the missing-eye thermal/dark visor regression. Taking XR's side does not compile, because tlutfmt is no longer a parameter. The new RenderPaletteEntry only receives the decoded entry, whose is_xfb_copy has been cleared by SetNotCopy, and an AbstractTexture*, so the source metadata XR's gates use must be threaded through. Upstream also changes failure semantics. The entry is now allocated and cached before the pipeline check, so an unrendered texture is returned on pipeline failure. RenderReinterpretEntry/RenderPaletteEntry are called before the `if (decoded_entry)` null checks, so an allocation failure becomes a null dereference; that is more plausible under Quest's 2-layer VRAM pressure. Texel-upload failure now passes an uninitialized offset. This is a design-level manual port in an XR caution area (EFB copies and stereo textures).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** TextureCacheBase.cpp: XR's ApplyPaletteToEntry layered-pipeline selection (L346-L392) uses vr_metroid_thermal_visor_fix / vr_metroid_d3d_thermal_palette_fix, IsMetroidVulkanPaletteFixGame/IsMetroidD3DPaletteFixGame (L72-L114), IsMetroidPrime1ThermalStereoSourceCandidate, IsMetroidPrime2DarkHighlightTextureCandidate (L127) and the VR_PALETTE log. It relies on XR's ShaderCache::GetPaletteConversionPipeline(TLUTFormat, bool layered) and m_layered_palette_conversion_pipelines (ShaderCache.cpp L1549). XR did not change ReinterpretEntry or the call sites (L969/L976/L1770/L1774/L1783). XR's TextureCacheBase.h additions auto-merge.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Manual port; never resolve with 'theirs'. Take upstream's split, then move XR's existing selection block unchanged into RenderPaletteEntry in place of upstream's GetPaletteConversionPipeline(tlutfmt). That covers the fix flags, the MP1/MP2 candidate gates, the layered-to-mono fallback and the VR_PALETTE log. It is portable to a static function, since it only uses globals, file-static helpers and a static member. Keep the gate inputs taken from the SOURCE entry (native_width/height, GetNumLayers(), is_xfb_copy) by passing the source entry or a precomputed use_layered_pipeline from the three call sites (DoPartialTextureUpdates; GetTexture combined reinterpret+palette path; GetTexture unconverted_copy path). The decoded entry loses is_xfb_copy. Keep (or restore) null checks before calling Render* so allocation failure does not crash. Test with VR_PALETTE logging on: GM8E01 thermal visor, G2ME01 dark visor plus red highlight, and R3ME01 Trilogy, on Vulkan (PC and Quest), D3D11 and D3D12, checking both eyes. Also sanity-check a non-VR game that uses palette/reinterpret EFB copies.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** No upstream code at the target (771fb154059c) calls the new static Render* functions outside TextureCacheBase, so adjusting their signature in XR has no upstream knock-on. The earlier same-file commits (G056, G097, G137, G139) are not prerequisites; the alone merge has only the XR conflict.
- **Commits:**
  - `9263f6b7c6` VideoCommon: separate out reinterpret and palette texture creation from rendering — **HIGH**: Refactors ApplyPaletteToEntry/ReinterpretEntry, where XR's Metroid stereo (layered) palette-conversion fix lives. The conflict resolution needs a design decision, and an easy wrong resolution compiles while silently dropping the VR fix.

### G335 — PR #14789 — Optimize vertex_shader_uid_data down to 28 bytes

- **Subsystem:** VideoCommon shader UIDs (VertexShaderGen, NativeVertexFormat VB_HAS_* bits, XFMemory texgen enums, PipelineUtils ApplyDriverBugs, GX pipeline UID cache)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-09-06 (merge `debe8e9fd9`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GXPipelineTypes.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/NativeVertexFormat.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/PipelineUtils.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VertexShaderGen.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VertexShaderGen.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/XFMemory.h`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/VideoCommon/GXPipelineTypes.h`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/PipelineUtils.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VertexShaderGen.cpp`, `Source/Core/VideoCommon/VertexShaderGen.h`
- **Upstream intent:** Repack vertex_shader_uid_data from about 41 bytes (more than 80 on MSVC, which ignored pack(1) for mixed-type bitfields) to a fixed 28 bytes enforced by static_asserts. The commit: (a) renumbers VB_HAS_* bits (TEXMTXIDX0-7 -> bits 0-7, POSMTXIDX -> 8, NORMAL/TANGENT/BINORMAL -> 9-11, COL0/1 -> 12-13, UV -> 16-23) and drops the UV bits from uid.components; (b) replaces texcoord_elem_count/texMtxInfo_n_projection/texMtxInfo[8]/postMtxInfo[8] with a 2-byte-per-texgen texGenInfo[8] {texcoord_elem_count:2, inputform:1, sourcerow:4, is_regular_texgen tag, union regular{postmtx_index, postmtx_normalize, projection} / other{texgentype, emboss_sourceshift, emboss_lightshift}}; (c) narrows TexSize/TexInputForm/TexGenType/SourceRow to u8, with BitFields using explicit u32 storage; (d) sets dualTexTrans_enabled only when a regular texgen exists and adds ASSERT(sourcerow <= Tex7); (e) bumps GX_PIPELINE_UID_VERSION 8 -> 9. Generated VS code is equivalent for valid XF states.
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is a shader-pipeline UID refactor in an XR caution area, and it requires several XR-specific decisions: (1) Textual conflicts in two XR-modified headers. In VertexShaderGen.h, XR's `u32 code_version : 6;` (VERTEX_SHADER_CODE_VERSION=21) sits in the exact first bitfield word upstream rewrites. In GXPipelineTypes.h, XR has 13 and upstream has 9. (2) Silent compile break, with no textual conflict, on PC and Quest. XR-only ShaderHunter.cpp ComputeVertexFamilySignature (about L89-121) reads uid.texcoord_elem_count, uid.texMtxInfo_n_projection, uid.texMtxInfo[i].{inputform,texgentype,sourcerow,embosssourceshift,embosslightshift} and uid.postMtxInfo[i].{index,normalize}. Upstream removes all of these, and uid.components changes meaning. (3) Persisted VR override identities break. XR's exact VS hash is CRC32 over the raw VS UID bytes (VertexManagerBase.cpp about L1127), so every VS hash changes. In Data/Sys/GameSettingsVR, 37 Type=VS exact_hash ShaderOverrides stop matching (RMCE01 20, GMSP01 8, GMSE04 4, G9SP8P 3, G9SJ8P 2). So do 83 legacy (v1 / element_only_v5) ElementsGroup selected_match_*_vs_family values that are compared against that hash (RMCEA2 33, GEAE8P 27, GUPE8P 15, D43E01 5, G9SJ8P 2, RSPP01 1). Users' own INIs are affected too. The 9 v2 semantic VS families survive only if the rewritten function reproduces the old values. (4) Bumping FAMILY_SCHEME_VERSION is not a drop-in fix. ShaderHunter (legacy_scheme checks at about L1561 and L1709) and ElementsGroupManager::DoesSelectedMatchFilterPass (about L1873) treat any version below current as a raw CRC for both VS and GS, so a naive bump also breaks all v2 GS families. (5) The GX pipeline UID version must resolve above XR's 13. These design decisions make it HIGH.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** VertexShaderGen.h: XR added `u32 code_version : 6;` after position_has_3_elems, which conflicts with upstream's new first word. XR's UseVRMultiviewVSProjection/GenerateVRMultiviewVSProjection declarations auto-merge. GXPipelineTypes.h: GX_PIPELINE_UID_VERSION 13 (XR, last set by 4825e49442 'Exact Screen Depth'; XR history 8 -> 10 -> 12 -> 13) vs 9 (upstream), a conflict. VertexShaderGen.cpp: XR's `uid_data->code_version = VERTEX_SHADER_CODE_VERSION;` in GetVertexShaderUid sits next to upstream's rewritten components/UV block and auto-merges (verified with merge-tree). XR's multiview VS projection, vr_depth flat varying, o.viewPos, clip-distance neutralization and vertex-rounding hunks are disjoint and read no texgen UID fields. PipelineUtils.cpp: XR's passthrough-coverage MRT dual-source strip at the top of ApplyDriverBugs is disjoint from upstream's VS line/point-expand texcoord_elem_count hunk, and auto-merges. Semantic overlaps outside the commit's files: XR-only ShaderHunter.cpp (ComputeVertexFamilySignature, ComputeShaderFamilySignature, DumpShader), ElementsGroupManager.cpp selected-match filters, VertexManagerBase.cpp exact-hash computation, and Data/Sys/GameSettingsVR/*.ini persisted VS hashes and families. NativeVertexFormat.h and XFMemory.h are not XR-modified. No XR code hardcodes VB_HAS_* bit positions, and no other XR struct uses the narrowed enums as bitfields.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not auto-pick. Option A: defer. This is a pure optimization with no user-visible fix, and nothing later in range touches these files or uses texGenInfo/VB_HAS_SHARED; schedule it for a planned VR-profile recapture window. Option B: integrate. 1) Cherry-pick e5eabd08f5. Resolve GXPipelineTypes.h to GX_PIPELINE_UID_VERSION = 14 (XR 13 + 1). Do not use 9: that is upstream Dolphin's own post-PR value, and the .uidcache loader only checks magic, version and size-multiple. 2) In VertexShaderGen.h, take upstream's struct. Keep XR's `u32 code_version : 6;` in the first word after `VSExpand vs_expand : 2;` and shrink `u32 pad : 8` to `pad : 2` (14+1+1+4+2+2+6+2 = 32), so static_assert(sizeof == 28) holds. Keep XR's multiview declarations and the auto-merged code_version assignment. 3) Rewrite ShaderHunter::ComputeVertexFamilySignature for texGenInfo. To keep FAMILY_SCHEME_VERSION = 2 values stable, mix the old values reconstructed from the new UID:    - components_old = ((c & 0xFF) << 2) \| (((c >> 8) & 1) << 1) \| (((c >> 9) & 0x1F) << 10) \| sum over i = 0..7 of ((texGenInfo[i].texcoord_elem_count != 0) << (15 + i)).    - texcoord_elem_count_old = 0, because families use the pre-ApplyDriverBugs UID.    - texMtxInfo_n_projection_old = sum of (is_regular_texgen ? regular.projection : 0) << i.    - Per texgen: inputform and sourcerow as-is; texgentype = is_regular_texgen ? Regular : other.texgentype; emboss shifts from other.* only for EmbossMap, else 0; postmtx fields from regular.* only when dualTexTrans_enabled, else 0.    This is exact for all valid XF states except two: dualTexTrans enabled with only non-regular texgens, and invalid texgentype 4-7. If a v3 scheme is preferred instead, the legacy checks at ShaderHunter about L1561/L1709 and ElementsGroupManager about L1873 must become per-version (v1 = raw CRC, v2 = old-semantic VS plus semantic GS). 4) Exact VS hashes change unavoidably, the same as an XR VERTEX_SHADER_CODE_VERSION bump. Recapture the 37 VS exact_hash overrides and the 83 v1 Elements VS families, ideally converting them to shader_family v2, and tell users to recapture their own VS exact-hash overrides. 5) Build on Windows (VS2022, ENABLE_VR=ON) and Quest. Device-test RMCE01, GMSE01/GMSP01, D43E01 and Metroid Prime with specialized shaders and ubershaders, on D3D11/D3D12/Vulkan (GS stereo) and Quest Vulkan multiview. Also test Shader Hunter capture and dump.
- **Upstream dependencies:** none identified
- **Notes:** No in-range upstream prerequisites (greedy_mb clean), and no later in-range commit touches VertexShaderGen.*, NativeVertexFormat.h, XFMemory.h, GXPipelineTypes.h or PipelineUtils.cpp, so it can be deferred on its own. Future upstream VS work beyond this range will assume the new layout, so drift grows if it is skipped long-term. MSVC/VS2022: upstream's static_asserts (sizeof == 28, texGenInfo 2 bytes, offsets) verify the MSVC 17.x bitfield packing at compile time, and Common::BitField at XR HEAD already supports the explicit StorageType parameter. Side benefit for XR: per upstream's commit message, MSVC ignored pack(1), so XR's raw-UID VS hashes and v1 families currently differ between PC (MSVC) and Quest (Clang) builds. After this commit the layout is pinned to 28 bytes everywhere, so a one-time recapture should make VS identities portable between PC and Quest. The new ASSERT(sourcerow <= Tex7) in GetVertexShaderUid raises a PanicAlert on invalid XF states, which can be intrusive mid-VR session; this is an edge case. ShaderHunter's m_uid_cache is in-memory only, so no on-disk UID bytes beyond the .uidcache files are invalidated.
- **Commits:**
  - `e5eabd08f5` Optimize vertex_shader_uid_data down to 28 bytes — **HIGH**: Repacks the VS UID. Conflicts in XR's VertexShaderGen.h (code_version:6) and GXPipelineTypes.h (13 vs 9). Breaks the build of XR-only ShaderHunter::ComputeVertexFamilySignature. Changes every exact VS hash (CRC32 of the raw UID), invalidating 37 VS exact_hash overrides and 83 legacy Elements VS families in GameSettingsVR. Needs a family-signature compatibility decision.

## Blocked commits

These commits are simple, or even LOW by content, but depend on a deferred (MEDIUM/HIGH) commit. "Root cause" is the deferred commit that must land first, resolved transitively through the dependency data. Integrate the root, then these usually apply as-is.

G038 is a refactor running through four XR-modified files in frame-pacing and OSD caution areas: Core.cpp (CpuThread Reset, Callback_FramePresented), CoreTiming.cpp (SleepUntil/Throttle/AdjustEventQueueTimes), VideoInterface.cpp (EndField) and OnScreenUI.cpp (Finalize). I scanned XR HEAD's include graph with the System.h->VideoEvents.h edge cut. 21 XR-added or XR-modified TUs lose VideoEvents.h through System.h, including VRPane.cpp, OpenXRUtilitySession.cpp, CullingCodeFinderWidget.cpp, OpenXRWiimoteConfigSessionController.cpp, jni/Input/HotkeyDispatcher.cpp, HotkeyScheduler.cpp, State.cpp, UICommon.cpp, GeometryShaderManager.cpp and TextureInfo.cpp. None of them uses VideoEvents, PresentInfo, GetVideoEvents() or the *_event members. The files that use EventHook/HookableEvent (State.cpp, UICommon.cpp) still reach Common/HookableEvent.h through other headers. No XR code uses g_perf_metrics or PerformanceMetrics through System.h, so no XR build break is expected once G038 is in.

Build Windows (VS2022) and Quest. If any XR TU reports an incomplete type VideoEvents or PerformanceMetrics, add the direct include in that XR file; the scan found none.

#### G092 — PR #14540 — HW:HSP: Change read/write interface to use std::span and other cleanups.

- **Subsystem:** HW/HSP (High-Speed Port devices: Null, ARAM expansion, Game Boy Player) + HW/DSP ARAM DMA
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-02 (merge `f11d535d3e`), 3 commit(s)

- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Why not integrated automatically:** Every touched file is XR-untouched (HSP/*, DSP.cpp), and HSP/DSP is on the rubric's isolated-subsystem list. greedy_xr is clean, with no XR overlap and no toolchain change, so the content is LOW-class. However, 207fe1f5de and 9410094d9f modify HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.cpp/.h and the `case HSPDeviceType::GBPlayer` factory line. These only exist after 3ece5f7d7b (G086) and are listed in missing_at_xr_head. merge-tree confirms 207fe1f5de conflicts without G086. G086 is MEDIUM (Quest builds mGBA via 606bcbe3ed; XR-modified Android files via 5167adc8df), so the PR as a unit is BLOCKED. Exception: 187fcfa7dc touches only HSP.cpp/HSP.h, has no in-range prerequisite (clean alone and in greedy_xr) and is behavior-neutral, so it is LOW on its own.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** None directly. HSP/*, DSP.cpp and Memmap::GetPointerForRange are XR-untouched. HSP_Device.h gains <span> and TRANSFER_SIZE; XR-modified MainSettings.cpp includes it only for the HSPDeviceType enum, which does not change. Behavior note for 9410094d9f: on the GameCube (non-Wii) ARAM DMA path for ARAddr >= 16 MiB with the default None device, reads now leave MRAM untouched instead of writing zeros. The SDK's ARAM-expansion probe zero-fills its read buffer before the DMA, so the outcome should be the same. Every GC title runs this probe at boot, including the Metroid/Zelda VR targets.
- **Suggested integration approach:** 1) 187fcfa7dc can be cherry-picked any time; it is independent. 2) After G086 is integrated (3ece5f7d7b present), cherry-pick 207fe1f5de and then 9410094d9f. merge-tree confirms both are clean on top of G086 whether or not 187fcfa7dc was applied earlier, and the final tree is identical to upstream order. 3) Build Windows Build-vs2022. The std::span, std::ranges::fill/copy and fmt::join (<fmt/ranges.h>) patterns compile with MSVC 19.44 under Dolphin's /W4 /WX /permissive- flags (probe). 4) Smoke-test a GameCube VR title boot with audio (e.g. GM8E01) to cover the ARAM-probe path. If HSPDevice is set to ARAM expansion, also check an expansion-RAM game. 5) Then apply G105, G106, G109 and G110, which build on this interface (and G123 later). If the fork never takes G086, 207fe1f5de and 9410094d9f can still be ported by hand by dropping the HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.* hunks and the GBPlayer factory case. That would be manual, MEDIUM-style work.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 3ece5f7d7b (G086)
- **Handle together with:** G086 (HW: Implement Game Boy Player.), G105 (Improve Game Boy Player audio conversion to PWM), G106 (HW/GBPlayer: Fix GBA cart detection in GBI.), G109 (HW/GBPlayer: Fix swapped L/R triggers.), G110 (HW/GBPlayer: Use u32 in SampleToPWM), G123 (HW/GBPlayer: Improvements.)
- **Notes:** No STATE_VERSION change; HSPManager::DoState format is unchanged. At XR HEAD, AddDevice/RemoveDevice have no callers outside HSP.cpp. G106 edits code inside the new Read(span) and G109 edits the new keypad byte layout, so both follow 9410094d9f.

The null checks were dead code because HSPDevice_Create always returns a device and DoState already dereferenced m_device. #### G119 — PR #14571 — GameSettings: Remove disabled Immediate XFB for many games that no longer need it.

- **Subsystem:** Data/Sys/GameSettings (ImmediateXFBEnable overrides)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-11 (merge `27e09633de`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GAU.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GDJ.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GJU.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GSM.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GVR.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GXL.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R2D.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R2P.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R3P.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RM6.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/ROQ.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RPY.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SAK.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SCY.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SEM.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SK8.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SKY.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SQI.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SSP.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/STS.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SVX.ini`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Why not integrated automatically:** This is INI-only for non-VR titles, which rule 7 alone would treat as LOW. It is BLOCKED for two reasons. (1) It depends on upstream PR #14512 = 51e488e84a (G079, 'VideoCommon: ignore zero-stride EFB/XFB copies'). G079 wraps the whole EFB/XFB copy branch of BPWritten in 'if (destStride != 0)' inside XR-modified BPStructs.cpp, and conflicts both alone and greedy. That branch is exactly where XR inserted VR::NotifyVRXFBCopyRegion, VR::g_openxr->StampXFBPose(destAddr), g_framebuffer_manager->RequestVRClearEFB() and the head-pose lock (LocateViews, InvalidateVRHeadPose, InvalidateControllerAnchorCache), so G079 is MEDIUM/HIGH. (2) XR changes what these overrides do. XR's Quest build defaults Immediate XFB to ON: GraphicsSettings.cpp has '#if defined(__ANDROID__) && defined(ENABLE_VR) constexpr bool DEFAULT_IMMEDIATE_XFB = true', and Android's BooleanSetting.GFX_HACK_IMMEDIATE_XFB defaults to BuildConfig.IS_QUEST. On Quest, these 19 games would therefore switch automatically from deferred to immediate XFB, and VideoConfig::VRLockHeadPosePerFrame() (= !bImmediateXFB) would flip. Without G079, the black flickering these lines prevented would return on Quest.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** No file overlap: XR never modified Data/Sys/GameSettings. The only VR-profile game touched is GAUE08 (GAU.ini), and that edit is comment-only with the override kept. Behavioural overlap: the Quest DEFAULT_IMMEDIATE_XFB=true (Core/Config/GraphicsSettings.cpp, Android BooleanSetting.kt), VideoConfig.h VRLockHeadPosePerFrame(), and, through the G079 dependency, XR's per-XFB-copy VR hooks in VideoCommon/BPStructs.cpp.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Cherry-pick (it applies cleanly) only after G079 (51e488e84a) is merged into XR's BPStructs.cpp and validated. When resolving G079, decide explicitly whether zero-stride XFB copies should skip XR's StampXFBPose, RequestVRClearEFB and head-pose-lock hooks. Then test on Quest, where Immediate XFB is on by default, with at least one Skylanders game (SSP/SKY/SK8/SVX), Disney Infinity or Toy Story 3, plus X-Men Legends, looking for black flicker and head-tracking judder. On PC, test with Immediate XFB enabled globally. If G079 is deferred, defer G119 too, along with the other INI groups that remove ImmediateXFB overrides (G154, G220, G221).
- **Upstream dependencies:** 51e488e84a (G079)
- **Handle together with:** G079 (VideoCommon: ignore zero-stride EFB/XFB copies), G154 (GameSettings: Allow ImmediateXFB for a handful of games that), G220 (GameSettings: Disable fast depth calc to fix missing text an), G221 (GameSettings: Don't force immediate XFB off in Ultimate Spid)
- **INI video-setting change:** yes (EFB/XFB/copy/vertex-rounding settings)
- **Notes:** Touched game IDs: GDJ, GJU, GVR, GXL, R2D, R2P, R3P, RM6, ROQ, RPY, SAK, SCY, SEM, SK8, SKY, SQI, SSP, STS, SVX. None is a VR-profile or Metroid title. The deleted INIs are not referenced by ApprovedInis.json or any build script. On PC the XR default Immediate XFB is false, so only users who enabled it globally see a change. Some INIs had the key spelled ImmediateXFBenable; that was already effective, because IniFile keys are case-insensitive.
- **Commits:**
  - `23a1c7bd94` GameSettings: Remove disabled Immediate XFB for many games that no longer need it. — **BLOCKED**: Removing these overrides is only correct once upstream PR #14512 (G079, 51e488e84a) is present, and G079 rewrites XR's VR-hooked XFB-copy path in BPStructs.cpp. On Quest, XR's Immediate-XFB-on default makes the removal take effect automatically.

#### G123 — PR #14560 — HW/GBPlayer: Improvements.

- **Subsystem:** Core/HW Game Boy Player (HSP device HSP_DeviceGBPlayer) + HW/GBACore (mGBA wrapper)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-04-13 (merge `c51d285264`), 3 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/Core/HW/GBACore.h`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/HSP/HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/HSP/HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.h`, `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`, `Source/Core/Core/HW/GBACore.cpp`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/Core/State.cpp`
- **Why not integrated automatically:** On its own merits this would be LOW. GBACore.* and HW/HSP/* are subsystems XR never touched (`git diff b55aaa8ca7 5e9a304a65` is empty for them), and the rubric names them as isolated. The only XR-file hunk is the State.cpp STATE_VERSION line, which has the documented trivial resolution. There is no toolchain change. The mGBA submodule is identical (0b40863f) at the merge-base, XR HEAD, 571d166c7c and the upstream target, and it already has mAVStream::audioRateChanged/postAudioBuffer, mCoreCallbacks::videoFrameEnded and addCoreCallbacks. No feature VS2022 lacks is used (designated init, std::ranges::fill, span CTAD). Rule 4 fails, though. 571d166c7c rewrites HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.cpp/.h, which do not exist at XR HEAD: they are created by 3ece5f7d7b in G086 (HW: Implement Game Boy Player). G086 is at least MEDIUM: 5167adc8df edits XR-modified Android BooleanSetting.kt, SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt and strings.xml; 606bcbe3ed edits the XR-modified root CMakeLists.txt to enable mGBA in non-Qt builds, which changes the Quest NDK build; 19157cacd5 edits the XR-modified MainSettings.cpp. So the whole PR is BLOCKED on G086. The greedy_xr conflict on HSP_DeviceGBPlayer.cpp is a cascade artifact: greedy skipped ef997d0d82 (G105), whose only conflict is STATE_VERSION, and therefore also b083ce5912 (G110), which 571d166c7c's context lines need. greedy_mb is clean.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Only Source/Core/Core/State.cpp. Upstream changes a single line, `constexpr u32 STATE_VERSION = 188;  // Last changed in PR 14579`, to 189/PR 14560. XR's line 100 is `STATE_VERSION = 181;  // Last changed for VR pane-remap GS constant`. XR's other State.cpp edits are disjoint: the <future> include, the CompressAndDumpStateArgs::compression_type field, the GetCompressionType/CreateExtendedHeader/WriteHeadersToFile signature changes, and the removal of s_use_compression. No XR code references GBACore, HSP or GBPlayer; git grep over Source/Android, VideoCommon/VR and InputCommon/ControllerInterface/OpenXR finds nothing. The new 4096 Hz CoreTiming event is registered only while the GB Player HSP device is attached, and it is scheduled in GC ticks via GetTicksPerSecond(). XR's VR forced-VBI change in CoreTiming::RefreshConfig only scales m_config_oc_factor, i.e. cycles per downcount, so it does not interact. VR frame pacing, video and input are not touched.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate only after the GB Player / GBACore stack is in, in landing order: G078 (d76757bba6), G080 (670a5df7ce, which makes TimedCallback a MoveOnlyFunction; this is required for the capturing lambda in RegisterEvent), G082, then G086 as a whole group, handled as MEDIUM because of its Android and CMake parts. After that apply G092, G105, G106, G109 and G110, and preferably G098 and G108. Then cherry-pick cba86c42f5, 571d166c7c and bfb121ef72 in that order. The merge c51d285264's diff equals the sum of the three. Resolve the State.cpp conflict by setting STATE_VERSION to XR current + 1; no other conflict is expected (greedy_mb clean). If G086 is deferred or skipped, skip this whole group. Verification: build Windows VS2022 with ENABLE_VR=ON, which takes the HAS_LIBMGBA path. Also build Quest assembleQuestDebug, because after 606bcbe3ed this file compiles for ARM64. Smoke-test a GB Player disc (UGP*) with a GBA ROM in GBA slot 5. Check that boot waits for the Control power-up and that audio plays without 'GBPlayer: Audio buffer underrun' warnings. Also check video scanline IRQs, a GB/GBC ROM, and a savestate round-trip. Apply 1edea92358 (G274, shadowed-variable rename in the same file) only after this group.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 3ece5f7d7b (G086), 19157cacd5 (G086), 2f4badc357 (G086), 670a5df7ce (G080), d76757bba6 (G078), de0999a19c (G082), f103155658 (G082), 207fe1f5de (G092), 9410094d9f (G092), ef997d0d82 (G105), 415f8c1f8a (G106), 798fff86fe (G109), b083ce5912 (G110)

- **Notes:** Blockers to name: 3ece5f7d7b (G086) creates the files, and G086 as a group is MEDIUM+ because of 5167adc8df, 606bcbe3ed and 19157cacd5. G080 (670a5df7ce, CoreTiming.cpp, which XR modified, but the hunk in RegisterEvent is disjoint from XR's RefreshConfig VBI code) becomes an extra blocker if it is classified above LOW. G086 already needs it too, because the pre-G123 GBPlayer code also used capturing lambdas. Soft dependencies: 26583fd026 (G098, MEDIUM because of XR-modified Android files and MainSettings.cpp) makes GBA cores always threaded. Without it, PushEvent runs HandleEvent synchronously when GBA Threads=false, so G123 still works but runs mGBA inline on the CPU thread. c05cb75d6c and 38674f5152 (G108) add the mCoreSync early runLoop exit and the GBACore AVStream refactor. G123 installs its own AV stream on the GBPlayer slot; this relies on 2f4badc357 skipping SetAVStream/SetSIODriver for Config::GBPLAYER_GBA_INDEX. The PR was rebased onto 6ed8dec7ac (the G121 merge), so the upstream previous STATE_VERSION is 188, from 219eab74f2 (G121). GB Player savestates made before this change become incompatible, which the version bump covers. Downstream dependent: 1edea92358 (G274) is based on this PR's resulting blob ccc6e29e16. There are no Android or jni changes and no GameSettings INI changes, so rules 6 and 7 do not apply. The 3 commits are not obsolete: XR has no GB Player implementation.

Nothing overlaps. Semantic prerequisite: 3eea3a90b5 (G073), a 2-line DiscIO region-flag fix. Upstream deliberately held these INIs until that fix landed, because without it US dumps are identified as GNLJ82 and would be offered the NTSC-J codes (enabling wrong-revision codes causes fatal errors).

- **Upstream dependencies:** 3eea3a90b5 (G073)
- **Handle together with:** G073 (Triforce: Check only first byte in region flag.)
- **Notes:** Cross-batch warning for G073: once it lands, NTSC-U MKAGP2 dumps change ID from GNLJ82 to GNLE82, so XR's GameSettingsVR/GNLJ82.ini stops applying to US dumps. If that profile was authored on a US dump (no record either way), add a GNLE82 or 3-char GNL VR profile. That is a data-only follow-up and belongs to G073's review.
- **Commits:**
  - `f55001b9d9` GameSettings: Add INI files for Mario Kart Arcade GP 2. — **BLOCKED**: Opt-in cheat codes only. Needs G073's Triforce ID fix for correct region mapping.
- **Adversarial-review downgrades:**
  - skeptic (all) on `f55001b9d9`: LOW → BLOCKED: Hidden semantic prerequisite on a MEDIUM commit (rule 4). At XR HEAD, VolumeGC::GetRegion() and VolumeDisc::GetCountry() switch on the full u32 Triforce region_flags. USA Mario Kart Arcade GP 2 boot.id stores 0E FF FF FF, which falls into 'default: JAPAN', so every USA dump gets the ID GNLJ82. That is also the ID XR's GameSettingsVR/GNLJ82.ini VR profile uses. With this commit alone, those USA dumps load the new GameSettings/GNLJ82.ini and are offered the NTSC-J (2007/02/06 20:29:25) codes, whose addresses do not match the USA build (Skip Boot Check 0402E8B8 vs USA 0402E858; Widescreen hook C22C86EC vs C22C80D4). Meanwhile GNLE82.ini can never be loaded. The commit body says upstream held these INIs back until #14470 landed, because enabling codes for the wrong version causes fatal errors. #14470 is 3eea3a90b5 (G073), which is final MEDIUM in decisions.json because it changes the ID XR's VR profile is keyed on. The classifier itself listed it in depends_on. Opt-in codes do not avoid this hazard, which is exactly what upstream gated on. Land this together with G073 and its GNLE82 VR-profile decision. (blockers: 3eea3a90b5a6c3f337f1eb951c63b5ca3ab9b06d)

The InputCommon control names ("Button N", "Axis N+", "Hat N E") come out byte-identical, so saved mappings are unaffected, and the u8/u16/int values are still printed as numbers. Two problems block it. NetPlayIndex::Add lost its {{"X-Is-Dolphin","1"}} header and AllowedReturnCodes::All argument. Also, the bools has_password/in_game are now sent as true/false instead of 1/0, which broke the lobby index. Upstream fixed this in 9386b577b3 (G342).

No XR Android source references NetPlayUICallbacks, InetAddressToString, GetNetPlayName or NetPlayIndex (git grep came back empty).

If G223 is deferred or rejected, run `git cherry-pick -n 08e183d322`, discard the change to Source/Android/jni/NetPlay/NetPlayUICallbacks.cpp (the file doesn't exist in XR), commit, then cherry-pick 9386b577b3. G342's alone conflict exists only because it needs G280's text. No JNI signature change.

#### G293 — PR #14755 — windeployqt: Disable uneeded DLLs

- **Subsystem:** Windows build: DolphinQt CMake POST_BUILD windeployqt deployment
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-07-31 (merge `f1b4345d08`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **XR-modified files touched:** `Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt`
- **Why not integrated automatically:** The text applies cleanly because XR never edited the windeployqt block, but the change breaks XR's toolchain. XR pins bundled Qt 6.5.1 (root CMakeLists.txt l.493-499), and Build-vs2022/CMakeCache.txt resolves WINDEPLOYQT_EXE to Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64/bin/windeployqt.exe. That binary has no 'no-system-dxc-compiler' option: the UTF-16 option string is absent, and so is any dxcompiler/dxil deployment logic. I ran it with --dry-run plus the new flag and it printed "Unknown option 'no-system-dxc-compiler'." and exited 1; the same command without the flag parses normally. Taken alone, the dolphin-emu POST_BUILD step would fail on every Windows build (MSB3073), leaving Qt DLLs and plugins undeployed. This violates rule 5. The flag only works with a Qt >= 6.8 windeployqt, which means after G207 (e87225beb2), and G207 is classified HIGH as a fork toolchain decision. Per rule 4 that makes this BLOCKED.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** Source/Core/DolphinQt/CMakeLists.txt. XR's edits there are a leading blank line, the VR/override/ShaderHunter/CullingCodeFinder/VRPane sources added to add_executable, and the new dolphin-emu-sys-copy target plus the $<TARGET_FILE_DIR> Sys-copy POST_BUILD (l.536-549). Upstream's one-line hunk is in the separate windeployqt add_custom_command (XR HEAD l.592-602). The two are textually disjoint, but the hunk changes how XR's own post-build deploy step behaves, because XR uses bundled Qt 6.5.1 windeployqt.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Do not auto-pick. While XR stays on bundled Qt 6.5.1 (G207 skipped), skip 6ec57bcc5d. Qt 6.5.1's windeployqt never deploys dxcompiler.dll or dxil.dll, so there is nothing to suppress, and the flag would break the post-build step. If the maintainer migrates to Qt 6.8.3 (G207 option B), cherry-pick it right after e87225beb2, together with G340 (e9c5b57d88). Then delete Build-vs2022/CMakeCache.txt so WINDEPLOYQT_EXE re-resolves to the Qt6.8.3 tool, and build Release. Confirm that the windeployqt post-build step exits 0 and that Binary/x64/Release contains no dxcompiler.dll or dxil.dll. If a version-agnostic CMake is ever wanted, adapt the upstream line to be added only under a Qt6_VERSION check for the Qt release that introduced the option (absent in 6.5.1). Do not add it unconditionally.
- **Upstream dependencies:** e87225beb2 (G207)
- **Handle together with:** G207 (Update bundled Windows Qt to 6.8.3), G340 (DolphinQt: Properly define QtPaths)
- **Notes:** This is consistent with the existing G207 decision (B09-build: HIGH, handle_together_with G293/G340) and with G340's note, which found the same 'Unknown option' failure independently. G340 (e9c5b57d88) later rewrites this windeployqt command. Its hunk context ends at `--no-translations` and does not include the dxc line, so skipping G293 adds no extra conflict to G340. The upstream target's file contains the flag at l.612, so XR's file will intentionally diverge on this one line while it stays on Qt 6.5.1.
- **Commits:**
  - `6ec57bcc5d` windeployqt: Disable uneeded DLLs — **BLOCKED**: It needs a Qt >= 6.8 windeployqt (the G207 bump). The bundled Qt 6.5.1 windeployqt rejects the new flag with 'Unknown option' and exit code 1, which would fail the POST_BUILD deploy step.

This is an isolated IOS bug fix, so LOW. Build and run the tests target, especially FileSystemTest.*. Then smoke-test save and load state in a Wii game. If G193 is deferred, defer this commit too. The later release-branch copy 6376e0c1f1 (G349) becomes an empty cherry-pick; skip it.

- **Commits:**
  - `8a90cbf638` IOS/FS: Fix loading savestate when files are open — **BLOCKED**: Isolated IOS/FS DoState handle bookkeeping plus a unit test. No XR files, clean, no format change. Semantically requires G193 (its unit test depends on G193's metadata savestating).
- **Adversarial-review downgrades:**
  - skeptic (all) on `8a90cbf638`: LOW → BLOCKED: REFUTED: this commit has a hard semantic prerequisite on d35fe1b78b (G193 'IOS/FS: Rewrite NAND savestating'). G193's final verdict is MEDIUM: it rewrites the NAND savestate format beyond the version line, and it in turn needs 95dec13203 (G139, MEDIUM). (1) The bug fixed here does not exist at XR HEAD. There, HostFileSystem::DoStateRead and DoStateWriteOrMeasure (FS.cpp:282/327) still use host File:: calls and never touch m_handles. The commit message itself says 'due to d35fe1b'. Upstream backported it as 6376e0c1f1, and I verified that d35fe1b78b is an ancestor of that backport. (2) Concrete failure if it is auto-picked alone: merge-tree onto XR HEAD is clean and it compiles (FileHandle::Release, GetStatus and the FileHandle(fs, fd) constructor already exist), but the new gtest FileSystemTest.DoState fails. Delete("/tmp/a") erases the FST entries (FS.cpp:549, parent->children.erase). Pre-G193 DoStateRead then recreates only the host directories and files. GetMetadata("/tmp/a") therefore falls back to GetFstEntryForPath's default entry (FstEntry::data is Metadata{}, so uid 0/gid 0), and check_directory_metadata(..., Uid{1}, Gid{2}) asserts. That turns the unit-test suite red. Its runtime change alone is otherwise harmless: m_handles is copied, cleared and restored, the same Handle fields are serialized, and the format does not change. It must be integrated as part of the G193 unit (d35fe1b78b -> 682490c220 -> 8a90cbf638 -> G344). It touches no XR file. (blockers: d35fe1b78b5acd2bcb74641803dd588ca51cb005)

#### G325 — PR #14746 — Add Gecko code for Star Fox Adventures (Retroachievements)

The problem is the hash. The commit changes Data/Sys/ApprovedInis.json but updates no hash. It relies on 55c015e6ac (G283), which generates ACHIEVEMENT_APPROVED_LIST_HASH from the JSON at build time. G283 does this with a CMake add_custom_command, CMake/GenerateAchievementHash.cmake, AchievementApprovedHash.h.in, and a constexpr StringToDigest in Common/Crypto/SHA1.h. XR HEAD still has the hand-maintained APPROVED_LIST_HASH at AchievementManager.h:85.  If this commit is cherry-picked without G283, AchievementManager::LoadApprovedList() fails its SHA1 check and returns {}. Every RA-approved INI code and patch is then rejected in hardcore mode, with only a WARN log, and PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes fails. G283 changes what the Windows and Android builds do, so rule 5 makes it MEDIUM; that makes this commit BLOCKED.  There is also a text dependency. The JSON is a single line whose pre-image is exactly the output of a5f6ba81fa (G239). That requires the whole chain c96d1b7b4a (G151) -> 3944310308 (G158) -> 915b839598 (G209) -> a5f6ba81fa (G239) to be applied first. If G283 ends up classified LOW, this group becomes LOW.

GSAE01 (Star Fox Adventures) has an XR VR profile, Data/Sys/GameSettingsVR/GSAE01.ini, containing the HideObjectCodes entry "Remove Mini Map 1", ElementsGroupOverride entries, and [Graphics.VR] DetectRenderTargets/SmallViewportsOnScreen/UnitsPerMeter=20. Upstream only adds an opt-in code to GSAE01r1.ini, so default VR behavior does not change.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate right after G283 (55c015e6ac), which itself needs a108fb849f (G096) and the JSON chain G151/G158/G209/G239. Apply G325, G332 and G353 in landing order as one pass; once G283 is in, they are plain data cherry-picks. If G283 is deferred, use one of these: (a) hold this group; (b) cherry-pick it and hand-set ACHIEVEMENT_APPROVED_LIST_HASH in Source/Core/Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h (added by G096) to D5C6D1B9CB05FC0ED26D5317C099E90BC2A02F93. I checked that the file is already in picojson canonical form, so the raw-file SHA1 equals the runtime serialize() SHA1; (c) apply only the GSAE01r1.ini hunk and defer the JSON. Verify with tests --gtest_filter=PatchAllowlist.*.

The RA_PROGRESS_ENABLED key already exists at XR HEAD (Core/Config/AchievementSettings.cpp:29), and AchievementManager.cpp already includes Config/AchievementSettings.h. It applies cleanly alone and in greedy_xr, and the context matches XR HEAD exactly. This is an isolated RetroAchievements fix, which the rule 3 exception covers. It has no dependencies.

- **Commits:**
  - `3a55a0e1a2` RetroAchievements - Fix Achievement Progress Toggle — **BLOCKED**: Isolated RetroAchievements config-gate fix. Applies cleanly, and the config key is already present at XR HEAD.
- **Adversarial-review downgrades:**
  - skeptic (all) on `3a55a0e1a2`: LOW → BLOCKED: The diff is fine on its own: AchievementManager.cpp is not XR-modified, merge-tree is clean, Config::RA_PROGRESS_ENABLED exists (AchievementSettings.cpp:29) and its header is already included. But the commit has a hidden semantic prerequisite: G006 99d82829c8 'Android: Fix defaults for RetroAchievements settings', which is final MEDIUM because it edits XR-modified BooleanSetting.kt (rule 6). At XR HEAD the defaults disagree. The C++ Config::RA_PROGRESS_ENABLED defaults to FALSE (AchievementSettings.cpp:29-30), but the Kotlin BooleanSetting.ACHIEVEMENTS_PROGRESS_ENABLED defaults to TRUE (BooleanSetting.kt:931-935). The Android GUI reads it through NativeConfig.getBoolean, whose JNI Get() returns layer->Get<T>(loc).value_or(kotlin_default) (jni/Config/NativeConfig.cpp:101-104,195-200). The switch is shown on Quest: Config > Achievements is added unconditionally (SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt:285, not IS_QUEST-gated), the Progress switch is at :1120-1126 when achievements are enabled, and USE_RETRO_ACHIEVEMENTS is ON on all platforms (CMakeLists.txt:100). Before this commit the core ignored the flag, so popups always appeared, which matched the GUI's ON. With this commit alone, HandleAchievementProgressIndicatorShowEvent returns early on the C++ default false. On Quest/Android, a never-touched profile then shows 'Progress' ON while progress popups are silently suppressed. That is exactly the GUI/core mismatch G006 fixes (landed upstream Feb 2026, 7 months before this commit, so upstream never shipped this combination). Desktop/Qt is consistent either way because the Qt checkbox reads the C++ default. There is no VR or pacing impact: the moved update_event.Trigger only reaches the Qt AchievementsWindow hook. Fix: pick G338 together with G006. Both merge-tree cleanly onto XR HEAD. (blockers: 99d82829c84880402784ae1ace684e0f6e635ef6)

- **Why not integrated automatically:** The element it changes (a View with background @color/settings_search_outline inside settings_search_mode_container) and the color resource itself are both introduced by 893be0996e (G317, 'Android: Add global settings search'). Neither exists at XR HEAD, so the commit conflicts alone and in greedy_xr, and is clean on pure upstream (greedy_mb). G317 is a large Android settings UI change (+1342/-703) that conflicts with XR-modified SettingsFragmentPresenter.kt (XR +1313), SettingsActivityPresenter.kt, SettingsFragment.kt and strings.xml, so it is HIGH-class. This small fix therefore has to wait for it. On its own merits it would be MEDIUM (Android; activity_settings.xml is not XR-modified; no id or binding change; MaterialDivider is already used in XR layouts with material 1.13.0).
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** None directly: XR has not modified activity_settings.xml, and the quest flavor's res only overrides values/arrays.xml and themes.xml. The only connection is indirect, through the blocker G317, which reworks the settings activity, fragment and presenters that XR's Quest VR settings UI is built on.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Integrate together with G317 and right after it: cherry-pick 893be0996e and 93706022d5 (G317), resolving their XR settings-UI conflicts, then b5748b81d6, which should then apply cleanly. XR's standard flavor still has minSdk 21, so API 21-28 devices would hit this crash if G317 lands without it. Quest (API 29+) is not affected. If G317 is skipped, skip G352 as well.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 893be0996e (G317)
- **Handle together with:** G317 (Android: Add global settings search)
- **Notes:** 93706022d5 (G317's reformat commit) does not touch activity_settings.xml; the textual prerequisite is 893be0996e alone. Treat G317+G352 as one unit so the API ≤ 28 crash is never shipped.
- **Commits:**
  - `b5748b81d6` Fix android settings crash on API level 28 and lower — **BLOCKED**: 4-line layout fix to the settings-search divider added by 893be0996e (G317). That element and @color/settings_search_outline don't exist at XR HEAD. G317 is HIGH-class (conflicts with XR's heavily modified settings UI).

#### G353 — PR #14870 — RetroAchievements - Additional Code Approvals

- **Subsystem:** Game INIs + RetroAchievements allowlist (Data/Sys/ApprovedInis.json)
- **Landed upstream:** 2026-09-30 (merge `771fb15405`), 1 commit(s)
- **Files affected:** `Data/Sys/ApprovedInis.json`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GDJEB2.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GF7E01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GFTE01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GMSE01.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GPOE8P.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GQPP78.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/GYAE78.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/R3R.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RBU.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RDGEA4.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/RHO.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SBDE08.ini`, `Data/Sys/GameSettings/SBDP08.ini`
- **XR-modified files touched:** none
- **Why not integrated automatically:** All INI pre-images are identical at XR HEAD, and the only additions are opt-in code sections and *_RetroAchievements_Verified lists. There are no [*_Enabled] entries and no Video_* keys, so under rule 7 the content is LOW, including for the two VR-profile games, GFTE01 and GMSE01.  The ApprovedInis.json change carries no hash update. It depends on 55c015e6ac (G283) generating ACHIEVEMENT_APPROVED_LIST_HASH; XR HEAD still has the hand-maintained APPROVED_LIST_HASH in AchievementManager.h. Without G283, LoadApprovedList() fails verification and all RA approvals are rejected in hardcore mode. G283 is a MEDIUM build-system change, so this is BLOCKED.  The single-line JSON pre-image is the output of 4a9db521c1 (G332), so G332, G325 and the earlier chain must land first. It becomes LOW once G283 is in.
- **Conflicting / overlapping DolphinXR code:** No XR-modified file is touched. Two games have VR profiles: - GFTE01 (Mario Golf: Toadstool Tour; VR profile has FrameSizeFromXFB=True plus TextureElement and ElementsGroup overrides) gains an opt-in "$16:9 Widescreen [gamemasterplc]" in [ActionReplay]. - GMSE01 (Super Mario Sunshine; VR profile has [Graphics.VR], ShaderOverride "Water fix", and TextureElementOverride "$Heat Haze" handling=skip for texture a0ee80f74dd3b117) gains the opt-in Gecko code "$Disable Heat Wave" (0419F83C 4E800020). Neither code is enabled by default, so XR's VR defaults do not change. XR's CullingCodeFinderWidget saves generated AR codes to the user INI by name; the new code names do not collide with it.
- **Suggested integration approach:** Apply last in the approvals pass, after G283 and after G325 and G332 in order. It is a plain data cherry-pick once G283 is in. If G283 is deferred, either hold, or cherry-pick and hand-set the hash in Source/Core/Core/AchievementApprovedHash.h to 4F153428B0EC04C6E7D2AFB7A8B90AFB843D06F6 (checked: canonical JSON), or take only the Data/Sys/GameSettings hunks and defer the JSON. Do not edit the upstream INIs to suit VR; VR-specific behavior belongs in GameSettingsVR.
- **Upstream dependencies:** 55c015e6ac (G283), 4a9db521c1 (G332), 0901c6dbe5 (G325), a5f6ba81fa (G239)
- **Handle together with:** G325 (Add Gecko code for Star Fox Adventures (Retroachievements)), G332 (Add AR codes for Timesplitters 2 (Retroachievements))
- **Notes:** (1) GFTE01's new 16:9 code is written with Gecko code types (04, C2, 20, E2) but sits in [ActionReplay] and is approved under [AR_RetroAchievements_Verified]. This looks like an upstream content slip. It is opt-in, so leave it as upstream has it. (2) Sunshine's "Disable Heat Wave" Gecko code is an alternative to XR's texture-skip "$Heat Haze" override. A VR profile could reference it later; that is not part of this integration. (3) Enabling the 16:9 or 60FPS codes on VR-profile games changes projection or frame timing, but only when the user opts in. (4) Merge 771fb154059c is the upstream target tip.
- **Commits:**
  - `d51d80b9ec` RetroAchievements - Additional Code Approvals — **BLOCKED**: The opt-in code additions are harmless (including for GFTE01 and GMSE01), but the JSON approval needs G283's generated hash (MEDIUM). It also needs the JSON pre-image produced by G332.

## Potentially obsolete commits

Do not cherry-pick these. They are release-branch duplicates of master commits (patch-identical by `git patch-id --stable`; the master originals are what get integrated), commit+revert pairs that net to zero, or fixes XR already has in another form.

### G067 — PR #14504 — Android: Pin R8 9.1.34 to fix release minify crash

- **Why obsolete:** Temporary workaround. aeb3fec03a (G246) deletes exactly this settings.gradle.kts block (the file returns to blob 1a68f74a48), so G067 plus G246 net to zero. It is only needed if XR stays on AGP 9.1.0.
  - `336f604b3a` Android: Pin R8 9.1.34 to fix release minify crash — not a duplicate: R8 pin that only applies with AGP 9.1.0 from G063 (HIGH); net-zero once G246 is applied.

### G333 — PR #14842 — VideoCommon: Use correct size for PE token

- **Why obsolete:** Commit+revert pair: reverted by 3e95d0fc16 (G337, PR 14847), merged within hours; the net diff is empty.
  - `e4349ae179` VideoCommon: Use correct size for PE token — not a duplicate: Two DEBUG_LOG_FMT format strings only, away from XR's XFB-copy edits in BPStructs.cpp. Reverted by G337, so skip the pair.

### G337 — PR #14847 — Revert "VideoCommon: Use correct size for PE token"

- **Why obsolete:** Revert of e4349ae179 (G333). Together they net to zero, and XR HEAD already has the post-revert text.
  - `3e95d0fc16` Revert "VideoCommon: Use correct size for PE token" — not a duplicate: Reverts G333's two log-format lines back to XR HEAD's current text. Skip with G333, or apply right after it.

### G349 — release-branch merge — Merge branch 'release-prep-2609'

- **Why obsolete:** 15 of 17 commits are release-branch copies (identical by git patch-id --stable) of master commits that already land earlier in the range: b2c4d543dd->d5d2f7c8fb (G279), 6d2e3a1ecc->af38db4a23 (G279), 5aa711db41->ce85da6b96 (G306), b6282fb9ab->95ee7de40e (G267), 2f5232fbf4->a3740b5f71 (G296), 8f1e33a5f4->ffb0e2c594 (G296), 4eb90c7314->feeb36bf07 (G296), b6d8da24b3->4782a04c20 (G296), 611d864897->21d44f32ba (G259), 5e237fd284->8fb464040c (G281), a926974153->5b9961b0c6 (G266), 7c4ed02b27->24aafdeb47 (G268), f29ed945d0->49453c045c (G268), 6376e0c1f1->8a90cbf638 (G303), 94b20d52f0->c490bc2c2e (G271). All 15 originals are ancestors of 233b2dfe6d, and none is in XR yet. Upstream itself threw the copies away: the inner merge d085f97001 resolved its only conflict (DolReader.cpp) to master's side, and the final merge differs from master only in the version line. 596d71fbba (MINOR 0->1, '2606a') is undone by f84df02055 (MINOR 1->0). The only real content left is MAJOR 2606->2609, which is a fork-policy choice, not a functional change.
  - `b2c4d543dd` HW/DSPHLE/AXVoice: Check array bounds in ApplyUpdatesForMs() — patch-identical to master commit `d5d2f7c8fb (G279)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `6d2e3a1ecc` HW/DSPHLE/AXVoice: Prefer BitCastPtr over BitCastToArray in ApplyUpdatesForMs() — patch-identical to master commit `af38db4a23 (G279)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `5aa711db41` DSPHLE/Zelda: prevent out-of-bounds stack read — patch-identical to master commit `ce85da6b96 (G306)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `b6282fb9ab` IOS/NetIPTopDevice: Zero-initialize sockaddr structs — patch-identical to master commit `95ee7de40e (G267)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `2f5232fbf4` GCZ: Don't trust GetBlockCompressedSize — patch-identical to master commit `a3740b5f71 (G296)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `8f1e33a5f4` GCZ: Don't trust block_num either — patch-identical to master commit `ffb0e2c594 (G296)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `4eb90c7314` GCZ: validate while loading — patch-identical to master commit `feeb36bf07 (G296)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `b6d8da24b3` GCZ: use 64-bit for m_data_offset — patch-identical to master commit `4782a04c20 (G296)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `611d864897` fixes: make dolreader validate section addresses and sizes — patch-identical to master commit `21d44f32ba (G259)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `5e237fd284` DolReader: Fix integer wraparound — patch-identical to master commit `8fb464040c (G281)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `a926974153` NetPlay: bound LZO decompression output — patch-identical to master commit `5b9961b0c6 (G266)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `7c4ed02b27` Core: validate standalone ELF input ranges — patch-identical to master commit `24aafdeb47 (G268)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `f29ed945d0` Core: log invalid ELF input — patch-identical to master commit `49453c045c (G268)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `6376e0c1f1` IOS/FS: Fix loading savestate when files are open — patch-identical to master commit `8a90cbf638 (G303)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `94b20d52f0` BuildMacOSUniversalBinary: Add flag to enable CCache — patch-identical to master commit `c490bc2c2e (G271)`; integrate the master original instead
  - `596d71fbba` ScmRevGen: Bump version to 2606a — not a duplicate: Unique commit: version bump to 2606a (DOLPHIN_VERSION_MINOR "0"->"1"), a hotfix version that only ever existed on the release branch. Rule 8 fork-policy -> MEDIUM. It nets to zero with f84df02055, which sets MINOR back to "0". Applied alone to XR it conflicts, because XR is still at "2512" and the context needs MAJOR "2606" from 6094cfcf7b (G202), which in turn needs d8558142ea (G020). It applies cleanly in greedy order. The effect on XR builds is cosmetic, since their version strings come from git describe.
  - `f84df02055` ScmRevGen: Bump version to 2609 — not a duplicate: Unique commit: version bump to 2609 (MAJOR "2606"->"2609", MINOR "1"->"0"). This is the only net content of the merge on master. Rule 8 fork-policy -> MEDIUM. Its context needs MINOR "1" from 596d71fbba and MAJOR "2606" from 6094cfcf7b (G202), which needs d8558142ea (G020); it applies cleanly in greedy order. If XR adopts upstream numbering, one hand edit to 2609/0 can replace the whole bump chain.

### G351 — PR #14892 — Android: Set abiFilters for ndk

- **Why obsolete:** XR already moved abiFilters from cmake{} to ndk{} (per product flavor), which is upstream's fix. Applying upstream's defaultConfig ndk block on top would union with the quest flavor's filter (AGP MergedNdkConfig.append uses addAll) and regress the Quest build by adding x86_64.
  - `5f395ab792` Android: Set abiFilters for ndk — not a duplicate: Clean textual apply on XR-modified build.gradle.kts, but redundant with XR's per-flavor ndk.abiFilters. Its defaultConfig ndk block would be unioned into the quest flavor (adding x86_64). Skip it.

## Build/test results

Everything below ran in the worktree `C:\Users\Christophe\Documents\DXR-upstream`. Its `Build-vs2022` was configured with `cmake -G "Visual Studio 17 2022" -A x64 -DENABLE_VR=ON`, and the resulting option set is identical to the main checkout's `Build-vs2022/CMakeCache.txt` (all `ENABLE_*`/`USE_*` options diffed: no differences).

| Check | Baseline: `openxr-work` @ `5e9a304a65` | Integrated: `upstream-integration` @ `8ca3600964` |
|---|---|---|
| Windows build: `cmake --build Build-vs2022 --config Release --parallel` (VS2022 / MSVC 19.44, `ENABLE_VR=ON`) | success, full build 443 s, 0 errors | success, incremental (CMake auto-reconfigured, 1,276 translation units rebuilt, `Dolphin.exe` relinked) 325 s, **0 errors, no new compiler warnings** |
| Unit tests: `tests` target, then `Binary/x64/Tests/Release/tests.exe` | **1376/1376 passed** | **1376/1376 passed** (incl. `PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes`, which validates the integrated RetroAchievements approvals against the approved-list hash) |
| Quest build: `Source/Android/build-quest.ps1` → `gradlew app:assembleQuestDebug` (arm64-v8a, NDK r29, `ENABLE_VR=ON`) | BUILD SUCCESSFUL, 8 m 17 s (full) | **BUILD SUCCESSFUL**, 4 m 28 s (incremental); `app-quest-debug.apk` produced |

**Test-harness note.** Running the `unittests` target or plain `ctest` reports a failure (`Not Run` without `-C Release`; with `-C Release`, `PatchAllowlist.VerifyHashes` fails). The cause is the multi-config layout: the post-build step copies `Data/Sys` to `Binary/x64/Tests/Sys`, while `tests.exe` lives in `Binary/x64/Tests/Release/` and looks for `Sys/` beside itself. The behaviour is identical at baseline. With `Sys/` copied next to the exe, every test passes both before and after integration. This is a pre-existing harness issue, not a regression.

**Warnings.**
- Windows: no compiler warning in the integrated build that was absent from the baseline. One pre-existing environment warning appears in both builds: Qt translations are skipped because `Externals/Qt/Qt6.5.1/x64/mkspecs/qconfig.pri` is missing from the bundled Qt.
- Quest: 3 new clang warnings, all from upstream code as written:
  - `GCAdapter.cpp`: unused variables `last_read_failed` and `last_successful_read_time` (from `caed400add`, G054). Their only uses are compiled out on Android. `upstream/master` has the identical code, so upstream's own Android build warns too.
  - `DirectoryBlob.cpp`: `-Wreorder` for `m_wrapped_partition`/`m_is_triforce` (from `9942fb780a`, G215). It is harmless because neither initializer reads the other member. Upstream fixes it in `69527ec44b` *DirectoryBlob: Resolve reorder warning*, which is deferred only because its PR G274 is kept atomic; it can be cherry-picked on its own.

**Not built or tested:** runtime behaviour (no game boot, no headset or PCVR/Quest session), the Quest release/R8 variant (`assembleQuestRelease`), the `standard` Android flavor, D3D12/Vulkan/OpenGL backend runtime paths, and Linux/macOS/Flatpak builds. These upstream changes do touch emulation behaviour (Triforce, GBA, DSP HLE bounds checks, JIT fixes, DiscIO validation) and **should get a normal smoke test (a few GC/Wii games in VR on PC and on Quest)** before `upstream-integration` is merged into `openxr-work`.

**Integrity checks:**
- The real branch tree equals the pre-computed simulation tree (`460cc688a66b`).
- All 52,205 non-trivial lines that DolphinXR added in 378 files are present after integration. The only exception is the deliberately renumbered `STATE_VERSION` line (181 → 190).
- XR's `Externals/fmt/fmt` and `Externals/OpenXR` gitlinks are unchanged. Only `Externals/SDL/SDL` (3.4.0 → 3.4.12) and `Externals/cpp-ipc/cpp-ipc` moved, through upstream's own commits.

### Reproducing the checks

- Exact replay: `python simulate2.py 5e9a304a65 plan.json out.json` (merge-tree only; it models the STATE_VERSION resolution).
- Integration driver: `python integrate.py plan.json log.json`, run inside the worktree. It aborts on any conflict other than the STATE_VERSION line.
- Preservation audit: `python xr_preservation_check.py`.
